| CD-011 | 2026-08-09 | **How the deferred script-guard row must be derived** (standing instruction for the session that vendors `tools/hub-export/`). The CD-008 Hub renderer script guard is written **from the actual harness code**, not from memory or from the SLOT README's summary, and is **cross-checked against the SB validator's list**. | CD-012 | 2026-08-09 | **Script guard is canon — CD-008's deferral closes; PENDING-P-002 ruled.** The guard is the SB validator's *verified* scope, in three tiers. **(1)** Arabic script is RED **anywhere, in any string**, rendered or metadata (absolute). **(2)** Arrows / emoji / symbol glyphs are RED in rendered text fields (`text_bn`, `text_en`, titles) and **GREY in metadata** — metadata legitimately carries →/⚠/🔒, so failing it there is a false positive. **(3)** Em-dash and ellipsis are **ALLOWED** in rendered text; the WATCH counter stays on **for one term** as a monitor only, then is reviewed and retired or promoted. Codepoint ranges are taken from the validator, not restated from memory. **Corrections to the old canon/language SLOT summary** ("no Arabic script / emoji / em-dash / arrows in JSON strings") — it was wrong on three of its four items: em-dash is **not** banned (it is allowed, tier 3); arrows and emoji are **not** banned in *all* strings (metadata is grey, tier 2); and "JSON strings" was the wrong unit — the boundary is rendered-text vs metadata, not JSON vs non-JSON. Only the Arabic item survives, and it survives strengthened. **Enforcement is authoring-side only** — the Hub harness performs no charset check (proven, `tools/hub-export/SMOKE.md` test 3), so nothing downstream catches a violation. | Principal ruling 2026-08-09 on PENDING-P-002; `validator_v2_rebuilt.py` check 8; `tools/hub-export/SMOKE.md`; CD-008; CD-011 |
| CD-013 | 2026-08-09 | **The Hub harness's missing charset check is logged as an UPSTREAM issue, not a local patch.** `validate_import.py` v1.0 LOCKED performs no script/charset validation, so content promoted to gold carrying Arabic or emoji would tofu in the Hub's own PDF renderer. Recorded as **UP-001** in `tools/hub-export/UPSTREAM_ISSUES.md` for the `scd-hub` repo to resolve in **its own D-series** — this repo does not own it. The vendored harness is **not patched here**: it is supersede-only (CD-003, AGENTS.md §7), and a local fix would silently diverge from the LOCKED contract. | Principal ruling 2026-08-09; CD-003; CD-012 |
| CD-014 | 2026-08-09 | **PENDING-P-003 ruled — Tier 1 stays as-is for every current workstream (Arabic RED anywhere), and its ground is restated as renderer capability, not doctrine.** An unshaped Arabic string tofus or breaks joining in the Hub's PDF renderer, and that failure surfaces after gold promotion, in a child's hand. The rule therefore lifts when the capability is proven, not when it is argued. A workstream may carry Arabic script in rendered fields only when **both** hold: **(1) proven shaping** — its full render path has passed an **executed** smoke test with real ayah text rendered and eyeball-verified (RTL correct, joining correct, no tofu), logged as `tools/hub-export/SMOKE.md` is, and proven **per render path** so a new renderer/font/export route re-proves it; and **(2) verbatim quoted source only** — every Arabic string is quoted source text with provenance (mushaf or hadith reference in `source_note`), reviewed in the **আলিম lane**, never model-composed and never transliteration-round-tripped. Until both hold, drafts use **Bangla + transliteration with an `ARABIC-SLOT` placeholder** marking the insertion point. That is `islamic-studies`' position today. Amends CD-012 tier 1 (ground and lift conditions); the tier-1 stance itself is unchanged. | Principal ruling 2026-08-09 on PENDING-P-003; CD-012; CD-013 |

| CD-020 | 2026-08-09 | **Third manifest status added: `VENDORED-UNPROVEN`.** `PENDING` = not vendored · `VENDORED-UNPROVEN` = present but never executed · `REQUIRED` = proven · `DEFERRED` = deliberately absent. The old two-state scheme went silent at exactly the wrong moment — a PENDING row's warn vanished the instant the file appeared, so a vendored-but-never-run tool looked identical to a proven one. Gate behaviour: VENDORED-UNPROVEN **warns until the file is named in its folder's `SMOKE.md`**, and FAILs if the file is missing; PENDING now also warns if the file is *present* (stale row); and **REQUIRED now FAILs unless a `SMOKE.md` run names the file** — REQUIRED asserts proof, so an unmentioned tool cannot hold it. A `SMOKE.md` line naming a file that also contains "UNPROVEN" is read as an explicit non-proof declaration and does not satisfy REQUIRED. **Applying this honestly reclassified four `tools/hub-export` rows** that were REQUIRED but never exercised — both `build_*` scripts and the plan/question payload schemas (only the stimulus schema was used in that smoke run). `render_plan.py` becomes the first intended VENDORED-UNPROVEN row; its proof needs a real P03 plan JSON, which arrives with **Step 4 (lesson-plans)**, so its warn is correct and expected until then. Negative-tested at implementation: all six new paths fire correctly. | Principal ruling 2026-08-09; CD-009; CD-019 |
| CD-021 | 2026-08-09 | **Class-test duration is canon: 35 minutes standard for a 25-mark test; 30 permitted; 35 is the maximum.** **Verified at source before writing this row: `MarkLogic_QuestionPolicy.md` §৬ specifies 25 marks and 4–6 questions lifted from the annual paper, but states NO duration — and no MarkLogic file mentions exam duration anywhere.** So this is **additive canon filling a genuine gap, not a supersede of a stated value**. The "৪৫ মিনিট" appearing in the produced class tests was carried from the reference documents and had no canon backing; it is superseded by this row. Recorded as an application note at the end of QuestionPolicy §৬. Consequence for tooling: duration is a **generator config default (35), not free text in the source markdown** — `tools/render/ct_docx.py` now enforces it and rejects a value outside 30–35. ⚑ The two reference CTs at `tools/render/reference/` still read ৪৫ মিনিট and are now non-conformant; they are authoring references, not canon, and need correcting or marking superseded before they are used as a pattern again. | Principal ruling 2026-08-09; `MarkLogic_QuestionPolicy.md` §৬ (verified silent on duration); CD-019 |
| CD-022 | 2026-08-09 | **`tools/images/` vendored and proven — Step 2 CLOSED.** Vendored from the storybook pipeline as neutral tooling only: `apply_strips.py`, `make_strips.py`, `crop_edges.py`, `pick_placements.py`. **The expected "pdftoppm verification helpers" did not arrive and do not exist** — none of the four scripts touches pdftoppm — so `verify_strip.py` was **written here** to measure band centre, span and coverage **on the output raster**; without it `apply_strips.py` could only be shown to run, not to place the band correctly, which is not proof. Smoke-tested on a synthetic fixture with a **known** being region (x 0.30–0.62, y 0.12–0.88): band centre measured **0.460 against 0.460 requested**, inside the being region, span within tolerance; no-living-being images copied through **byte-identical**; eyeball-confirmed the band crosses the being only, not the page. Three negative tests fire red (wrong x · no strip · strip off the being). **Calibration recorded not tuned away:** feathered strip ends make the measured span read ~2% inside the requested one, so the working tolerance is 0.03 and anything below ~0.025 fails on correct geometry. `pick_placements.py` is **VENDORED-UNPROVEN** and cannot be proven headlessly — it is an interactive tkinter GUI, so "executed" means a human clicking real artwork. Boundary restated: **CD-007 keeps the stripe *doctrine* out of school canon**; this folder supplies the mechanism only, and when a stripe belongs may not be inferred from it. `tools/assets/` remains DEFERRED (CD-009). | Principal ruling 2026-08-09; CD-007; CD-009; CD-020; SLOTTING_CHECKLIST Step 2 |

The generator is the living class-test print path; R-2 (fonts as supplied, no Nikosh) closes with it. **Reference-CT ruling: the two papers in `tools/render/reference/` are NOT edited.** They are the historical record of class tests actually given — an **imported reference corpus, not live templates**. They are marked **"FORMAT reference only"** in `tools/render/README.md`, in `SMOKE.md`, and in a new `reference/README.md`: their `৪৫ মিনিট` line is superseded by the 35-minute rule (CD-021) and **must never be copied**. **The generator's config default is the living rule; the references teach structure, not time.** Same principle covers their `৩ক`/`৩খ` headers (CR-001) — the header comes from `--ct-number`, never from the reference. It broke ties on length, so a comma decoding to `I` made `Suddenly,` score better as `puddenlyI` and report itself missing. It deleted running heads from the stream, eating `unit` inside `United Nations`. **A third encoding was found**, a −29 display subset (`a^fiv kbtp` = `DAILY NEWS`). | CD-051 | 2026-08-09 | **Both source gates were English-shaped, and one of them returned a false green on the first Bangla file — fixed, and the fix proved against all twenty English units.** **(a) The false green, kept on the record.** Run against `Class 5 Bangla.pdf`, `source_textcheck.py` printed **`VERDICT : AGREE — the channels account for each other completely`** and exited 0, having compared **zero words against zero letters**. That is the worst available failure here, because **§7.4 buys reduced spot-check depth against exactly that Section B**. Three faults stacked: the body anchor took `^# Unit \d+` only, so a `# পাঠ ১` file was never sliced and the provenance header — md5, `ilovepdf`, `gitignored` — entered the comparison as words of the book; `letters()` kept `[a-z0-9]`, so the Bengali transcription contributed nothing; and **`str.isalnum()` is False for any Unicode Mn character**, which every Bengali matra, hasant and chandrabindu is, so `বাংলাদেশের` was rejected while `পাঠ` was kept — **three words standing out of a five-page chapter**. Fixing the anchor alone still printed AGREE; that intermediate run is recorded too. **(b) The gate can now refuse.** `source_textcheck.py` returns **REFUSE (exit 3)** — not AGREE, not DISAGREE — when it has nothing to compare: a stream under 40 letters per page (measured against this corpus: the English book's own layer runs ~680/page), no extraction words, or a majority-Bengali transcription against a stream holding no Bengali. **An absent channel must say so out loud rather than by staying quiet.** **(c) Scaffolding is defined by script, not by a stop-list.** §7.2(c) makes every extraction Bengali-scaffolded around a body in the book's language, so making `letters()` Bengali-aware injected `অনুশীলনী`, `ছাপা`, `পাঠ্যাংশ`, `পৃষ্ঠা` into Section A of **all twenty English units** — four invented disagreements each. A hard-coded stop-list would have hidden them and would also have deleted three of those four from a Bangla book, where they are printed on the page. **Regression-proved old script vs new across all 20 units: 0 of 20 differ** on Section A count, Section B count, verdict or exit code. **(d) `source_check.py`** now reads `পাঠ` as well as `Unit` in both the scope line and the body heading (a correct Bangla extraction was failing RANGE and PAGES on grammar alone); gains a **DEPTH** check enforcing CD-050(d); and **scopes PAGES monotonicity to the transcribed body** — the slot cross-reference cites pages per slot and commentary cites them per point, so an unscoped scan reported a correct file as jumping from page 5 back to page 1. **Unit 1 passed the unscoped version only because it happened to put its pages in a column — luck, not design**, and the same class of error already recorded in `parse_offset_table`. Its selftest now draws fixtures from **every extraction on disk** and picks one per seed, so the Bangla-grammar and single-channel seeds have something to bite on: **7 seeds RED, 21 controls CLEAN** (previously 6 seeds against a single hand-picked English fixture). **(e) No Bijoy/SutonnyMJ decoder was written, deliberately.** The only Bangla text layer in this book is p142, the back imprint, so a decoder would have nothing in scope to be proven against, and an unproven decoder inside a REQUIRED tool is what CD-020 exists to prevent. The refusal message names it as the missing capability so the next book that needs it says so. | Principal instruction 2026-08-09; CD-020; CD-046; CD-047; CD-048; CD-050; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-052 | 2026-08-10 | **`Class 5 Math.pdf` classified, offset re-derived from scratch, and the book's real structure read off the raster — production step ① opens for C5 গণিত.** **(a) §7.7 confirmed by measurement, not by family resemblance.** The book comes off the same publisher pipeline as `Class 5 Bangla.pdf` (Adobe Illustrator → iLovePDF, same 20 Dec 2025 ModDate), so the outlined-born-digital class was *expected* — and expectation is not evidence, so it was measured. The result is **more extreme than Bangla**: `pdftotext` over 190 pages returns **190 characters, which is exactly one form-feed per page and not one real character** (Bangla returned 421, of which 312 were the back imprint); **all 190 pages register zero fonts** — every page checked, not a sample; and every one of thirteen sampled pages holds **zero `BT`/`Tj`** against 5,208–76,887 curve operators. Bangla had at least one page with a text layer; **this book has none anywhere**. So §7.7 applies in full: **single channel, full human check book-wide, no §7.4 sampling**, and `source_textcheck.py` returns **REFUSE (exit 3)** naming the reason — *"0 letters over 16 page(s), under the 640-letter floor"* — which is an independent corroboration of the classification rather than a restatement of it. **(b) Offset +৭, derived for this book and carried from nowhere.** Bangla's offset is +৯; assuming it here would have been wrong by two on every page reference in the book. Verified at **18 points** spread across all 190 pages — the ten chapter-opening folios (১ · ১৭ · ৩১ · ৪৯ · ৬৩ · ৯১ · ১০৫ · ১১৫ · ১৪৯ · ১৭১) plus eight interior folios read off the raster (pdf 9→২, 10→৩, 50→৪৩, 90→৮৩, 101→৯৪, 140→১৩৩, 167→১৬০, 188→১৮১) — constant throughout. **(c) Structure read against the book, both ends.** The সূচিপত্র (PDF 6) lists **ten অধ্যায় and stops**; all ten openings were confirmed on the printed page with banner and number, and **every chapter's last page was confirmed too** — each carries the preceding chapter's content, with no gap, divider or unlisted section between them. **Printed ১৮১ (PDF 188) is the last numbered page; PDF 189 carries সমাপ্ত in place of a folio; PDF 190 is the back cover. There is no glossary, answer key or appendix after অধ্যায় ১০.** This is the check CD-050 had to make on Bangla after "২৪+" turned out to be a range nobody had read against a book; it is made here **before** extraction rather than after. **(d) No excluded chapter, and that is recorded as not-yet-checked rather than as a finding.** Bangla's পাঠ ১২ is excluded on Islamic-values grounds (CD-050(b)); nothing of that kind has appeared in this book so far, but only অধ্যায় ১'s first page has been read, so the statement carries its own limit: **it is verified chapter by chapter as the extraction reaches them, never declared in advance.** | Principal instruction 2026-08-10; CD-004; CD-029; CD-037; CD-045; CD-050; CD-051; SOURCE_POLICY §2.1, §7.5, §7.7; AGENTS.md §5 |

After the fix the same file reports `grammar : chapter word 'অধ্যায়'`, RANGE PASS and PAGES PASS with the offset constant at +7 over 18 rows. **The list has now been wrong once per subject**, which is the durable finding: a gate that hard-codes the vocabulary of the subject it was written for reports red on correct work in every subject it was not, and the next subject will look exactly like this. **(b) `COMMENTARY` needed the same two headings** (`## এই অধ্যায়ে যা নেই`, `## এই অধ্যায়ে যে নামগুলো আছে`) in **both** gates, or `transcribed_body()` would not cut and PAGES monotonicity would count the commentary's page citations as body references — the precise error CD-051(d) scoped that check to avoid. `source_textcheck.py`'s body anchor took the same word: on this book a mis-slice can no longer produce a false AGREE (the REFUSE guard catches it), but it would corrupt the comparison silently on the first Math book that *does* carry a text layer. **(c) A collision between two rules, found by running the gate rather than by reading it.** CD-051 widened the selftest fixture pool to **every extraction on disk** so the Bangla-grammar and single-channel seeds had something to bite on. The controls assert an unmutated fixture is not red — which silently assumes every extraction on disk is **finished**. AGENTS.md §3 requires the opposite: work in progress lives in files under `_wip/` so a killed session is resumable. On a 181-page Math book that cannot be transcribed in one session, a half-built chapter on disk is the **normal** state, and it turned the selftest **`[FALSE+ ] control · C5_MATH_Source_01.md must not be red`** and **`SELFTEST: FAIL`** — a red tool gate for the entire repository, caused by a file correctly reporting that it is not finished. **Resolved in code, not waived in chat (AGENTS.md §5):** a file may declare `**অবস্থা:** নির্মাণাধীন` and is then excluded from the pool, **and the selftest prints every file it skipped and why** — an extraction can be held out, but never quietly and never by accident. `run()` still checks such a file and still reports it red; the marker buys nothing except exclusion from being used as a control. **The removal of that line is written into the resume instructions**, because a marker that is never taken out would keep the finished file out of the selftest forever. **(d) Re-proved, not assumed:** `source_check.py --selftest` **8 seeds RED, 32 controls CLEAN, PASS**; `source_textcheck.py --selftest` **10/10 PASS**; `tools_check.py` and `canon_check.py` both CLEAN. | Principal instruction 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-040; CD-046; CD-047; CD-050; CD-051; CD-052; AGENTS.md §3, §5, §6 |
| CD-054 | 2026-08-10 | **PENDING-P-014 RULED: multi-session extraction at full resolution — the schedule gives way, never the dpi.** The first Math book made the cost of §7.7's full-eye depth measurable: a true 400 dpi page must be delivered in **four** tiles (a larger tile is downscaled in transit, so the rule is broken by the delivery, not the render), which is **≈720 reads for 181 printed pages** — beyond one session. **The Principal ruled no relaxation.** Two reasons are on the record rather than assumed: **(a) every near-miss so far lived exactly where dpi was thinned** — the five Bangla cases (`ঝকঝক`→`ঝকঝাক`, পাঠ ৪'s letter date, পাঠ ৬'s chandrabindu, `শাঁখ`→`শীঁখ`, and `প্রচণ্ড` transcribed `প্রচন্ড`, which reached a commit before CR-001 caught it); **(b) in গণিত the exposure differs in kind, not degree — a mis-read numeral becomes a wrong answer key, and a book with no second channel has nothing downstream that will catch it.** Digits are the highest-stakes glyphs in the pipeline. **Cadence ruled: one or more complete অধ্যায় per session at full care · checkpoint-commit per chapter · stated resume point in `STATE.md` · a fresh session each sitting.** A session whose care is degrading stops at the resume point rather than finishing a chapter badly. **A ten-chapter book is about a week of sittings, and that is the accepted cost of a source with no second channel** — an agent that thinks the trade is worth making raises a PENDING row, it does not make it. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.8** (v1.4). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10 closing PENDING-P-014; CR-001; CD-050; CD-052; SOURCE_POLICY §7.4, §7.7; AGENTS.md §6 |
| CD-055 | 2026-08-10 | **PENDING-P-015 RULED: `নির্মাণাধীন` adopted as convention — AGENTS §3 and the fixture discipline reconciled, not traded off.** §7.8's cadence guarantees a half-built extraction sits on disk between sittings, and AGENTS.md §3 requires precisely that so a killed session is resumable; CD-051's fixture pool draws controls from every extraction on disk and asserts an unmutated one is not red, which assumes each is finished. **Both rules are right, and together they reddened the whole repository's tool gate the first time a chapter was interrupted.** The Principal ruled the self-declaration is the reconciliation: an unfinished file carries **`**অবস্থা:** নির্মাণাধীন`** in its header with what is transcribed, what is not, and where to resume; it is excluded from the selftest's **controls only**, and **the selftest prints every file it skipped and why** — held out of the pool is permitted, held out quietly is not. **The marker is not a waiver:** `source_check.py` still runs on such a file and still reports it red, and SIGNOFF, DEPTH and every other check are untouched. **Removing the line is part of finishing the chapter** and is written into the resume instructions, because a marker nobody takes out is the same silent-exclusion failure pointing the other way. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.9** (v1.4) so the next subject inherits it rather than rediscovering it. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10 closing PENDING-P-015; CD-020; CD-051; CD-053(c); CD-054; AGENTS.md §3, §5 |
| CD-056 | 2026-08-10 | **AGENTS.md §9 amended (v1.1 → v1.2): a rename-aside destination must be outside every git-parsed path.** The §9 practice of renaming lock files aside exists because the Cowork sandbox cannot unlink inside `.git/`. It was written without saying **where** the aside goes, and the default — leave it where it was — put a `main.lock.aside-…` inside **`refs/remotes/origin/`**, where git parses every entry as a ref. The next session's `git pull` died with **`fatal: bad object refs/remotes/origin/main.lock.aside-1786332402816460410`** and `did not send all necessary objects`: a failure that reads like a corrupt remote and is nothing of the kind, **created by the workaround itself**. **Ruled: asides go to `.git/lock-debris/` directly — the only destination — and never remain inside `refs/`.** A second observation is recorded with it, because it is what makes the first one bite repeatedly: **each git write re-creates the lock it cannot remove**, so a successful `git add` still leaves `index.lock` behind and the *next* command fails. The lock is moved aside immediately before **each** git write, not once per session. Today's failure is named in §9 as the reason, so the next agent meets the explanation before it meets the error. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-040; AGENTS.md §9 |
| CD-057 | 2026-08-10 | **`math_arith_check.py` built, seeded and proven — and it caught a real agent error on its first run against real content.** **(a) Why a gate and not a script.** SOURCE_POLICY §7.7 records that this book has no text layer, so `source_textcheck.py` REFUSEs. True of the *file*; not true of the *content* — **mathematics carries its own redundancy**: partials must sum to the total and each partial must equal multiplicand × multiplier digit × place value, so **a mis-read digit does not balance**. That is exactly the failure CD-054's full-resolution ruling exists to catch, and by CD-020 a script run once is not evidence. `tools/audits/math_arith_check.py`, MANIFEST row **REQUIRED** (`tools/audits` is SMOKE-exempt — a gate evidences itself, and its verbatim run is the record). **(b) The three limits are in the docstring verbatim** and are the point rather than a disclaimer, because a check whose reach is overstated buys depth it has not earned: **computed working only · words, names and instructions out of scope · problem-statement figures unchecked where the book prints no working.** **(c) Seeded selftest, 16 cases, all PASS:** a flipped digit in the total, in a partial, in the multiplicand, in the multiplier and inside a blanks-block each turn it RED; a dropped partial row is RED on shape; a step table that does not balance is RED; and — the branches that matter most — an under-determined block reports **AMBIGUOUS**, a pure scaffold reports **WIDTH**, and a file containing only those returns **REFUSE, never CLEAN**, so a depth claim can never rest on a block where no digit was verified. **(d) The first real run went RED on two blocks, and the gate was right.** কাজ ৩(১), ৮৩৪৬ × ১৫৪৫: the agent had transcribed **eight** empty boxes in the fourth partial row where the book prints **seven** — ৮৩৪৬ × ১০০০ = ৮৩৪৬০০০ is seven digits, and with no zero in the multiplier the widths ৫·৬·৭·৭ are arithmetically forced. Logged as **CR-002**. **The digits were perfectly legible at 400 dpi; the count was wrong. High resolution protects reading; only a second channel protects counting** — the two answer different failures and neither substitutes for the other, so CD-054 stands untouched. **(e) The second RED was the gate's own blind spot, and became a rule.** কাজ ৩(২), ৬২৫৮ × ৬০৯৭: the third partial is ৬২৫৮ × ০ × ১০০ = 0, one digit, and **the book still prints six boxes** — it does not narrow the scaffold for a zero multiplier digit. Checking that width reddens a correct transcription, so zero-digit rows are excused **by rule, in code, with the book cited as the reason**, and the exemption is itself seeded: a zero-digit block with a conflicting printed digit still goes RED. Recorded in the extraction's `যেভাবে ছাপা আছে` as a feature of the book, not an error. **(f)** Reduced depth where the check reports CLEAN is recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.10** (v1.5); AMBIGUOUS and WIDTH earn nothing. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-050; CD-052; CD-054; CR-001; CR-002; SOURCE_POLICY §7.4, §7.7, §7.10; AGENTS.md §5, §6 |
| CD-058 | 2026-08-10 | **`math_arith_check.py` extended to equality chains — the distributive check, built as an evaluator rather than three pattern-matchers, and it retro-covers printed ৪–৭.** **(a) One idea covers four printed shapes.** Distributive expansion (`A × B = A × (b₁ + b₂ + …)`), the `(X − ১)` trick, the ×১০০ rearrangement box and the split-cell ladder table are the same claim in different clothes: **every fully-numeric way of writing a line must come to the same number.** So the gate now carries a small recursive-descent evaluator over `+ − × ( )` in Bengali numerals — **no `eval`, because an extraction is input** — and compares the `=`-separated segments of each line, continuing a chain across blockquote lines that begin with `=`. A segment containing `☐` is never evaluated: it is an exercise blank, not a claim. Written as an evaluator on purpose, so shapes not yet met are covered on arrival instead of each needing its own matcher. **(b) Two parser fixes the real file forced.** The `| ৭৪ | × ২৯ | = | ২১৪৬ |` ladder splits its operands across table cells and the original `STEP_RE` could not see it. And **every distributive line on printed ৪ and ৬ was invisible for a reason worth recording: the book's item label.** `(১) ৬০৪২ × ১৫১৪` tokenises as a parenthesised number juxtaposed with another, so the whole segment failed to parse — while the ×১০০ box, which carries no item label, read fine. A leading `(১)`/`(ক)` label is now stripped before parsing. **(c) Seeded, both directions, as ruled:** distributive terms summing to the multiplier and a mutated term going RED; `(X − ১)` matching the left-hand number and both sides mutated in turn; the rearrangement chain holding and one line of it mutated; the ladder row balancing and not; and a `☐`-bearing segment left unevaluated. **Selftest 30 cases, all PASS.** **(d) The point of the exercise:** the extraction went from **8 verified to 18**, and the twelve hand checks recorded for printed ৪–৭ in `evidence/MANUAL_ARITH_p04-07_2026-08-10.txt` are now **machine-corroborated** rather than resting on one human reading. MANIFEST row unchanged — `math_arith_check.py` was already REQUIRED and `tools/audits` is SMOKE-exempt, verified at source rather than assumed. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-054; CD-057; SOURCE_POLICY §7.10; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-059 | 2026-08-10 | **Coverage honesty: `math_arith_check.py`'s summary must state what it did not read, so a flat number can never again pass for completeness.** Four printed pages were added to the first Math extraction and the verdict line still said `8 item(s) verified` — unchanged, because the gate does not parse those working shapes, and **nothing in its output said so.** A count of what passed is not a statement of coverage. The summary is now `CLEAN — N verified · M uncovered · shapes not parsed: …`, with a `NOT LOOKED AT` line above it, both fed by a census that walks every numeric-bearing line in the **transcribed body** (anchored at the chapter heading, so the header's own offset table cannot pad the count) that no check consumed, grouped by signature: **`÷ long division` · vertical-bar trailing-zero layout · blank-only exercise cells · `ARITHMETIC LINE NOT PARSED` · bare exercise (no printed answer to check) · prose carrying numbers.** Two of those categories are deliberately kept apart, because collapsing them would inflate the alarm and get the line ignored: a line with an `=` **asserts something the gate failed to read** and should provoke an extension, whereas a bare `A × B` exercise **asserts nothing at all** — there is no printed answer to check it against and no extension will ever change that. **Silence about unparsed content is acceptable only where the output names what it skipped.** Seeded: a file whose only unread content is a `÷` line must have `÷ long division` named in its summary. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-058; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-060 | 2026-08-10 | **`math_arith_check.py` extended to long division — the highest-coverage extension left in অধ্যায় ১, and the gate caught the agent citing it before it existed.** **(a) The invariants are as forced as multiplication's**, and the book prints all of them: **ভাগফল × ভাজক + ভাগশেষ = ভাজ্য**; **every subtraction row = ভাজক × that ভাগফল digit, in order**; and **ভাগশেষ < ভাজক at every step** — which is *why* the next digit is brought down, and the first invariant a mis-read digit usually breaks. **(b) Simulated, not read by column.** The check re-derives the whole working from the printed ভাজ্য and ভাজক and compares its own rows against the printed ones. Reading the layout positionally would make the gate depend on how an agent happened to space a code block; simulating depends only on the two numbers the book states. Intermediates are matched as an **ordered subsequence** so a book that omits a bring-down row does not redden a correct transcription, while a mis-read value still matches nothing and goes RED. A block carrying `☐` is **REFUSED, never guessed**. **(c) Seeded both directions — nine new cases, selftest now 38, all PASS:** ভাগফল, ভাজক, ভাজ্য, a subtraction row, an intermediate bring-down row and the ভাগশেষ each mutated in turn to RED; a clean division and a clean division-with-remainder as controls; and the `☐` path REFUSED. **(d) A bug worth recording because it hid in the arithmetic, not the parser.** The first `simulate()` brought the next digit down *inside* the loop as well as at the top of the following iteration, double-counting it: ৪২৭৫ ÷ ৪৫ produced subtrahends `[৪০৫, ২২৫০]` instead of `[৪০৫, ২২৫]`. **It would have been invisible on any one-step division** and was only caught by running the simulator over eight real divisors before wiring it in. **(e) `canon_check.py` red-flagged this row before it was written.** The code cited `CD-060` while `canon/DECISIONS.md` did not yet contain it, and the repo-wide gate reported **`FAIL CD-CITE: CD-060 cited in tools/audits/math_arith_check.py but not in canon/DECISIONS.md (phantom citation)`** — AGENTS.md §4's phantom-citation rule doing exactly its job, on the agent, in the same session. **(f)** The census's `÷` category was narrowed at the same time: `÷` **with** an `=` is unread working; `÷` **without** one is a bare অনুশীলন exercise with no printed answer, and filing those as an unparsed shape would report a gap no extension can ever close. MANIFEST row unchanged — already REQUIRED, verified at source. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-054; CD-057; CD-058; CD-059; AGENTS.md §4, §5 |
| CD-061 | 2026-08-10 | **Pedagogical partial workings: the fence convention IS the rule — no parser extension.** The book teaches division by printing **two boxes for the same division** — first the opening step alone (৬৬৭৪ ÷ ২১৪ showing ভাগফল `৩`), then the complete working (`৩১`, ভাগশেষ `৪০`). `math_arith_check.py` treats any fenced block as a *complete* division, so fencing the partial box turns the gate **red on a correct transcription**. **Ruled: the convention, not the code.** A pedagogical partial working **stays outside the ``` fence, with its reason written in the extraction**, keeps **full manual depth**, and is corroborated wherever the complete working of the same division is fenced on the same page — which is the case here: **every digit of the partial box also appears in the complete box, and the complete box is machine-verified.** The gap is therefore smaller than it looks, because the numbers the partial stage shows are pinned by the working that follows it. **Why not extend the parser:** a partial-stage mode adds a new failure surface — the gate would have to decide what "incomplete" means and could then mis-classify a genuinely wrong complete working as a partial one and pass it. **The honest gap is smaller than the extension's risk**, and CD-059's census keeps the unfenced lines visible rather than silent. **Revisit condition, stated so a later session does not improvise:** if a book ever prints a partial working with **no complete working anywhere**, that is a **PENDING-P row**, not an agent decision. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-059; CD-060; SOURCE_POLICY §7.10; AGENTS.md §5, §6 |
| CD-062 | 2026-08-10 | **`math_arith_check.py`: an equality chain may not jump over a line the evaluator could not read — a false-RED bug, fixed the session it appeared.** অধ্যায় ২'s very first worked equation reddened a **correct** transcription: `৮ × ক = ৪৮` set the chain's carry; the next line, `ক = ৪৮ ÷ ৮`, yielded nothing evaluable (`÷` is not in the operator set, and `ক` is a letter); the line after it, `= ৬`, then chained ৬ back to the stale ৪৮ and reported **`equality chain does not hold: ৪৮ ≠ ৬`**. **The skipped line had changed the subject entirely** — which is what an unreadable line may always have done, so continuing a chain across one is never safe. The carry is now cleared whenever a line yields no evaluable segment: **silence is not continuity.** Seeded both ways — the অধ্যায় ২ shape must REFUSE rather than go red, and a genuine two-line chain (`৭৪ × ২৯` / `= ২১৪৬`) must still join. **Selftest 40 cases, all PASS; অধ্যায় ১ re-run unchanged at 24 verified, so the fix removed a false positive without removing reach.** **This is a bug fix, not the coverage extension the sitting's ruling barred:** a gate that reddens correct work cannot be left standing (AGENTS.md §5), and papering over it by contorting the transcription would have hidden the flaw while it recurred on every worked equation in the chapter. **A related hazard is recorded in the extraction rather than fixed:** this chapter teaches by printing **deliberately false** equations marked with a red ✗ (`৪৮ ÷ ৩ = ৮` ✗, `৪৮ ÷ ৪ = ৮` ✗, `৪৮ ÷ ৫ = ৮` ✗, then `৪৮ ÷ ৬ = ৮` ✓). They reach no check today **only because `÷` is unread — luck, not design** — and the file says so, so that whoever adds `÷` to the evaluator excludes the ✗-marked lines first, with seeds and a decision row. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10 (no new tooling this sitting unless a shape blocks transcription outright); CD-020; CD-057; CD-058; CD-059; CD-061; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-063 | 2026-08-10 | **Design note, ruled now and implemented only when the `÷`/`>` extension is built: a false-by-design line is CHECKED WITH INVERTED EXPECTATION, not excluded.** অধ্যায় ২ teaches গাণিতিক বাক্য by printing deliberately wrong equations and marking each with a red **✗**, then the right one with a green **✓** — `৪৮ ÷ ৩ = ৮` ✗, `৪৮ ÷ ৪ = ৮` ✗, `৪৮ ÷ ৫ = ৮` ✗, `৪৮ ÷ ৬ = ৮` ✓. The obvious handling is to exclude the ✗ lines from the arithmetic check. **The Principal ruled that exclusion is the wrong answer, and the reason is precise: it leaves a hole exactly where a mis-read hides.** If the book prints `৪৮ ÷ ৩ = ৮` ✗ and a transcription mis-reads the divisor as ৬, the line becomes **true** — and an excluded line is checked by nothing, so the error passes. **Inversion catches it: a ✗-marked line that balances goes RED.** Symmetrically, a **✓-marked line must balance**, exactly as any other identity. **The mark is data, and the check should read it** rather than treat it as a reason to look away. Note what this buys: on this chapter the ✗ lines become the *most* strongly checked content in the file, because a mis-read that flips a false statement true is precisely the error that no other channel would notice. **Scope and sequencing:** nothing is implemented today — the evaluator reads neither `÷` nor `>`, so no ✗ line reaches any check, and building inversion before the operators exist would be untested machinery. When the `÷`/`>` extension is built (its own timeboxed ruling), it carries inversion with it, seeded both ways: a ✗ line that balances RED, a ✗ line that does not balance CLEAN, a ✓ line that balances CLEAN, a ✓ line that does not balance RED. **Until then the extraction's warning block is the guard** — it names the ✗/✓ marks, says the lines are false by design, and forbids "correcting" them (SOURCE_POLICY §3). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-058; CD-059; CD-062; SOURCE_POLICY §3, §7.10; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-064 | 2026-08-10 | **`÷` and marked comparisons added to `math_arith_check.py`, carrying CD-063's inversion in the same commit.** **(a) Why now rather than later.** CD-063 sequenced inversion behind the operator extension on the assumption that the false-by-design hazard waited on `÷`. **ছাপা ১৯ disproved it:** the book's own worked trial `১ × ২৪ + ১৫০ = ২৪৬` → `২৪ + ১৫০ = ২৪৬` → `১৭৪ = ২৪৬`, verdict **মিথ্যা**, uses only `+` and `×` — operators the evaluator already read. Only the book's table layout kept it away from the checker. The Principal re-sequenced; this is that build. **(b) `÷` evaluates exactly, as `Fraction`.** Not floor division, which would make a mis-read *look* right (`৫০ ÷ ২৪` floors to ২, the same as the correct `৫০ ÷ ২৫`), and not float, which would import a tolerance this book never asked for. `১২ ÷ ৫` is exactly `12/5` and equals nothing else in its chain. Division by zero yields an unreadable segment, not an exception. **(c) The mark is read as data (CD-063).** `✗ ✘ মিথ্যা` set the expectation to *does not hold*; `✓ ✔ সত্য` to *holds*; **the mark is stripped before the line is split, or the segment carrying it evaluates to nothing and the chain silently vanishes — handing the mark's whole purpose back to luck.** Both equality chains and `>`/`<` comparisons are checked against it. **(d) An unmarked comparison is NOT assumed true.** ছাপা ১৮ prints `২৫ + ৪ > ৩০` with its verdict two speech-bubbles away; assuming a bare printed comparison holds would redden a correct transcription. No mark means no claim this check can test — reported uncovered, never RED. **(e) Seeded all four ways plus the boundaries — selftest 53 cases, all PASS:** ✗ that does not balance CLEAN · ✗ that balances RED · ✓ that balances CLEAN · ✓ that does not RED · the verdict **word** মিথ্যা read as the mark, both directions · a মিথ্যা comparison that is false CLEAN and one that is true RED · a সত্য comparison CLEAN · an unmarked comparison REFUSE · and a table-held marked block still REFUSE, so **CD-061's layout protection is now itself under test**. **(f) A CD-062 seed had to be re-cut, and the reason is worth recording:** its fixture `ক = ৪৮ ÷ ৮` stopped exercising the carry guard the moment `÷` became readable — the chain now legitimately joins. The guard is unchanged and still needed; the fixture was replaced with a genuinely unreadable prose middle line. **A seed that silently stops biting is worse than a missing one.** **(g) Yield:** অধ্যায় ২ went **3 → 8 verified** with ছাপা ২০'s `÷` chains now machine-checked; অধ্যায় ১ **unchanged at 24**, so reach was added without disturbing what was proven. **(h) Stated limit:** the ✗ lines on ছাপা ১৭ and ১৯ are table-held and therefore still unchecked — per the ruling, layout protection stands. Inversion covers the prose-form cases only, and those two pages remain at full manual depth. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-058; CD-059; CD-061; CD-062; CD-063; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-065 | 2026-08-10 | **Rendering choice ratified and promoted to canon: where two renderings are equally faithful, prefer the one the checker reads.** ছাপা ২২ of C5 গণিত forced the decision — the book prints সমাধান-১ and সমাধান-২ side by side in a bordered two-column box, and rendering that as a markdown table would have put **every printed digit behind a `|`**, where `math_arith_check.py` reads nothing. Two labelled blocks carry the same content and are read; the table form is not more faithful, only more literal about a presentational border. **Faithfulness stays the constraint and is never traded** — the rule only chooses *among* renderings that already satisfy it. The reason it matters is §7.7: on a single-channel source, a correct-but-unchecked transcription is weaker than the same transcription checked, and that difference is the whole margin. **The exception points the other way and is kept sharp:** for a block the book itself marks **false** (`✗`, or a verdict cell reading মিথ্যা), **protection comes first and it stays table-held** — CD-064's inversion reaches prose-form marked lines, so making an already-held block machine-readable would trade a working guard for a partial check. **The boundary is a stop, not a judgement call:** a false-worked block the book does **not** print as a table is **stop-and-ask**, a PENDING-P row. Inventing a layout to keep a gate quiet is the failure CD-061 rejected; inventing one to make a false block checkable is that same failure in reverse. **Promoted out of the workstream on purpose:** the rule was written into `canon/_wip/c5-math/STATE.md` when the page forced it, and **a rule that lives only in a workstream's state file dies with the workstream** — every subject that meets a bordered box meets this. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.11** (v1.6). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-057; CD-059; CD-061; CD-063; CD-064; SOURCE_POLICY §7.7, §7.10, §7.11; AGENTS.md §6, §8 |
| CD-066 | 2026-08-10 | **Pure-exercise convention: reduced page depth, undiminished numeral depth.** A section containing **only bare exercises** — no printed working, no printed answers, no `✗`/`✓` marks — is transcribed at the **150 dpi working render**, with the CR-001 high-resolution rule **applied to the numerals themselves via 400+ dpi spot-crops**. The reasoning is exact rather than economic: such a section has no worked line to mis-read, no answer key to corrupt, and nothing `math_arith_check.py` can read — but it **does** carry problem-statement numerals, and **a mis-read numeral there still reaches a question paper**. So the page and the numerals are separated: the furniture (instruction line, item numbering, layout) drops to the working render; the numerals do not. **This is not the relaxation CD-054 refused** — that rejected lowering dpi *for number-dense content*, and here the number-dense content is read exactly as before. **Everything else keeps full treatment unchanged:** worked examples, answer-bearing content, tables, marked lines, word lists, figures. **The test is the section's content, not its heading:** an অনুশীলন carrying one worked example is not a pure-exercise section, and a section stops being one the moment a printed answer is noticed in it. **Exercise-only sign-off rows state the convention**, so the Principal reads which depth each row was taken at instead of inferring it. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.12** (v1.7). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CR-001; CD-054; CD-057; CD-059; SOURCE_POLICY §7.8, §7.10, §7.12 |
| CD-067 | 2026-08-10 | **Cadence amended: as many complete chapters as full care allows, up to three per sitting — superseding §7.8's one-chapter line.** §7.8 is left as written (AGENTS.md §7, forward-only); **SOURCE_POLICY §7.13** carries what is in force. **Chapter close stays atomic and is never batched across chapters:** per chapter, full read → SLOTS → `নির্মাণাধীন` removal → sweep → checkpoint-commit. **Three half-closed chapters are not two closed ones.** **The stop-rule is unchanged and explicitly outranks the ceiling** — the moment care would thin, the sitting stops at a stated resume point, mid-chapter if that is where it lands. The standing reason is the near-miss record, not a preference: **`ঝকঝাক` for `ঝকঝক`, `প্রচন্দ` for `প্রচণ্ড`, `শীঁখ` for `শাঁখ`, and the eight-box count that should have been seven** — every one of them happened where attention or resolution had thinned, and the last reached a commit. **Three is a ceiling, never a target.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CR-001; CR-002; CD-054; CD-055; SOURCE_POLICY §7.8, §7.13; AGENTS.md §7 |
| CD-068 | 2026-08-10 | **A fourth source-input class: OCR-drafted — and the agent's role inverts from reader to verifier.** The Principal runs local OCR over the page rasters and stages per-chapter draft markdown in `_inbox/` alongside the PDF; the agent stops reading the book cover-to-cover by vision and starts hunting the draft for disagreement. **The motive is cost and is stated plainly:** vision-reading every page at 400 dpi ×4 tiles was the budget driver, and a machine that reads prose well enough to be argued with is cheaper than one that reads nothing. **(1) The draft is never trusted and never discarded.** It is a machine channel of exactly the standing a text layer has under §7.3 — a disagreement-hunting input, never an authority — and the §7.3 proof carries over unchanged: a channel's fluency says nothing about its truth, and the C5 English text layer returned plausible English with every comma silently gone. A passage may not enter an extraction on the draft's authority, and a draft reading may not overrule a crop. **(2) Verification depth is enumerated, not sampled at large.** The agent spot-crops and vision-reads at 400 dpi: **(a) every numeral** — digits are always where the marks live, and the test run's `৪→8`, `৭→٩`, `৫→¢`, Cyrillic `ЪΟ` are why; **(b) all known weak-glyph classes** — conjuncts, ণ/ন, শ/স/ষ, ড়/র, chandrabindu/hasant clusters, mixed-digit runs; **(c) every table cell, blank-box count and `✗`/`✓` mark** — CR-002 is the standing proof that resolution saves reading and does not save *counting*; **(d) headings, exercise labels and poem lines in full**; **(e) a random 10% sample of plain prose lines.** Prose the OCR and the sample-check agree on is accepted at draft value and recorded as **`OCR-corroborated`** in the sign-off row's depth column — a new depth value following §7.12's pattern, so the Principal reads which depth each row was taken at instead of inferring it. **(3) Every OCR-vs-crop disagreement is logged** in the file's `## চ্যানেল-অমিল` section with its resolution and crop citation. **This log is what the source class buys** — it replaces `source_textcheck.py`'s REFUSE, which on this book is the honest verdict of a channel that has nothing to say (0 letters, 190 pages, zero `BT`/`Tj` operators) and therefore guards nothing. **(4) All existing doctrine is unchanged:** arithmetic gates run identically; the fence rule (CD-061), `✗`/`✓` inversion (CD-063/064), §7.11 rendering choice, §7.5 artwork quarantine, Principal-only sign-off and `নির্মাণাধীন` (§7.9) all stand exactly as written. **Four amendments proposed by the Cowork agent and accepted, recorded in the same row:** **7.14.2a — the prose sample is a trip-wire, not a formality:** one substantive disagreement inside the 10% sample widens it to **25%**; a second **voids OCR-corroboration for that chapter**, which drops to raster-full. A sample that can never fail is not a check. **7.14.2b — the sample is deterministic:** seed = chapter id, and the sampled indices are logged, so a later reader can re-draw the same sample and the agent cannot re-roll until the sample is quiet. **7.14.2c — draft provenance is recorded in the header** (OCR engine, exact version, dpi, page range), and **three occurrences of the same weak-glyph pattern become a `PATTERN` row in the corrections ledger** per AGENTS §6, with promotion to an executing gate proposed. **7.14.3a — the draft is committed as evidence, not merely staged.** `_inbox/` is gitignored (§2.1); a draft left there would make the disagreement log cite a file no other device can see, and the corroboration claim unauditable. **7.14.4a — the pipeline buys no cadence.** The §7.13 ceiling and its stop-rule lift only after **three consecutive gate-GREEN §7.14 chapters**, and not before; a faster channel is a reason to verify more, not to close chapters sooner. **7.14.5 — first use is the C5 গণিত অধ্যায় ৩ resume, and it starts with a control-set diff:** the draft is diffed against the *already-verified* pages of the same chapter and the result reported **before any new page is touched**, so the channel is measured against known-good ground before it is trusted on unknown ground. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.14** (v1.8). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-046; CD-048; CD-050; CD-054; CD-055; CD-057; CD-061; CD-063; CD-064; CD-065; CD-066; CD-067; CR-001; CR-002; SOURCE_POLICY §2.1, §3, §7.3, §7.5, §7.9, §7.11, §7.12, §7.13, §7.14; AGENTS.md §5, §6 |
| CD-069 | 2026-08-10 | **The §7.14 trip-wire counts only the disagreements it was built to catch — 7.14.2a's counting rule superseded on the day it was written, by its own first control run.** **(a) The flaw.** 7.14.2a counts **any** substantive OCR-vs-transcription disagreement: one widens the prose sample to 25%, a second voids OCR-corroboration for the chapter. **It does not ask which channel was wrong.** The C5 গণিত অধ্যায় ৩ control set (ছাপা ৩১–৩৪, the pipeline's first use per 7.14.5) produced **four** disagreements, which read literally would have voided the chapter — **including the one where the OCR was right and our own পূর্ণ-depth transcription was wrong.** A rule that retires the pipeline for finding exactly what the pipeline exists to find is not a strict rule, it is a broken one. **(b) In force:** the trip-wire counts **only a substantive disagreement where the OCR loses on a plain-prose line inside the 10% sample.** **Excluded: the §7.14.2(b) weak-glyph classes and the §7.14.2(c) table / blank-box / `✗`-`✓` classes** — those are **crop-mandatory and never accepted at draft value**, so an OCR failure there measures nothing about the sample; the line was always going to be read at 400 dpi. **Also excluded: any disagreement the OCR wins.** That is **evidence the channel works**, and what it tightens is scrutiny of **our own prior reading** — it belongs in the corrections ledger as our error (AGENTS.md §6), not in the trip-wire count as the machine's. **(c) The threshold did not move, only what is counted toward it:** one counted disagreement still widens to 25%, a second still drops the chapter to raster-full. **(d) Applied to the run that exposed it: zero trip-wire hits, one canon error caught.** The three OCR failures — `পুরণ` for `পূরণ` (ূ→ু), `গল্লটি` for `গল্পটি` (ল্প→ল্ল), `সর্বনিয়` for `সর্বনিম্ন` (ম্ন→য়) — are all weak-glyph-class and all crop-mandatory. The fourth was **CR-003**: a **য-ফলা dropped from `উপলক্ষ্যে`** on a page already read at 400 dpi and already marked পূর্ণ, found by the machine channel on its **first** control run and confirmed by crop. `source_textcheck.py` REFUSEs forever on this book (§7.7, zero text layer), so **until this run the book had no second channel at all** — CR-002 proved dpi does not save counting; CR-003 proves dpi does not save a single reader. **(e) Recorded forward-only:** 7.14.2a is **left as written** with a SUPERSEDE NOTE, per the §2.3/§4/§6 house precedent; **SOURCE_POLICY §7.15** carries what is in force. The record therefore shows the rule was written direction-blind and corrected by measurement rather than by preference — **which is the provenance worth keeping.** Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.15** (v1.9). | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-068; CR-001; CR-002; CR-003; SOURCE_POLICY §7.3, §7.7, §7.14, §7.15; AGENTS.md §5, §6, §7 |
| CD-070 | 2026-08-10 | **`OCR-corroborated` becomes an enforced depth value, and cell ORDER joins the cell-crop clause — one ruling, because the policy line and the gate that holds it up are the same fact.** **(a) The hole.** CD-068 minted the depth value `OCR-corroborated`; for one session **nothing enforced it**. `source_check.py`'s DEPTH check asked only `পূর্ণ not in cell`, and the rows written under the new value read `OCR-corroborated + পূর্ণ (সংখ্যা)` — **so they passed because the string happened to contain পূর্ণ.** Substring luck, not a check. **A depth value the gate cannot enforce is a depth value the file can claim for free**, which is precisely the class CD-020 ("placed is not run") and CD-057 ("a script once run is not a gate") exist to close. **(b) The value is now recognised explicitly and is not free.** A row may claim `OCR-corroborated` only with **(i)** `পূর্ণ (সংখ্যা)` — the numerals themselves cropped at 400+ dpi, because **OCR corroboration never extends to digits** (§7.14.2(a)); **(ii)** a `## চ্যানেল-অমিল` section in the file — **corroboration with no disagreement log is a second channel that was never run** (§7.14.3); and **(iii)** for any row describing tabular content, a record that **cell order** was matched. Any other depth string is now a FAIL rather than a silent pass. **(c) Cell order, recorded above the workstream as SOURCE_POLICY §7.14.2c-i.** ছাপা ৩৫ produced the case: the OCR read **all eleven** numerals of a table correctly and **reordered them** — ১২ and ৩৬ drifted to the end. **A correct numeral in the wrong cell is as wrong as a misread one and is invisible to every spelling diff**, because nothing is missing and nothing is misspelt. The match is therefore positional — cell *n* against cell *n* — and the rule is **wired to the gate rather than left in prose**: the policy line and `check_depth`'s order test cite each other, so neither can drift without the other going red. **(d) Seeded, and the seeds are synthetic on purpose.** The only file carrying the new value is `C5_MATH_Source_03.md`, which declares itself `নির্মাণাধীন` and is excluded from the fixture pool by CD-055. Drawing these seeds from the pool would report BROKEN today and — worse — report **green** the day that file finishes, the silently-stops-biting failure CD-064(f) recorded. **The fixture is built inside the selftest, so every seed bites every run**, and the live file is additionally asserted straight from disk. **11 cases, all PASS.** **(e) The selftest earned its keep on its first run, against the author.** The order test was written with a substring match, and **`"ছক" in "একটি নিছক গদ্য-অনুচ্ছেদ"` is True** — নি+**ছক** — so a plainly non-tabular row was ordered to prove cell order it has no cells for, and **the gate went red on a correct file**: the same shape as the নমুনা-in-the-wrong-column bug already recorded in `check_depth`, and the shape that gets a gate ignored. Fixed to **prefix-not-substring** matching over letter-split tokens — Bengali is agglutinative, so `ছকের`, `ঘরগুলো`, `সারিতে` must still bite while `নিছক` must not — and **all three cases are now seeded in both directions.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-055; CD-057; CD-064; CD-066; CD-068; CD-069; SOURCE_POLICY §7.12, §7.14, §7.14.2c-i, §7.15; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-071 | 2026-08-10 | **Assamese `ৰ`/`ৱ` become an executed gate — `tools/audits/bangla_script_check.py` — because this is the error class a human eye cannot police and a machine polices perfectly.** **(a) The finding (CR-005, PATTERN).** The C5 গণিত অধ্যায় ৩ OCR draft emits **`ৰ` (U+09F0) where Bengali `র` (U+09B0) belongs**: **27 occurrences across 8 distinct words**, of which **18 are `প্ৰাথমিক`** — the running head of **every page of the book**. AGENTS.md §6's three-occurrence threshold was passed many times over. **(b) Why it is gate-able at all, stated exactly.** `ৰ` and `ৱ` are the **Assamese** letters of the shared Bengali-Assamese script; Bengali has `র` and `ব`. **There is no valid Bengali use of either character**, so a hit is never house style and never a judgement call — the check is **free of false positives by construction**, which is precisely the property that earns code rather than a proofreading habit. **(c) Why it matters more than its size suggests — and this is the strongest single justification for the §7.14 pipeline on record.** Against `প্রাথমিক`, the string `প্ৰাথমিক` differs by one glyph's inner curve at reading size. **No human proofreader catches that reliably.** **CR-003 is the exact mirror image:** the machine channel caught a dropped য-ফলা in `উপলক্ষ্যে` on a page already read at 400 dpi and already signed off at পূর্ণ depth — an error no machine-free process was going to find. **One error a machine misses and a human finds; one a human misses and a machine finds, in the same chapter, within one sitting.** Neither channel is the reliable one. **The reliability is in the disagreement between them**, which is what §7.3 asserted, what §7.14 was built on, and what is now measured in both directions rather than argued. **(d) Two exemptions, both narrow, both visible in the source.** A file declaring itself **`MACHINE OUTPUT`** (§7.14 draft header) is **counted, never failed** — a committed draft is *supposed* to carry these characters; it is evidence preserved byte-for-byte so the disagreement log can cite it, and correcting it would destroy the evidence. And a citation inside **backticks** is allowed, because **the log and the ledger must be able to quote the defect in order to name it** — a gate that forbids naming the defect makes the defect unwriteable. Bare prose stays clean; **fenced blocks are still checked**, since in an extraction a fence carries authored transcription. **(e) The backtick rule was itself found by running the gate, not by designing it.** The first repo sweep went **RED on `C5_MATH_Source_03.md` and `CORRECTIONS.md`** — the two files that *document* the error. That is a false positive of the same family as the নমুনা-column bug and the `নিছক` bug, and the answer was a **convention rather than a hole**: cite in backticks, and both files were one edit from correct markdown. **(f) The selftest rejected the author's own naming on its first run.** The constant table called U+09F0 `ASSAMESE LETTER RA`; Unicode calls it **`BENGALI LETTER RA WITH MIDDLE DIAGONAL`** (and U+09F1 `…WITH LOWER DIAGONAL`). The assertion that the codepoints really are the characters named caught it. **A gate that misnames its own targets invites someone to fix the wrong codepoint later.** **(g) 12 selftest cases, all PASS**, seeding both characters, both directions, the backtick convention, the post-code-span boundary, the fenced-block non-exemption, the declared-draft control, and the codepoint identities. **Repo sweep: 138 files, 0 in authored text, 54 in 2 declared drafts.** Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.16** (v1.11); policy and gate cite each other. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-068; CD-069; CD-070; CR-003; CR-005; SOURCE_POLICY §7.3, §7.14, §7.16; AGENTS.md §5, §6 |
| CD-072 | 2026-08-10 | **A long-division block could vanish from `math_arith_check.py` entirely — fixed, and the fix is ruled in two parts because the incident had two causes.** **(a) The vanishing, which is the serious half.** `parse_divisions` appended a parsed block, or appended an unparsed marker when the block carried `☐` — **but a block that reached neither branch was appended to nothing.** It was **not verified and not reported**, and **the census cannot name a shape the parser never returned**, so the extraction read as fully covered while two complete divisions on ছাপা ৩৮ went unchecked. **This is strictly worse than an uncovered shape:** CD-059 exists so unparsed shapes are visible, and CD-060(b) has always made a `☐` block REFUSE out loud. **In force: every exit path from a shape parser appends something; unparseable is a REFUSE with a line number, never an absence.** **(b) The cause of the miss — the parser was keyed to a house style, not to the book.** Subtraction rows were identified by a leading `−`. **C5 গণিত prints its long divisions with no minus signs at all**, just aligned digits under rules, so `subs` came back empty and the guard dropped the block. Measured rather than surmised: the same ৬৯০৫ ÷ ৪ block returned **`no minus -> 0 blocks; with minus -> 1 block`**. **(c) The transcription was not bent to fit the gate.** Adding `−` would have printed something the book does not print (SOURCE_POLICY §3). **The parser learned the layout instead:** the row immediately above a rule is the row being subtracted — which holds for the signed style too, so **one reader now handles both** and the signed path is untouched. **(d) The sibling check (AGENTS §6) came back clean, and it is the reason the two-style reader is right rather than merely convenient.** ছাপা ৮ was re-cropped at 400 dpi: the book **does** print `− ৪ ০ ৫` and `− ২ ২ ৫` there. `C5_MATH_Source_01.md` is faithful and owes no correction. **The book itself uses both conventions in different chapters — a gate keyed to either one alone would have been silently wrong on half of its own book.** **(e) Yield, immediate:** অধ্যায় ৩ went **10 → 12 verified**, and the two newly-read divisions **independently reproduced the hand-verification** recorded the previous sitting — ৫৩৯৪০ ÷ ৪ = ১৩৪৮৫ ভাগশেষ ০ and ৬৯০৫ ÷ ৪ = ১৭২৬ ভাগশেষ ১, every subtraction row checking out. **(f) Five new seeds, all PASS, and the ordering is deliberate:** a signless division CLEAN; signless with ভাগফল mutated RED; with one subtraction row mutated RED; with ভাগশেষ mutated RED; and **an unparseable block REFUSE — the seed that guards (a), whose point is not the label but that the block appears in the output at all.** Both pre-existing signed-style controls still PASS. Closes **PENDING-P-016**. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.17** (v1.12); policy and gate cite each other. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-020; CD-057; CD-059; CD-060; CD-061; CD-070; CD-071; SOURCE_POLICY §3, §7.10, §7.17; AGENTS.md §5, §6; PENDING-P-016 |
| CD-073 | 2026-08-10 | **The মই-ভাগ (ladder) becomes a named census shape — visible now, verified later, and the split is the ruling.** **(a) The gap.** C5 গণিত finds লসাগু with a ladder: the divisor line carries **several** dividends (`২ ) ১২, ১৮`), and each row is the row above divided entry by entry with indivisible entries **carried down unchanged**. `DIV_LINE` does not match it and `parse_divisions` returns nothing — **correct**, because a ladder is not a long division and simulating it as one would be worse than not reading it. **The defect was the next step: also nothing.** Claiming no shape meant CD-072/§7.17(a)'s never-vanish guarantee never engaged, and the census had **no name for a ladder at all** — so a block dense with this book's own hazard class (multi-column numerals in fixed layout, §7.14.2c) was invisible, on a page where চ্যানেল-অমিল row 5 had already caught the OCR **reordering** a multi-column row while reading every digit correctly. **(b) In force:** a fenced block whose divisor line matches `<prime> ) <n>, <n>[, …]` is reported **`ladder (মই-ভাগ)`, REFUSE**, with line number and rung count. অধ্যায় ৩'s two ladders now appear in the census where they previously did not; `uncovered` went 1 → 3, **and that increase is the fix working** — the honest number was always 3. **(c) The scope limit is the ruling, not a shortfall.** Verifying a ladder is a real evaluator — divisibility per rung, carry-down, and the left column's product against the লসাগু — and it is **a separate, larger decision**. Bundling it here is precisely the detour that cost a sitting at CD-072. **The shape is named and refused; its arithmetic stays hand-read at 400 dpi**, and `REFUSE` says exactly that. **(d) This names an already-checked shape, not an unchecked one.** Both ছাপা ৩৯ ladders were hand-verified before the gate existed: `২ ) ১২, ১৮ → ৬, ৯ → ২, ৩` with `২ × ৩ × ২ × ৩ = ৩৬` = লসাগু(১২, ১৮), and `২ ) ১২, ১৪, ১৮ → ৬, ৭, ৯ → ২, ৭, ৩` giving **২৫২** = লসাগু(১২, ১৪, ১৮), where ৭ carries down undivided per the book's own printed step (২). **Nothing was unchecked — only unnamed.** **(e) Six seeds, all PASS, in both directions:** a two-column ladder REFUSEs; a three-column ladder with a carried-down entry REFUSEs; each is **counted** by `parse_ladders` (the census can name it); and — the no-regression half — a **signed** long division and a **signless** one are each read as **1 division, 0 ladders**. **(f) The selftest caught a name collision the diff could not show.** The new fixture was first called `SYNTH_LADDER`, which was **already taken** by ছাপা ৫'s ×১০০ rearrangement box; it silently overwrote it and sent two unrelated chain cases to REFUSE. Renamed `SYNTH_MOI`. **Two different things in this book get called a "ladder" in English, and only one is the মই-ভাগ** — the fixture names now say which. **(g) Corroboration recorded while transcribing ছাপা ৪০:** the book prints **`নববর্ষ উপলক্ষ্যে`** there, with the য-ফলা. This **independently confirms CR-003** — the ছাপা ৩৩ correction was right and the book is consistent, so the OCR channel's find was a genuine catch rather than a coincidence of one page. Closes **PENDING-P-017**. Recorded as **SOURCE_POLICY §7.18** (v1.13); policy and gate cite each other. | Principal ruling 2026-08-10; CD-059; CD-060; CD-068; CD-072; CR-003; SOURCE_POLICY §7.14.2c-i, §7.17, §7.18; AGENTS.md §5; PENDING-P-017 |
| CD-074 | 2026-08-11 | **`math_arith_check.py` learns the decimal point, and `_num` stops dropping it — one commit, because the second makes the first safe (PENDING-P-019).** **(a)** `EXPR_OK` admits `.` and every numeral is read as an exact **`Fraction`, never float** — `০.১ + ০.২` is exactly `০.৩`, and a tolerance is a thing this book never asked for and would absorb the chapter's signature error, a point one place out. The two truncated forms the book actually prints are handled: `৪.` (ছাপা ৭৩) and `.৮৭` (ছাপা ৮৯). **(b)** `_num` **refuses** a decimal instead of stripping it. It used to return `৬` for `০.৬`; no live path reached it while `.` was rejected everywhere, and (a) makes decimals ordinary — a de-pointed divisor would turn a correct transcription RED or a wrong one CLEAN. Long division stays integer-only by design, so a decimal block REFUSEs and the census names it; extending the simulator is a separate ruling. **(c)** A chain never crosses a sentence separator (`;` `।` `·` `→` ` ও ` ` এবং ` ` আর `). Found by the gate itself on its first real run: an annotation reading `০.৩২ × ১০ = ৩.২ ✓; ০.৩২ × ১০০ = ৩২ ✓` was split on `=` across the whole line, so `৩.২` and `৩২` landed in one chain and a correct line went RED. Splitting *adds* coverage — that line is three verifications. **(d)** The mark belongs to the **statement**, not the line: a sign-off row naming both the `✗` block and the `✓` block of one printed item lent its `✗` to the `✓` half. A line carrying both kinds of mark now lends neither. **Measured effect on `C5_MATH_Source_05.md`: 8 → 143 verified chains; `÷ long division` uncovered 78 → 45, `ARITHMETIC LINE NOT PARSED` 26 → 12, nothing rose.** |
| CD-075 | 2026-08-11 | **A block is *expected wrong* on either of two signals, not one (PENDING-P-020).** CD-063 read the book's `✗` and inverted the expectation against it. ছাপা ৮৬ prints three more deliberately-wrong long divisions **with no mark at all** — the only signal is the instruction above them, *নিচের হিসাবগুলোতে কী ভুল আছে ব্যাখ্যা করি এবং তা ঠিক করি।* Keying on the mark alone would have reddened three faithful transcriptions: the same failure as skipping a `✗` line, reached from the other side. | CD-082 | 2026-08-12 | **A "both channels agree" control row requires independent third corroboration (Principal, 2026-08-12 — CR-010 elevated).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-081`; the only higher token in the repo is the constructed `CD-999` seed fixture from CD-080(e).)* **(a) Two-channel agreement is not corroboration when both channels can fail the same way.** The rotated marginal year on ছাপা ৯৬ read `২০২৭` in the crop and `२०२७` in the draft. **The agreement was manufactured by one printing, one glance and a wrong rotation direction** — two independent errors that happened to land on the same wrong digit. **The true value is `২০২৬`.** Had it been written, it would have entered the file as the *strongest-looking* row type: a two-channel control. **(b) The rule.** A row may be written as a two-channel control **only when something independent and third corroborates it** — a **second printing** of the same value, **series continuity**, an **arithmetic consequence**, or a **book-wide constant**. **Where no third control exists, the row is single-channel at full crop depth and says so.** ছাপা ৯৬ shows both sides of this in one page: the four five-digit numbers are printed **twice** (statement and table header), so their agreement is a real control; the year is printed **once**, so its was not. **(c) Rotation direction is a measurement, not an assumption** — prove it against a control every time, **rotating the same crop both ways side by side**. This is the **fourth member of the CR-002 / CR-003 / CR-006 family**: dpi does not save counting, does not save a single reader, is not magnification, and **does not fix orientation**. **(d) The third-control class is doctrine, not anecdote.** CR-009 (series continuity) and CR-010 (book-wide constant) are two instances of one thing: **controls that are neither channel are the only thing that can catch a two-channel agreement error.** **Look for one before writing any control row.** **(e) Sweep scope, recorded now and NOT run** (gate-sitting work, not mid-chapter per CD-078(c)): **every existing two-channel control row in অধ্যায় ৩ · ৪ · ৫ · ৬** — confirm each has third corroboration or **demote it to single-channel**. Queued behind the P-025 census re-run. | Principal ruling 2026-08-12; CR-002; CR-003; CR-006; CR-009; CR-010; CD-068/069 (§7.14.1); CD-078(c); CD-081; PENDING-P-025 |
| CD-083 | 2026-08-12 | **Push permissions are branch-state, not per-commit (Principal, 2026-08-12 — CR-011 elevated).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-082`; no `CD-083` token exists anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) The defect.** CD-079(b) and CD-081(a) grant permission to a **commit**. `git push` does not push a commit — it **advances a branch and carries every unpushed commit beneath HEAD**. A permitted commit stacked on a held one **releases both**. This is the **third instance of the canon's recurring shape**: CD-070's substring-vs-token (`"ছক" in "নিছক"`), CD-077's presence-vs-correctness (a slot ID appearing ≠ a slot correctly identified), and now permission-vs-ancestry. **A rule written in a finer unit than the one it is enforced in passes on paper and leaks in practice.** **(b) The new rule, superseding the per-commit form in CD-079(b) and CD-081(a):** **push is permitted only when EVERY commit in `git log origin/main..HEAD` is itself permitted. One held commit anywhere in that range holds the push.** **(c) Mandatory before every push, and stated in the push report:** the output of **`git log --oneline origin/main..HEAD`**, with **each commit named and classified permitted or held**, and **the permission it travels under**. *(This was the agent's self-imposed interim precaution after CR-011; the Principal ratified it as governance.)* **(d) Commit hygiene, binding: a correction to already-committed content is NEVER committed together with new extraction.** CD-081(a) cannot release a correction without releasing the page it rides with. **One commit, one class.** **(e) What already went out, recorded not reverted:** `c20e707` (ছাপা ৯৬) and `5a7ba50` (CR-009 + ছাপা ৯৫) **reached origin early under the defective rule**. Not reverted — **a force-push is a larger risk than the error**, and both commits are full-depth, gated and evidenced. **Early, not incorrect.** **অধ্যায় ৬ remains `নির্মাণাধীন` and unclosed; nothing downstream may source it.** **(f) The omission, not the push, is the part that matters.** CR-011's report stated that the CR-009 correction was pushed and **did not state that the ছাপা ৯৫ page travelled with it**. **(c) exists to make that omission structurally impossible** — a push report that must print the range cannot quietly under-report it. | Principal ruling 2026-08-12; supersedes the per-commit form in CD-079(b) and CD-081(a), whose text is unedited; CD-070; CD-077; CD-081; CR-011; AGENTS.md §3, §3.1, §5 |

The disagreement log and the corrections ledger must be able to quote `প্ৰাথমিক` in order to say it is wrong; a gate that forbids naming the defect makes the defect unwriteable. The classification performed in unification session 1 was performed **under this text held as draft**, which that session's report states rather than claims as compliance. **(b) Amendment accepted — §12.1 gets a prose lead.** The agent's own flag: every other section of AGENTS.md opens in prose and then tabulates; §12.1 opened straight into a table, which reads as an appendix rather than a rule. **(c) Amendment rejected, and the reason is the point.** The agent flagged §12.4's *same-ID-different-bytes → stop* clause as unenforceable, since a gate can detect the condition but cannot rule on it. **Rejected. The section's job there is to say whose call it is, and that is protocol, not gate logic.** A rule is not weaker for being addressed to a person rather than to a script. The rejection is written into §12.4 itself so the flag is not raised a second time by a later session reading the same clause. **(d) New §12.7 — retention.** Anything still staged in `_inbox/` at session close is **listed in that session's `SESSION_LOG.md` block with a one-line reason and a named owner**; a file appearing on that list three sessions running is raised to the Principal. **The measured condition that produced the rule:** at adoption `_inbox/` held **8 OCR drafts, 4 textbook PDFs and 2 font files with no review point at all**, four of them staged in April and carried through every session since without once being named. **`_inbox/` is gitignored (§12.1), so nothing in it appears in a diff, a commit or a pull** — accumulation there is invisible by construction, not by neglect. **Staging that accumulates silently is how a stale PDF gets picked up six weeks later as though it were current**, by a session with no way of telling: no date in the filename, no header, no row anywhere recording when it arrived or what supersedes it. **(e) §12.7 is applied retroactively to this session's own close**, so the rule's first exercise is the session that made it. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (session-2 ruling 2); CD-026; CD-034; SOURCE_POLICY §2.1, §7.14; AGENTS.md §2, §5, §8, §9, §12 |

Two different rows held one number for two days with three live citations pointing at the older one. **The Math lane's CR-012 keeps the number**: it was written first and it is the one cited. **The Math lane was not touched**; only this repo's tools ledger renumbered. **A next-free check that reports a number free when it is not is worse than no check** — it converts a collision into a documented non-collision, which is what happened here. **(c) `_ref06_header.txt` moves `canon/refs/` → `tools/`.** **Canon holds authority; a build input is not authority.** The file is the DERIVED banner block `cat`-ed onto REF-06's markdown twin at each regeneration — an input to a command, not a thing anything cites — and it sat in `canon/refs/` with a `canon/MANIFEST.md` **REQUIRED** row, which to any later session walking that index reads exactly like the LOCKED REF files around it. | CD-102 | 2026-08-14 | **§3.10 — six Bloom levels are stored, four NAPE domains are derived.** The LOCKED payload's enum is the **six English Bloom levels** and it has **no domain field**; NAPE mandates the four domains (MarkLogic §৩) and the teacher's ছক requires a domain column — *"এই ছক ছাড়া প্রশ্নপত্র জমা নেওয়া হবে না"*. **Mapping, fixed here: জ্ঞানমূলক→Remember · অনুধাবনমূলক→Understand · প্রয়োগমূলক→Apply · উচ্চতর দক্ষতামূলক→Analyze + Evaluate + Create.** Storing six and deriving four keeps the payload conformant without a schema change and keeps the finer distinction the Bloom layer makes. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` v1.0 §10; MarkLogic_QuestionPolicy §৩; LOCKED_QuestionPayload_Schema_v1.json |
