| CD-110 | 2026-08-14 | **§3.18 — two topic-ID authorities, neither demoted.** `ref19_topic_id` is a **slug** (`BAN-POEM`), owned by **REF-19** and hard-validated by the harness; `topic_tag` is a **number** (`TOP-BAN-C5-05`), owned by **`canon/topics/TOPIC_NUMBERS.md`** under CD-044. **REF-19 has never carried numbers** — verified at source again this session: it contains **zero `TOP-` strings** and no slug with a numeric suffix, exactly as CD-043 recorded. **Both fields are required on every item and neither derives from the other**; it is correct for them to disagree in granularity. **`TOPIC_NUMBERS.md` is NOT demoted** — an earlier draft proposed that on the false premise that REF-19 carried numbers. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` v1.0 §10; CD-043; CD-044; REF-19 |
| CD-111 | 2026-08-14 | **The bank shape is fixed (QUESTION_POLICY §4).** **Unit:** one Pool per chapter, per class × subject, topic-tagged within. **Sizing:** floor **20 per chapter** per **REF-09 §4.3**, **no ceiling** — the real bound is content, and the governing test is **coverage, not count**: every topic, every question-type its spine slots need, every difficulty band. Built in waves, each sized to what the extraction supports, with the per-chapter target and its one-line reason **stated in the bank file header**. **Bloom scope is the chapter (D-050)** — within a multi-period chapter the mix **climbs**, and a bank built session-by-session **drifts low**. **Metadata:** the ten LOCKED-payload fields — `qid` · `topic_tag` · `ref19_topic_id` · `question_text` · `question_type` · `paper_role` · `bloom_level` · `difficulty` · `tier` · `marks` — **verified this session to match the schema's `required` array exactly and in order**, with `additionalProperties: false`. `paper_role` is a closed enum naming the **paper-section family**, never the pool; the pool label lives authoring-side in `pool_index` outside the payload, pending upstream **UP-002**. **Difficulty:** REF-09 §3's floor at both levels — easy ≥30%, hard ≤25% — and the Pool must be *able to supply* it. **Keys:** per REF-09 §5, no question is finished until its key is written; the schema enforces this by type. **Rubric (interim):** two bands and a single `islamic_alignment` criterion row, because a four-row rubric nobody marks from drifts from the scheme actually in use. **Answer length:** per MarkLogic §৭ — marks signal length and marking counts **distinct points, not lines**; in Math NAPE's rule is mandatory — **no working shown, no marks** — printed in the paper's instructions so no student meets it first at the scholarship exam. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` v1.0 §10; REF-09 §3, §4.3, §5; REF-08 §4; MarkLogic §৭; master D-050; UP-002 |
| CD-112 | 2026-08-14 | **The repetition rule (QUESTION_POLICY §5) — and MarkLogic §৮ is superseded in ONE row, for `Remember` items ONLY.** **In force:** `bloom_level: Remember` items may be reused **verbatim** across HW · AS · CT **and into the half-yearly and annual papers**; at every other level verbatim reuse is barred, though same chapter and same type remain fine. **What is overridden, named exactly:** §৮'s table row **`শ্রেণি পরীক্ষা ও বড় পরীক্ষা`**, whose চলবে না cell reads **`হুবহু একই প্রশ্ন`**. **Every other §৮ row and every other Bloom level stands unchanged.** **Why the override is right:** §৮'s stated risk — a student memorises the CT answer and the annual measures nothing — is sound for reasoning and **false for recall**. MarkLogic §৩ fixes শব্দার্থ, নামতা and বর্ণমালা as জ্ঞানমূলক by construction: there is one correct meaning for a word, a second different correct question **cannot be invented**, and memorising the answer *is* knowing it. **A rule that cannot be followed for part of the syllabus is worse than one narrowed on purpose.** **Exposure is capped by the domain ratio and by nothing invented here:** MarkLogic §৩ sets C5 জ্ঞানমূলক at **৩০%, ±৫% and no more**, so **at most ৩৫%** of a C5 paper is eligible. **v0.2 wrote a flat ৩০% ceiling the source does not state and was stopped on it.** **No flag is authored** — `bloom_level` is already required, so the rule is machine-checkable as it stands. Also binding per MarkLogic §৬: at least one extended or উচ্চতর item in **every** class test, every question type appearing at least twice **across the year's tests**, and the domain ratio met **across the year, not within any single CT** — 25 marks cannot carry four domains in proportion. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` v1.0 §10; MarkLogic_QuestionPolicy §৩, §৬, §৮; QUESTION_POLICY §9 |
| CD-113 | 2026-08-14 | **The gate list is fixed and ordered (QUESTION_POLICY §6).** **1.** `build_question_envelopes.py` fans the chapter bank into per-item envelopes. **2.** `validate_import.py` L1–L4 on every envelope, born conformant, **verbatim output pasted** (AGENTS §5) — **`--envelope-schema tools/hub-export/import-contract.schema.json` must be passed explicitly**, per SMOKE.md finding V-1, verified at source. **3.** `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`, eleven checks: mark value against spine values · source traceability to the chapter extraction · script guard per LANGUAGE_RULES §7 · `ref19_topic_id` against REF-19's slug set · `topic_tag` against `TOPIC_NUMBERS.md`, where **an unminted number FAILs and is never auto-minted** · key/rubric present per type · Bloom band at **chapter** scope · difficulty easy ≥30% / hard ≤25% with the Pool *able to supply* · repetition (no verbatim reuse above `Remember`) · coverage of every topic and spine slot-type · **domain ratio at paper level only, never per pool**. **4.** Anchor coverage per REF-26. **5.** Human review per REF-09 §9 — **the Principal acts as Subject Lead until Subject Leads are named**. **6.** Hub import as `draft` → teacher in-app review → Principal promotes `reviewed → gold` (CD-003). **Every gate carries a seeded selftest that bites each run, from synthetic fixtures, never drawn from the live pool** (CD-055, CD-064(f)). **The suite is NOT built by this row** — it is session 3's work, alongside CD-088(d)(ii)'s ledger-collision gate. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` v1.0 §10; AGENTS.md §5; REF-09 §9; REF-26; CD-003; CD-055; CD-064(f); CD-088(d) |
| CD-114 | 2026-08-14 | **REF-01 → v1.3: scope opened to all classes and all subjects, and the file is declared living / append-extensible.** *(Next free verified at source: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through CD-113, minted this session.)* **(a) What was wrong with v1.2's scope line.** It read *"In scope (this version): Class 1 Bangla and Class 1 English … Out of scope: other subjects … and higher classes"*. **The 19 categories were always written subject-general** — the scope line described how far the *first pass* had been applied, and **a later reader could not tell the two apart.** Read literally it put every C5 bank, every Math chapter and the whole islamic-studies lane outside the only curation policy the school has. **(b) In force at v1.3:** in scope is **all classes and all subjects**; out of scope is **nothing by class or subject**. Where a category has little or no incidence somewhere, that is noted in its entry — **it does not put the class or subject out of scope**. **(c) Living / append-extensible, modelled on REF-21's mechanism** and consistent with REF-20 §5: **appends never restructure**, so each new class or subject inherits everything the last one found, and **a data-fill does not bump the version; a structural change does**. This is what makes (b) executable rather than aspirational — without it every new subject would need a full supersede cycle before it could be curated at all. **(d) Nothing else in REF-01 changed.** v1.3 is byte-identical to v1.2 outside §1.2 and the header. **v1.2 is retained on disk, unedited, as the archive** (master §5.3); the superseded text is preserved verbatim in v1.3's §1.2 archive block, per supersede-with-archive — a pointer stub, not a banner. **(e) Pre-supersede dependent check run (master §5.3):** REF-01 is **not** a `LOCKED_ProductionCore_v1` source — those are REF-02 · REF-07 · REF-08 · REF-18 — so **no built-asset rebuild is owed.** Same result for REF-09, REF-10 and REF-25 below. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (continuation, Step 3); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9; master §5.3 (D-PROJ00-061 pre-supersede dependent check, run — no rebuild owed) |
| CD-115 | 2026-08-14 | **REF-10 → v1.1: §4's বৃত্তি bullet superseded, and only that bullet.** The bullet described the scholarship paper as *"Curriculum-based but at higher application, under time pressure, MCQ-heavy with some written"* — **written before NAPE 2026 was approved.** That paper's structure is now settled by `canon/marklogic/`'s Rules and five spines, which reproduce NAPE 2026 directly, so a prose sketch of it in a Tier-2 guideline is a second and older description of a thing canon now specifies exactly. **What is retired is the description of the paper, not the preparation method** — curriculum-true items at scholarship difficulty, timed practice so accuracy holds at speed, and scholarship-quality distractors all stand. **§4's Olympiad and Admission bullets are untouched**, as are §2–§3's stretch profile and every other section. Executed as a pointer stub with the original bullet archived verbatim beneath it; v1.0 retained on disk unedited. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (continuation, Step 3); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9; master §5.3 (D-PROJ00-061 pre-supersede dependent check, run — no rebuild owed) |
| CD-116 | 2026-08-14 | **REF-09 → v1.1: §4.1's `easy ≥ 40%` line superseded, and §4's totals explicitly NOT superseded.** **(a) The line goes** because under MarkLogic §৬ + CD-021 the class test is **lifted from the annual paper unchanged, at identical marks** — so its difficulty is **fixed by inheritance**. A separate CT floor would either contradict §3's own split (easy ≥30% / hard ≤25%) or force the lifted questions to be re-chosen, **which is exactly the shrinking §৬ forbids**. **(b) The distinction that had to be got right, and nearly was not.** §4's **mark totals** were **overridden** — by the permission §4's own preamble grants: *"recommended defaults; the school's exam scheme … overrides them"* — **not superseded.** The same preamble says the **structure rules (sections, balance, keys) are binding regardless of the totals.** **Treating the override as a supersede would have retired binding structure rules along with the numbers**, which is why §9 records it as an override and v1.1 changes exactly one line. **(c) §3's difficulty floor is untouched** and remains the floor QUESTION_POLICY §4 applies at both pool and paper level. v1.0 retained on disk unedited; superseded line archived verbatim in v1.1. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (continuation, Step 3); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9; master §5.3 (D-PROJ00-061 pre-supersede dependent check, run — no rebuild owed) |

| CD-119 | 2026-08-14 | **MarkLogic QuestionPolicy §৯ becomes a pointer plus a class-general summary, each heading naming its REF-01 C-code.** §৯ carries eleven subject-general restriction headings — নাম · ছবি · গান ও নাচ · অভিনয় · টাকা ও লেনদেন · উৎসব ও দেব-দেবী · জাতীয় বিষয় · কবি ও লেখক · সালাম · জিনিসপত্র · ইসলামি প্রসঙ্গ স্বাগত. **REF-01 v1.3 §4.1's C-01…C-19 are the stance authority**, and REF-21's own header already concedes it: *"Where the two differ on a stance, **REF-01 governs**."* **§৯ is therefore not a second policy but a paper-side restatement**, kept because an author writing questions in Bengali should not have to open a 62 KB English policy for a routine check — **and each heading names its C-code so the two cannot drift.** **On conflict REF-01 wins, and the stub says so.** This is the same shape as CD-084(a) from the other direction: where scaffolding and policy disagree, the scaffolding is the thing that moved. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (continuation, Step 3); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9; master §5.3 (D-PROJ00-061 pre-supersede dependent check, run — no rebuild owed) |
| CD-120 | 2026-08-14 | **`QUESTION_BANK_POLICY` amended at §2, §3, §4 and §5 — pointer stubs with the superseded text archived in place.** **(a) §2 — one Pool per chapter.** QB-D-001's three disjoint pools (HW · AS · CT) are retired to **selection labels**. Three authored partitions **triple the authoring cost**, make per-chapter coverage unmeasurable, and — decisively — **make an item's instrument an authoring-time decision that cannot be changed without rewriting the item.** A selection label can be re-selected; an authored partition cannot. **No CW pool** still holds. **(b) §3 — `tier` added.** The field list was **incomplete**: it named six fields where the LOCKED payload requires **ten**, and `tier` was the omission. Verified against `LOCKED_QuestionPayload_Schema_v1.json`'s `required` array on 2026-08-14. **A policy listing fewer required fields than the schema enforces produces banks that fail at the harness and look like tooling faults.** **(c) §4 — REF-09 §5's key wording, and the domain ratio moves to paper level.** *No question is finished until its key is written* — the key is not a later pass. And MarkLogic §৩ sets the ratio for a **প্রশ্নপত্র**: a Pool is not a paper, and a pool-level domain gate would force every chapter to carry four domains in proportion, **which §৬ itself says is impossible even for a 25-mark class test.** **(d) §5 — the gate list becomes `QUESTION_POLICY` §6's eleven checks.** Two old checks are retired **by the one-Pool ruling, not for convenience**: *zero-overlap across three pools* has nothing left to compare, and *domain-ratio per pool* is (c)'s error. **The suite is not built by this row — it is session 3's work.** **(e) The file is not LOCKED**, so the amendments sit inline as stubs rather than in a successor file; **the superseded text is preserved verbatim in every case** and the header records the amendment. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 (continuation, Step 3); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9; master §5.3 (D-PROJ00-061 pre-supersede dependent check, run — no rebuild owed) |
| CD-121 | 2026-08-14 | **Two `QUESTION_POLICY` §6 defects corrected in one row, because they were found the same way — by building the gates §6 specifies. (a) The Bloom-band row was wrong.** §6 read *"Pool spans the wider of REF-06 §3.6 / MarkLogic §৩ at each level"*. **"The wider of" is dropped.** Per **UD-23** the **Bloom axis governs the pool** and the **domain axis governs the paper**: they are two axes, not two ranges on one axis, which is exactly why *"each level"* had no common referent above Apply — REF-06 bands **six Bloom levels**, MarkLogic §৩ bands **four NAPE domains** and collapses Analyze+Evaluate+Create into উচ্চতর. The pool bands against **REF-06 §3.6 only**; **MarkLogic §৩ appears at paper level and nowhere else.** §3 row 9 is unaffected — it ruled that the two do not *conflict*, and they do not, because they do not measure the same thing. **(b) The seeded-fixture citation was false.** §6 closed with *"built from synthetic fixtures and never drawn from the live file pool **(CD-055, CD-064(f))**"*. Read at source, **neither row says it**: CD-055 is the `নির্মাণাধীন` self-declaration and CD-064(f) records a seed that stopped biting. **(c) How a docstring became a canon sentence, recorded as its own small pattern.** The string originates as a **docstring** in `tools/audits/canon_check.py:458`, written at **`95cb9b0` (CD-080)**; it was copied to `tools/audits/tools_check.py:222` at **`b9a9cc9` (CD-089)**; and it entered canon at **`109b232`**, carrying a citation nobody re-checked at any of the three hops. **A convention that hardens into a canon sentence inherits the authority of the file it lands in without ever having been ruled** — and the more places it is repeated, the more settled it looks. **(d) The rule's real home is `workstreams/question-banks/DECISIONS.md` QB-D-012** — *"a fixture is a fixture… Gates may read stable canon in their fixtures; they may not read files that change every session"* — raised to canon here, because workstream-local is not enough for a sentence canon states. **(e) And the flat form forbade what canon requires elsewhere.** QB-D-012's rule is about **seeds**. **Controls are a different thing and MAY be drawn from the live pool**: `source_check.py`'s selftest does exactly that **by design** (CD-051(d), *"fixtures from every extraction on disk"*), which is the mechanism CD-055 then reconciled with `নির্মাণাধীন`. The blanket sentence flattened a real distinction, so §6 now carries both halves: **seeds synthetic, controls may be live, and a file held out of the controls declares itself and is named in the skip list.** `QUESTION_POLICY.md` → **v1.1**; superseded wording quoted in place at each site, never deleted. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 closing Q-1/Q-6; UD-23; QB-D-012; CD-051(d); CD-055; CD-064(f); CD-080; CD-089; CD-113; AGENTS.md §4, §8 |
| CD-122 | 2026-08-14 | **The three §6 rows that were underdetermined are now read, and each reading is recorded with its reasoning so it is not "tightened" later. (a) DIFFICULTY — "can supply" means `easy ≥30% present in the pool`, and nothing else. There is no pool-level hard test.** The reasoning is the ruling, not decoration: **a pool cannot fail a *ceiling*.** An author can always decline to use hard items, so however hard-heavy a pool is, a compliant paper stays constructible from it. **Absence is the only thing a pool can be guilty of — and easy is the only side where absence in the pool makes paper-level compliance impossible.** If <30% of the pool is easy, no compliant paper exists; if 90% of it is hard, one still does. The `hard ≤25%` ceiling is real and binding **at paper level** (§4). ⚠ **A later symmetric check would redden correct pools**, so the reason is written into the gate's own docstring and into §4, and the seeded case that used to prove the hard ceiling is **kept and inverted** — a 67%-hard pool must now stay quiet — so the symmetric form cannot creep back in unnoticed. **(b) COVERAGE — the per-chapter spine slot-mapping does not exist as data, and that absence is now recorded so its arrival is a known trigger.** What exists is `C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md`'s *কোন প্রশ্নের জন্য কোন পাঠ* table, which runs the **other way** — slot → best-source পাঠ, with a separate বিকল্প column — plus each পাঠ's *কোন প্রশ্নে কাজে লাগবে* prose. **Inverting a best/alternative table into a per-chapter obligation is inventing the mapping**, which §4 forbids. §4's own fallback governs: coverage is read against the **header-stated target with its one-line reason**. The day a slot-mapping is committed as data, this gate changes; the gate's docstring is the notice. **(c) STALE-MARKER — a bank that passes every gate while still declaring itself `নির্মাণাধীন` is RED.** It is either finished-and-mislabelled or unfinished-and-lying, and SOURCE_POLICY §7.9 makes removing the line part of finishing. This is §7.9's own named opposite-direction failure — *"a marker nobody takes out would keep a finished file outside the selftest forever"* — given an executor. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 closing Q-2/Q-3/Q-4; CD-111; CD-113; CD-121; SOURCE_POLICY §7.9; REF-09 §3, §4.3 |

| CD-129 | 2026-08-14 | **`int_id_check.py`'s three design choices RATIFIED, and its sink widened by stating a RULE rather than lengthening a list (Principal ruling 2026-08-14, closing PENDING-P-033 items 5 and 8).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-128`, minted this session; no `CD-129` token existed outside this row.)* **(a) All three ratified as built: two tiers · the literal-scheme-prefix classifier · the `# int-id-ok: <reason>` waiver form.** **The two-tier split is the part that matters, and its reason belongs in the docstring rather than only here:** `INT-ON-CAPTURE-UNTYPED` **reports** rather than fails because `math_arith_check.py` legitimately applies `int()` to ~30 captured Bangla numerals — *they are quantities; that is the gate's whole job.* **A flat "every capture is an ID" form would have made CD-088(d)(i) unimplementable, and an unimplementable rule gets "fixed" by weakening it.** Reporting the unclassified sites is what keeps the ruling executable at full strength; it is also the only way the lint's own reach is measurable (`SOURCE_POLICY` §7.17 — a gate reports or refuses, it never omits). **(b) The sink is widened to `float()`, `Decimal()` and `str.zfill()` — but the LIST is not the ruling. The rule is: *any transform that can map two distinct ID strings to one*.** `zfill` collapses `U9` → `U09` exactly as `int()` collapses `U09` → `U9`, in the opposite direction, which is why **"collapse" is the right word for the family and "normalise" is the wrong one**. `float`/`Decimal` on an identifier are nonsense that *silently succeeds*. **Enumerating sinks is CD-088's own disease one level up — a list that looks complete and is not, hardening into a definition because nobody re-reads what it was a list *of*.** So the docstring states the rule **above** the instances, and records that **the next widening must be an addition to `SINK_CALLS`/`SINK_METHODS` and nothing else**: *if it required rediscovering the principle, the principle was written down badly.* **(c) Two limits stated rather than closed.** The rule is **wider than the four** — a hand-rolled `strip_leading_zeros`, a `%d` format, an `int` in a comprehension key all collapse IDs and are invisible here; that is written into LIMITS, not apologised for. And **the gate keeps the name `INT-ON-ID-CAPTURE` though it is now wider than `int()`**: a gate name that appears in commit messages and CD rows is itself an identifier, and **this is the file that argues identifiers are not tidied**. the source extraction: `int()` maps **`U09` and `U9` to one `৯`**, so two **distinct** qids would silently have read one chapter. **CD-088's PATTERN sitting inside the suite that judges every bank.** The resolution is now the pure function `qb_resolve_chapter(full, unit)`: **the raw captured string is translated digit for digit, padding intact, and the section lookup is EXACT.** Where the qid is padded and the book prints its chapters unpadded, the unpadded spelling is tried as a **second, named** attempt and the mismatch is **returned as a report line** — *the two spellings stay two spellings.* **A resolver that quietly accepted both would be the thing hiding the evidence that PENDING-P-034 has to be ruled at all.** Pure by design — it takes text, not a path, so its seeds are synthetic strings needing no file on disk (CD-121(e)). **Five seeded cases, both directions**, including the negative that the fallback fires **only** for a padded token. **(b) `gates.py` `C([1-5])` — WAIVED, and it is the repo's FIRST `# int-id-ok:`.** One digit by construction: **no padding is expressible, so no two distinct ID strings can collapse**, and `class_level` is compared as an ordinal 1..5. **It was written to be the example the second waiver is copied from**, so it states the test: **not "this is fine today" but "the transform CANNOT merge two distinct IDs here".** **(c) The two dispositions sit three lines apart in one regex, and that is the record.** Same file, same pattern, opposite answers — because `C([1-5])` cannot express a second spelling and `U(\d+)` can. **A rule that produced the same answer for both would be measuring the wrong thing.** **(d) Recorded, not smoothed: the agent committed (a) before running `canon_check.py`, so `6df4463` cites `CD-130` while this row did not yet exist.** The CD-CITE gate caught it on the next full run, which is what it is for — but **the gate is not a substitute for running the gate**, and a phantom citation reached a commit. `AGENTS.md` §5's rule is *run it, then commit*, and it was applied in the wrong order for one commit. Not reverted: the row exists as of this commit and the citation resolves. | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 closing PENDING-P-033(1)(2); CD-088; CD-088(d)(i); CD-121(e); CD-129; PENDING-P-034; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-131 | 2026-08-14 | **CD-127(b) gets an executor: `SOURCE-EXCLUSION`, the 22nd gate — and the declaration does NOT live where the proposal put it, because that design could not cover the case it was built for (Principal ruling 2026-08-14, closing PENDING-P-033 item 6).** **(a) Why a gate and not a sentence.** CD-127(b) existed only as prose in a decision row, and **an extracted পাঠ ১২ would have passed all 21 gates** — it looks exactly like পাঠ ১–১১ to every reader downstream. Two reasons beyond পাঠ ১২ itself, both the Principal's: it **generalises to any future excluded chapter without a new mechanism**, and **a gate is the only form of the rule that survives a session which never reads CD-127.** **(b) The proposed home was an extraction-header field. It cannot work, and the reason is the ruling.** **পাঠ ১২ has no extraction, so it has no header.** A header-only declaration is blind **exactly while the prohibition is doing all of its work** — from now until someone exercises CD-127(a). The exclusion has to be readable *before* any extraction exists. **(c) So no new home was invented; the existing one was made readable.** The gate scans **every file under `canon/`** for a declaration, which today resolves to **`canon/_wip/c5-bangla/EXCLUDED_paath_12.md` — the file `SOURCE_POLICY` §7.6 and CD-050(b) ALREADY name as where this exclusion is recorded.** The form is CD-124's HTML comment, already in use for `ledger-prefix`: `<!-- excluded-from-consumption: subject=BAN class=5 chapter=12 cd=CD-127 -->`. **(d) The extraction-header half is PROPOSED AND NOT BUILT, per the ruling's own instruction.** **§7.9 is the precedent that settles it**: a machine-read line in an extraction header (`**অবস্থা:** নির্মাণাধীন`) was established as a **`SOURCE_POLICY` §7 clause carried by a CD row** (CD-055) — **not a field a gate may invent.** So it is raised, not written; the loader already scans headers and will read it the day it exists. **(e) The gate fails CLOSED, and the widening runs on the declaration side only.** A declared `chapter=12` matches the raw qid segments `12`, `012`, `0012`. **This is the opposite of CD-088's failure and it is the safe direction**: CD-088 forbids collapsing two IDs *when deciding they are the same*; an exclusion gate is deciding whether to **refuse**, so over-matching costs a false refusal while under-matching **ships a forbidden question**. The widening is applied to the declaration — text we author — and **never to a captured id**; the parser uses `str.split`, not a regex capture, so `int_id_check.py` sees no tainted `zfill` and the distinction stays honest rather than waived. **(f) A malformed declaration FAILs; no declaration REPORTS.** *A prohibition nobody can resolve to a chapter is not a prohibition*, and a gate with nothing to enforce says so rather than passing silently (§7.17). **(g) The suite is 22, and CD-123's invariant is preserved by counting what CD-123 was counting.** `SOURCE-EXCLUSION` is **the first gate carrying neither policy's § row** — it executes a decision row, so its authority cell names a CD. The assert now checks **the eleven §6 rows** rather than the total: asserting on the total alone would have made the §6 count unverifiable the moment any gate arrived from anywhere else — **CD-083's recurring shape, a check written in a coarser unit than the thing it protects.** **Seven seeded cases, both directions**, synthetic declarations injected through ctx and never read from live `canon/` (CD-121(e)). | Principal ruling 2026-08-14 closing PENDING-P-033(6); CD-127(b); CD-050(b); CD-055; CD-088; CD-121(e); CD-123; CD-124; CD-129(b); SOURCE_POLICY §7.6, §7.9, §7.17; QUESTION_POLICY §9 |

| CD-133 | 2026-08-15 | **`QUESTION_POLICY` §10's last sentence is FALSE and is corrected: the Unification Decision Register was never filed (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-132`; no `CD-133` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row. Repo tip at verification: **`6ecbe3b`** — the handoff chain carried `4bc66d7` and was two commits stale, which is recorded here because the next handoff must say so.)* **(a) The false sentence, quoted so the correction is legible without opening the old version.** §10 closes: *"The Unification Decision Register (60 rows) is the provenance record and is filed alongside."* **It is not filed alongside. It is not filed anywhere.** **(b) The census, run repo-wide and stated in full because a claim of absence is only as good as the search that produced it.** Every `UD-` token outside `.git/`: **`UD-60` ×15 · `UD-23` ×11 · `UD-11` ×2 · `UD-09` ×2. Four numbers out of sixty.** **No file defines a single row.** `UD-01` returns **zero hits**, and `find` for `*UNIFICATION*`, `*REGISTER*` and `*UD_*` returns **nothing**. **(c) RECORDED AS NOT-IN-REPO. The term is minted here and is not borrowed.** **`NOT-IN-REPO` = asserted as filed, not present on disk, and may survive outside the repo** — in the session that produced it, or in an export. **`LOST` = known to have existed here and now unrecoverable.** The register is **NOT-IN-REPO**. **CD-090(b) is cited for its PRINCIPLE and explicitly not for its labels**: its principle is that **where two absence-states are distinguishable in kind but not by the evidence available, they are recorded separately and never folded, because folding them asserts a fact nothing supports.** At source CD-090(b) applies that to **numbers in a sequence** and mints `UNATTESTED-INSIDE-RANGE` (never issued) against `RECONSTRUCTED-UNKNOWN` (issued, every citation lost). **The register is a different object — a whole file §10 asserts was filed, absent from disk — so neither of those labels fits, and reusing one would say something false about it.** `RECONSTRUCTED-UNKNOWN` in particular presumes the rows were issued and their citations lost; this register may still exist intact in an export. **The borrowing is recorded rather than smoothed: CD-090(b) supplies the reasoning, this row supplies the terms.** **A principle that transfers is not the same as a label that transfers**, and the drafted form of this ruling conflated them — **CD-121(c)'s pattern exactly, a phrase acquiring authority by citation rather than by ruling, caught this time before it landed.** **(d) NOT RECONSTRUCTED, and the reason is evidentiary rather than procedural.** **56 of the 60 rows are cited nowhere**, so there is nothing to reconstruct from; and for the four that are cited, **CD-090(c) already rules that cited-by is evidence a number was taken and never evidence of what it said.** A reconstructed body would be worse than an empty slot, because the next session would cite it. **(e) Where each cited UD's substance survives, so the citations stay resolvable in meaning even though the register is not resolvable in form.** **`UD-60(b)`** (SB-series `REF-1`/`REF-2` retired to P00 numbering by rename + HISTORICAL alias, historical citations untouched) — `canon/islamic-curation/README.md`, `canon/names/README.md`, `canon/refs/MANIFEST.md`, `canon/refs/SB_CITATION_BASELINE.md`, `canon/DECISIONS.md`, `SESSION_LOG.md`, and the gates `tools/audits/canon_check.py` and `tools/audits/int_id_check.py`. **`UD-23`** (the Bloom axis governs the pool, the domain axis governs the paper) — **CD-121**, `QUESTION_POLICY` §6, and `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`. **`UD-09`/`UD-11`** — restated in **CD-094** and `canon/refs/MANIFEST.md` note 4. **(f) §10's AUTHORITY claim is unaffected, and only the FILING claim was false.** §10 makes **the CD rows the authority and the file their readable form**; the register was the *provenance* record, not the authority. **Nothing that is in force depends on it** — every ruling it recorded was minted as a CD row, which is the whole point of §10's design and the reason this correction costs a sentence rather than a rebuild. **(g) Corrected rather than quietly dropped.** The sentence is replaced by a pointer to this row with the superseded wording quoted in place, per the file's forward-only rule. **A false sentence deleted leaves the next reader wondering what the register was; a false sentence corrected tells them.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §10 (v1.1) → v1.2; **CD-090(b) for its principle, NOT its labels**; CD-090(c); CD-094; CD-121(c); AGENTS.md §4, §8 |
| CD-134 | 2026-08-15 | **A chapter's own *কোন প্রশ্নে কাজে লাগবে* line is ADVISORY, not a slot cap — CD-122(b)'s ruling APPLIED, and this row says so rather than presenting it as new ground (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-133`, minted this session; no `CD-134` token existed outside this row.)* **(a) CD-122(b) already named this line and already ruled on it.** Quoted at source: *"plus each পাঠ's **কোন প্রশ্নে কাজে লাগবে** prose. **Inverting a best/alternative table into a per-chapter obligation is inventing the mapping**, which §4 forbids."* **The drafted form of this ruling cited only §3 row 15 and read as fresh ground.** It is not, and a row that re-derives a ruling already minted invites a later session to discover two authorities for one rule — so the citation is the correction and it comes first. **(b) RULED, restating CD-122(b) in the direction the bank needs.** CD-122(b) settled that the line **cannot be inverted into an obligation** — it does not *require* a slot. This row settles the other face: **it does not FORBID one either.** The line **does not narrow §4's coverage test**, which is defined against the spine, quoted at source: *"the Pool must supply every topic in the chapter, **every question-type its spine slots need**, and every difficulty band."* **A Pool may serve ANY spine slot the extracted content supports.** **(c) The recorded reason for an unserved slot is now constrained, and that is the executable half.** **Where a slot is not served, the reason recorded must be that the CONTENT does not support it — never that the chapter's own line did not name it.** The two reasons look identical in a header and are not: **content-absence is a fact about the book that no later wave can change; line-absence is a fact about a sentence describing the book's likely uses, and it caps nothing.** A bank that records the second is unfalsifiable — nobody can tell whether the material was looked for. **(d) The effect on `C5_BAN_U13_QuestionBank_v1.json`, with the release attributed per slot so a later reader can tell what each ruling bought.** `header.gaps[0]` listed **eight** slots as not served, on the reasoning *"পাঠ ১৩'s own 'কোন প্রশ্নে কাজে লাগবে' line names seven slots and these are not among them"* — **the cap reading, in the bank's own words.** **Released by this row alone — S04 শূন্যস্থান · S05 বহুনির্বাচনি · S11 প্রশ্ন তৈরি / বিরামচিহ্ন.** **Released by this row TOGETHER WITH CD-136 — S06 বিপরীত / সমার্থক শব্দ · S12 যুক্তবর্ণ ও শব্দ গঠন · S13 এক কথায় প্রকাশ**: this row makes them authorable *in principle*, and CD-136 supplies the key they need, because their answers are general Bangla language facts the chapter does not gloss. **Still OUT — S14 আবেদনপত্র · S15 রচনা**, on CD-136's boundary and not on this one. **The drafted form of this ruling released three and named only three; the count is six**, found by reading `MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md`'s slot names against CD-136's admitted key types rather than against the draft's own list. **(e) `gaps[0]` is rewritten to state a CONTENT reason per remaining slot**, and the released six leave it. **(f) NO §9 ROW. This is §4 applied, not superseded** — §4's coverage sentence is unchanged and this row says what it already meant. The §4 site gains the constraint at (c) in the same v1.2 bump. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; **CD-122(b) applied, not extended**; CD-111; CD-136; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §4, §3 row 15; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md` S04–S15; `banks/C5_BAN_U13_QuestionBank_v1.json` `header.gaps[0]`; AGENTS.md §4 |
| CD-135 | 2026-08-15 | **THE BLOOM CHECK AT POOL LEVEL IS A FLOOR, NOT A BAND — CD-122's reasoning applied to the axis it was not applied to (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-134`, minted this session; no `CD-135` token existed outside this row.)* **(a) The argument being carried across, quoted from CD-122(a) at source.** *"**a pool cannot fail a *ceiling*.** An author can always decline to use hard items, so however hard-heavy a pool is, a compliant paper stays constructible from it. **Absence is the only thing a pool can be guilty of**"* — and `QUESTION_POLICY` §4 records it *"at this length so the check is not later 'tightened' into a symmetric one that reddens correct pools."* **(b) It is axis-independent, and nothing in it was about difficulty.** A `Remember`-heavy pool still constructs a compliant paper, **because the teacher declines the surplus** — exactly as with hard items. **The upper bounds of REF-06 §3.6 therefore do not bind the pool.** That §4 wrote the argument out at length for one axis and left the other symmetric is an accident of the order the two were read in, not a distinction anyone ruled. **(c) RULED: at POOL level the Bloom check is REF-06 §3.6's LOWER BOUNDS ONLY. No upper bound FAILs a pool.** The **BAND — both bounds — continues to apply at PAPER level**, alongside the domain ratio. **(d) Where a level's floor is 0 — `Evaluate` and `Create` — nothing is required, and that is the point at which silence becomes the risk.** A level the chapter cannot supply is **stated in the bank header as a content fact**, not left silent: a 0% level passing a 0% floor is indistinguishable from a level nobody looked for, and CD-134(c) has just ruled that an unserved slot must record a content reason. Same failure, same remedy, one axis over. **(e) UD-23 stands unchanged: the Bloom axis still governs the pool.** What changes is **what "governs" means on that axis** — the same change CD-122 already made on the difficulty axis. **CD-121(a)'s axis assignment is untouched**; only its readability *as a band* is superseded. **(f) EXECUTABLE — `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`, the `REF06_C3_5` loop at 1091–1095.** The **`share > hi` half stops appending to `errs`**; the **`share < lo` half is unchanged**. **Per-level counts against floors are REPORTed on every run, not only on failure** — CD-122's own ⚠ is that a check written asymmetrically invites a later symmetric "fix", and a report that prints every run is what makes the asymmetry visible rather than inferable. **Three seeded cases, synthetic per QB-D-012**: a pool **above an upper bound → CLEAN where it previously FAILed** · a pool **below a floor → FAIL, unchanged** · a pool **exactly at a floor → CLEAN**. The first is the one that matters: it is the inverted-seed device CD-122(a) used for the hard ceiling, so the symmetric form cannot creep back unnoticed. **(g) RECORDED, because it is the shape that will recur: a percentage floor implicitly caps every other level.** `Understand` 25 + `Apply` 25 + `Analyze` 10 = **60**, so **`Remember` can never exceed 40% of a pool however large it grows.** The ceiling was never the only thing bounding the mix, and removing it does not make the pool unbounded — **it moves the binding constraint from the ceiling to the floors.** **(h) Where it bites first, computed against পাঠ ১৩'s projected 93 items** (`Remember` 30 = 32.3% · `Understand` 25 = 26.9% · `Apply` 32 = 34.4% · `Analyze` 4 = 4.3% · `Evaluate` 2 · `Create` 0): **every floor clears EXCEPT `Analyze`, which needs 10 items at that size and has 4.** **Wave 3 must author about six more `Analyze` items, and S08 বিস্তৃত উত্তর is where they come from.** **This is the ruling working, not failing** — the pool is now bounded by what the chapter can be asked to reason about, which is the real constraint, rather than by a percentage nobody had computed. The projection is the Principal's; **the actual counts are reported at wave 3's close, not assumed here.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; **CD-122(a)'s reasoning, carried across axes**; CD-121(a) (partially superseded — see §9); CD-113; UD-23; QB-D-012; REF-06 §3.6; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §4, §6 → v1.2; PENDING-P-036 |
| CD-136 | 2026-08-15 | **TEACHER-SUPPLIED LANGUAGE KEYS, raised from `QB-D-013` to canon — and the gate change the draft proposed is REFUSED, with the reason recorded at length so a later session does not build it (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-135`, minted this session; no `CD-136` token existed outside this row.)* **(a) Why it is raised at all.** `QB-D-013` ruled **four শব্দার্থ items in one bank**. Per **CD-121(d)** — *"workstream-local is not enough for a sentence canon states"* — the general form belongs here. **QB-D-013 is the instance; this row is the rule.** **(b) RULED.** Where the **STIMULUS resolves to the chapter extraction** and the **KEY is a general Bangla language fact** — **সমার্থক · বিপরীত · যুক্তবর্ণ বিভাজন · এক কথায় প্রকাশ · ভাষারীতি / পদ নির্ণয় / ক্রিয়ার কাল** — **a teacher-supplied key is acceptable.** The item **declares it in its own `model_note`**, so **the provenance travels with the item and not only with the bank header**: a header note is lost the moment an item is lifted into a paper, which is the only form in which items are ever used. **(c) THE BOUNDARY, and it is load-bearing.** This admits **LANGUAGE FACTS ABOUT CHAPTER MATERIAL**. **It does not admit new content, new facts, or prompts with no chapter anchor.** The test is the stimulus: *the word is in the chapter; its synonym is a fact about Bangla.* **S14 আবেদনপত্র and S15 রচনা stay OUT of chapter banks** — they **anchor to nothing in the chapter and carry no key at all**, so neither half of (b) is even in play. **(d) Their home, confirmed at source before this row cited it, and reported with its limit.** `workstreams/scholarship/MODEL_PAPERS_POLICY.md` **exists, at v1.1, and is the paper-level authoring policy** — §4 already carries the mechanism these slots need (*real questions from source extractions where an extraction exists, typed placeholders naming slot, marks and domain where none does, never invented content presented as real*). **REPORTED, not smoothed: it carries no S14/S15 clause today** — a grep for `আবেদনপত্র`, `রচনা`, `S14` and `S15` across that file returns nothing. **It is the right home and it is not yet furnished**, and saying so is the difference between citing a policy and assuming one. **(e) NO GATE CHANGE, and this is the half most likely to be "fixed" later.** **`SOURCE-TRACE` never read keys.** Both implementations — `g_source_trace` (§6 shape) and `g_qb_source_trace` (§5 shape) — **resolve only `source_index[qid]` against the extraction.** So *"the key need not resolve"* **is already true and needs no amendment.** **(f) The FAIL branch the draft proposed was wrong twice over, and both errors are recorded because each is a general shape.** It would have failed *"an item with a non-resolving key and no `model_note`"*. **First: it builds key-resolution the gate does not have, in order to enforce a LOOSENING** — a net increase in machinery to permit something. **Second: it would newly fire on legitimate items whose keys are the student's own work**, of which this bank already holds several. **An undeclared teacher key is not machine-detectable without that capability, and the capability costs more than the defect it would catch.** **(g) Enforcement is the §6 human review gate — the Principal as Subject Lead** (REF-09 §9). **`QB-D-013`'s own wording saw this first**, quoted from the bank header: *"**SOURCE-TRACE would be satisfied either way, which is precisely why the difference is written down rather than left to the gate.**"* **A rule enforced by a human is not an unenforced rule**, and writing a gate that cannot see the thing it judges is worse than naming the human who can. **(h) §9 gains a row.** `QUESTION_BANK_POLICY` §4's first bullet — *"**Content ONLY from the chapter's source extraction**"* — is **amended to STIMULUS scope**. | CD-137 | 2026-08-15 | **`SB_CITATION_BASELINE.md` IS RE-FROZEN to admit four HISTORICAL IMPORTS — and the agent's own new file was FIXED, not re-frozen, which is the distinction the row exists to carry (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-136`, minted this session; no `CD-137` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) What triggered it.** The handoff chain was moved into the repo at `handoffs/` (Principal ruling, same day). Five chain handoffs plus `SCD_UNIFICATION_SURVEY.md` were recovered from project knowledge and filed as read-only imports. **`REF-CITE` immediately failed four of them** — `HANDOFF_2026-08-09_unification.md` (4 citations), `HANDOFF_2026-08-10_scd-central-migration.md` (3), `HANDOFF_2026-08-15_question-policy-unification.md` (1), `SCD_UNIFICATION_SURVEY.md` (11) — for carrying retired `REF-1`/`REF-2` in bare prose. **(b) THE GATE WAS CORRECT TO FIRE, and this is recorded first so a later session reading "a red gate cleared by a CD row" does not learn the wrong lesson.** These files are old text whose citations **UD-60(b) ruled left as written**. They failed only because importing them made them **new files** to a check that **cannot distinguish imported history from new writing** — nothing in a file's bytes says how old it is. **A gate that GUESSED at a file's age would be worse than one that fires and asks**: it would silently exempt genuinely new text that happened to look historical, which is the precise failure `REF-CITE` was built to prevent. **The gate did not need fixing. It needed the one fact it cannot see, and a re-freeze is how that fact is supplied.** **(c) The re-freeze, per the baseline file's own procedure.** `SB_CITATION_BASELINE.md` states: *"Do not regenerate to clear a red gate… Re-freezing is a Principal decision and takes a CD row."* **This is that decision and this is that row.** Regenerated via the documented `python tools/audits/canon_check.py --write-sb-baseline`; the four imports enter at the counts they were imported with. **(d) THE AGENT'S OWN NEW FILE WAS FIXED, NOT RE-FROZEN — the distinction is the ruling.** `handoffs/HANDOFF_2026-08-15_wave3-and-floors.md`, written this session, also failed, for one bare `REF-2` in its §11 error list. **It was corrected to backticked form** — which `SB_CITATION_BASELINE.md` sanctions by applying SOURCE_POLICY §7.16 by analogy, *a gate that forbids naming the retired number makes the retirement unwriteable* — **and it is NOT in the re-frozen baseline.** **Putting new text into a history mechanism is laundering**: the baseline's whole function is to record what citations *were* at a moment, and a file written today has no such moment to record. **Five failures became four by fixing; four became zero by re-freezing. The two halves are not the same operation and the row refuses to blur them.** **(e) THE RECURRENCE CONDITION, STATED AS A RULE AND NOT LEFT AS AN INFERENCE.** **The exemption holds because these four are FROZEN IMPORTS THAT WILL NEVER BE EDITED.** `handoffs/README.md` declares them read-only, cited never continued (the CD-034 / CD-043(b) pattern). **If any of the four is ever edited, its citations become NEW TEXT and this exemption does not follow them** — the edited file must use P00 numbering or be backticked, exactly as (d) required of new writing. **The exemption attaches to the frozen-ness, not to the filename**, and a future session that edits one of these files and finds `REF-CITE` still quiet should treat that as a defect in the check rather than as permission. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; `canon/refs/SB_CITATION_BASELINE.md` (its own re-freeze procedure); UD-60(b); CD-034; CD-043(b); SOURCE_POLICY §7.16; AGENTS.md §5 |
| CD-138 | 2026-08-15 | **THE SLOT REGISTER — its schema ruled in three parts, and the first part was CORRECTED BEFORE ANYTHING WAS BUILT because its first draft made a marker string the gate's input (Principal ruling 2026-08-15, amended in the same session on evidence).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-137`; no `CD-138` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) WHAT THIS ROW IS THE TRIGGER FOR.** `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §4 names its own successor in its own words — *"When per-chapter spine slot-mapping exists as data, this replaces the header-stated target"* — and **CD-122(b) recorded the absence precisely so its arrival would be a known trigger**, quoted at source: *"The day a slot-mapping is committed as data, this gate changes; the gate's docstring is the notice."* **This row is that day.** The register is **`canon/marklogic/SLOT_REGISTER.json`**, built per (subject, class, slot) from the spines, carrying a `canon/MANIFEST.md` row. **(b) TASK MODE IS A DECLARED FIELD.** `task_mode ∈ {alternative, composite, simple}` per (subject, class, slot), with `admitted_set[]` and `selected` on alternative rows, `parts[]` on composite rows, `admitted_task` holding the spine's কারণ cell **verbatim**, plus `items_per_paper`, `marks`, `marks_per_item`, `d_code`, `authority` and `row_constraints[]`. **Declaration is authored from the spine's header and কারণ column and countersigned at the §6 human review pass. THE GATE READS THE DECLARED FIELD ONLY.** **No gate may derive mode, admitted-set membership, or set cardinality from the presence, absence or wording of a header marker** — *যেকোনো একটা*, *অথবা*, *বা*, *ও*, *+* and *ভেঙে* are **evidence for the declaration, not inputs to the check**. **Seeded:** a row whose header carries a marker while its declaration says composite must pass unchanged, and **a gate whose verdict moves when a marker string is edited is non-conformant**. **(c) WHY (b) READS THIS WAY — the drafted form did the opposite and the spines falsify it.** The draft made *"যেকোনো একটা"*'s presence the test for an alternative row and its absence the test for a composite one. Read at source in the same session: **`ENG-S07`** and **`ENG-S11`** carry the marker, but **`ENG-S01`** (*"৫টি শব্দের অর্থ মেলানো, **অথবা** সত্য-মিথ্যা"*), **`ENG-S06`** (*"দাগানো শব্দের Parts of Speech, **অথবা** নির্দেশমতো কাল পরিবর্তন"*, whose own body calls them *"দুটো রূপ"*) and **`ENG-S13`** (*"চিঠি, আবেদনপত্র **বা** ইমেইল"*) state alternatives and **carry no marker at all** — the drafted rule would have encoded all three as COMPOSITE, requiring a single item to do both halves. In the other direction, **`MATH-S01`'s class rows carry *"যেকোনো C4 অধ্যায়"*** in the কোথা-থেকে column, and `MarkLogic_MATH_Spine.md`'s own approval checklist **forbids** *"কোনো 'যেকোনো ৫টির উত্তর দাও' নেই — সব প্রশ্ন আবশ্যিক"* under **I-3**: the same token, in the same corpus, meaning a source-range and then meaning a thing that must never appear. **And the join is not only ও** — `BAN-S03` C1 joins with **+** (*"৫টি × ২ — বাক্য + সঠিক বিরামচিহ্ন"*) and `BAN-S12` joins with **ভেঙে**. **This is `QB-CR-011`'s shape — a surface read instead of the thing underneath — and it is that pattern's third instance.** **(d) DEMAND IS PAPER-LEVEL. No per-chapter divisor exists and none is used.** The register records per-slot demand as the paper's own numbers, and **`items_per_paper` and `marks` are separate fields because they are separate facts**: at BAN C5 the fifteen slots demand **56 items** and **100 marks**. **Only the marks column totals 100** (I-1); a self-check summing items to 100 is wrong, and the chain handoff's own demand line invites exactly that reading. **(e) CHAPTER OBLIGATION IS ADMISSIBILITY-GATED.** A chapter declares its admissible slots **in its own bank header**, with a **one-line content reason for each excluded slot**, countersigned at the §6 pass. **The gate never infers admissibility from content** — that inference is `QB-CR-011`'s shape and is forbidden here by name. It checks floors **over the declared set only**, and **FAILs on any item in a slot the chapter declared inadmissible**. Seeded both directions. **(f) `chapter_authorable` IS DERIVED, NEVER AUTHORED (Principal, in session).** It is computed from (e)'s per-chapter declarations and carries **no authored cell in the register's slot rows**. An authored slot-level copy would be **a second source of truth that no gate compares against the first** — and it is exactly the field CD-139 had to unpick one row later, on a book that falsified the generalisation it was carrying. **(g) An admissible chapter owes the paper's FULL per-slot demand, capped by `PENDING-P-036`'s `min()`.** No share-based reduction exists until MarkLogic §৪'s syllabus split is read and `PENDING-P-038` is ruled. **(h) COVERAGE is converted to read the register** — **admitted task, not slot-id presence** — which closes §4's successor clause and CD-122(b)'s recorded absence together. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15 (amended in session); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §4; CD-122(b); CD-134; CD-135; CD-136; CD-139; `QB-CR-011`; `PENDING-P-036`; `PENDING-P-038`; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md`; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_ENG_Spine.md`; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_MATH_Spine.md`; AGENTS.md §5, §6 |
| CD-139 | 2026-08-15 | **CD-136(c)'s BAR ON S14/S15 IN CHAPTER BANKS IS SCOPE-AMENDED — it was ruled on পাঠ ১৩'s evidence and generalised past it, and the book falsifies the generalisation at পাঠ ৪ (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-138`, minted this session; no `CD-139` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) What CD-136(c) says, quoted so the amendment is legible against it.** *"**S14 আবেদনপত্র and S15 রচনা stay OUT of chapter banks** — they **anchor to nothing in the chapter and carry no key at all**, so neither half of (b) is even in play."* **The reason is a claim about CONTENT**, and content is a per-chapter fact. **(b) The claim is true over the range it was ruled on and false one book-section away.** `canon/marklogic/C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md` records S14 as *"কোনো পাঠে সরাসরি নেই — বাইরের বিষয়"* — **and that table covers পাঠ ১৩–২৩ only.** পাঠ ৪ is outside it, and `canon/sources/c5/bangla/C5_BAN_Source_04.md` records the opposite in its own slot table: **`BAN-S14` — *"সরাসরি বাঁধা — এই বইয়ের একমাত্র পাঠ যেখানে S14 বাঁধা।"*** — নমুনা আবেদনপত্র (ছাপা ২৪), সাত-অংশের ছক (২৫), অনুশীলনী ২ ও ৪ — **with evidence crops already on disk** (`evidence/C5_BAN_P04_p024_boundary.png`, `evidence/C5_BAN_P04_p024_letter_hi.png`). **(c) AMENDED.** **S14/S15 are excluded from a chapter bank where the chapter's content does not anchor them — governed per-chapter by CD-138(e)'s declaration, not by a slot-level rule.** **(d) At C5 Bangla, as declared:** **পাঠ ৪ ADMITS S14** on the content at (b); **all other C5 chapters exclude it**, reason *no anchoring content*. **S15 remains excluded at every C5 chapter surveyed** — no chapter anchors it — **and this is a DECLARATION, revisable on evidence, not a slot-level rule.** **(e) CD-134(d) reads through unchanged.** Its *"Still OUT — S14 আবেদনপত্র · S15 রচনা, on CD-136's boundary and not on this one"* was correct about পাঠ ১৩ and stays correct about পাঠ ১৩; it made no claim about পাঠ ৪. **(f) `PENDING-P-038` gains its live case before it is ruled: S14 at C5 Bangla is admitted by EXACTLY ONE chapter.** The single-point-of-failure report that row proposes is no longer hypothetical — retire or exclude পাঠ ৪ and the slot has no chapter at all. **(g) The general shape, recorded because it is the transferable part.** CD-136(c) generalised from the chapters in front of it to a claim about every chapter, and **the range it was ruled on was never stated in the row** — which is what made the over-reach invisible to a later reader. **A content claim carries the range it was verified over, or it is a declaration wearing a rule's clothes.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; **CD-136(c) scope-amended, its text unedited**; CD-138(e); CD-134(d); `canon/sources/c5/bangla/C5_BAN_Source_04.md`; `canon/marklogic/C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md`; `PENDING-P-038` |
| CD-140 | 2026-08-15 | **THE TWO ITEM-SPLITS THE BAN SPINE NEVER STATED ARE RULED, AND FILED AS A ROW RATHER THAN LEFT `UNRESOLVED` IN THE REGISTER (Principal ruling 2026-08-15, authority স্কুল কর্তৃপক্ষ).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-139`; no `CD-140` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) WHAT WAS MISSING, and it was found by trying to write the data, not by reading the file.** `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md` gives every C1–C4 cell a mark total, and gives most of them an explicit `×n` split in the same cell — *৬টি শব্দ × ২*, *১০টি প্রশ্ন × ১*, *৪টি যুক্তবর্ণ × ২টি করে শব্দ*. **Exactly two cells give the total and no split:** `BAN-S12` at **C2 (10 marks)** — *যুক্তবর্ণ ভেঙে শব্দ, "ভেঙে লিখি", দ্বিতীয় শ্রেণির সবচেয়ে বড় ধাপ* — and `BAN-S09` at **C3 (10 marks)** — *কবিতাংশ পড়ে প্রশ্নের উত্তর বসানো হয়েছে*. **(b) WHY THE REGISTER COULD NOT SIMPLY INHERIT C5's.** Both are deviations, not মূল কাঠামো rows: C2 S12 is **D1 ↑** and C3 S09 is **D4 ↑**, and each is at double the C5 total. A মূল কাঠামো row may take C5's split because D0 means *হুবহু এক*; a row that has already departed from the base in marks has no such claim on the base's item count. **(c) WHY IT COULD NOT BE DERIVED.** CD-138(d): *no per-chapter divisor exists and none is used*. `10 = 10×1` and `10 = 5×2` are both arithmetically available and the register has no field that would record which one was a guess. **An invented divisor is indistinguishable from a read one the moment it is written down** — which is the whole reason CD-138(d) exists. **(d) RULED.** **`BAN-S12` C2 = ১০টি × ১ নম্বর.** **`BAN-S09` C3 = ৫টি প্রশ্ন × ২ নম্বর.** **(e) THE FORM MATTERS AS MUCH AS THE NUMBERS, and this is the transferable half.** The drafting session proposed carrying these as an `UNRESOLVED` marker inside the register rows. **Refused.** A register is the thing gates read; a row carrying `UNRESOLVED` is either ignored by the checker — in which case the gap is invisible — or it fails every run, in which case the column can never go green and the marker becomes something a later session deletes to make the suite quiet. **A question the Principal has to answer belongs in a decision row where it is answered, not in a data file where it is merely flagged.** The register carries the two values and cites this row; `slot_register_check.py` proves them like any other. **(f) AUTHORITY.** স্কুল কর্তৃপক্ষ, the same authority the spine's own C4 `BAN-S01` exception carries — this is a school decision about its own paper, not a reading of নেপ, and the spine is amended by this row's citation rather than edited (§7 forward-only). **I-9 is satisfied**: the code says *what kind* of change, this row says *why the number is what it is*. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15, authority স্কুল কর্তৃপক্ষ; CD-138(d); `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md` `BAN-S12` C2, `BAN-S09` C3; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_Rules.md` I-6, I-9; `canon/marklogic/SLOT_REGISTER.json`; `tools/audits/slot_register_check.py`; AGENTS.md §4, §7 |
| CD-141 | 2026-08-15 | **THE TEACHER-LANE STANDING AUTHORIZATION — an agent may push without per-commit approval inside a boundary drawn TWICE, and the row records what the autonomy rests on rather than only what it permits (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-140`; no `CD-141` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) SCOPE BY COMMIT CLASS.** Pre-approved: **build · corrections · log · promotion**. Not pre-approved, ever, in this lane: **ruling · gate · tools · canon**, or anything touching `AGENTS.md`, `canon/`, `tools/` or a policy file. **(b) SCOPE BY PATH, AND BOTH MUST HOLD.** Files under `workstreams/question-banks/` and `workstreams/question-banks/banks/envelopes/` only. **A commit whose CLASS is pre-approved but whose PATH is not — or the reverse — is NOT authorized and the agent stops.** The boundary is stated twice on purpose: a promotion-class commit reaching into `canon/`, and a canon-class commit reaching into `banks/`, are both plausible agent errors, and **either single test lets one of them through**. **(c) THE GATE CONDITION IS THE WHOLE SUITE, CLEAN, INCLUDING `PLAN` AND `ENVELOPE-SYNC`.** Not *the gates that apply*, not *CLEAN apart from*. **A gate reporting `N/A` is NOT CLEAN for this purpose** — `N/A` means the gate did not judge, and an unjudged bank is exactly what this row must not release. Eight gates report `N/A` on every qp6 bank because they read the other shape; the clause means **no gate that SHOULD judge returned `N/A`**, so if a future bank shape silently makes `PLAN` or `ENVELOPE-SYNC` inapplicable, **the authorization lapses rather than passing quietly**. Verbatim output is pasted in the session report as always (AGENTS §5). **(d) `PLAN` IS THE COUNTERSIGN, and that is the substantive trade.** The plan table the Principal signed before authoring is replaced by the gate (CD-142(b)). **The Principal stops signing arithmetic and keeps signing judgement.** **(e) ANYTHING ELSE → STOP AND REPORT. NEVER ASK THE TEACHER.** The teacher is a zero-Git operator (AGENTS §2) and cannot rule. **A question put to someone who cannot answer it is not a question, it is a way of proceeding without one** — and `QB-CR-013` is the recorded instance of an agent reading an answer about MECHANICS as an answer about SUBSTANCE. That row is why this clause is imperative. **(f) NEVER PRE-APPROVED, listed because these are what a session will be tempted by:** a new gate or a change to one · a policy edit · a decision row · an `_inbox/` classification · anything on the mounted drive · **a `git reset` or any history rewrite, pushed or not** · promotion to `gold`, which is a Hub act and not a Git state (CD-003). **(g) THE 40-ITEM MINIMUM IS A STATED CONSEQUENCE OF THE MARGIN RULE, ruled here rather than discovered by a session.** `PLAN` requires every positive REF-06 §3.6 floor to clear by >= 2 items, which needs `0.80n + 8 <= n`, i.e. **n >= 40. No bank under 40 items can pass `PLAN`, however it is tagged — arithmetic, not tagging.** পাঠ ১৩ at 110 is far clear. **A thin chapter GROWS or COMBINES, and which one is the Principal's ruling PER CASE** — the agent stops and reports rather than padding a bank to clear a floor, because **items authored to satisfy a gate are exactly the near-duplicates §4 forbids**. **(h) WHAT THIS AUTONOMY RESTS ON, recorded because it is thin and the Principal files it knowingly.** AGENTS §6 promotes a pattern to a gate at **three** instances; the ledger's only entry about agent judgement is **`QB-CR-013`, a single instance**. This row expands autonomy on that one instance. **It is filed deliberately, not incidentally**, and **a DRY RUN is required before handover**: one full teacher-lane session run end to end under this row with the Principal watching, before anyone relies on it unattended. **(i) REVOCATION IS UNILATERAL AND NEEDS NO ROW.** The Principal withdraws this by saying so. **The agent does not get to argue the conditions were met.** **(j) AUTONOMY REMOVES THE WAIT, NOT THE ACCOUNT.** Every push under this row still reports a per-commit range check with the class stated, and the post-push verification of AGENTS §3.1 including `git ls-remote` asked of the server. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; drafted at `workstreams/question-banks/_wip/RULING_DRAFTS_teacher-lane_2026-08-15.md` and reviewed before filing; **AGENTS.md §3.1 amended to point here**; CD-079 (ruling-only carve-out, unchanged); CD-083(b), CD-083(d); CD-003; `QB-CR-013`; CD-142; `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §6 Plan row |
| CD-142 | 2026-08-15 | **§6's HUMAN REVIEW SPLITS IN THREE AND EACH HALF GOES WHERE IT CAN ACTUALLY BE DONE — the venue moves, the standard does not (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: no `CD-142` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) ITEM-LEVEL CONTENT REVIEW → HUB SUBJECT EXPERTS.** Whether a question is good, whether a teacher-supplied key is right, whether a stem suits the class. **This never belonged to the Principal alone and does not scale to him.** CD-136(g) named him because the Hub review lane did not exist yet — **not because the judgement is his by nature**, and reading it that way would freeze a stopgap into a principle. **(b) PLAN-LEVEL COUNTERSIGN → THE `PLAN` GATE.** Bloom margins with the >= 2 rule, full per-slot demand (CD-138(g)), task declarations against the register (CD-138(b)), P-037's type rule, and the within-slot near-duplicate stem scan. **All of it is arithmetic over the register and the bank**, which is why a gate can hold it — and why a human holding it was **spending judgement on counting**. **(c) THE PRINCIPAL RETAINS, and the shorter list is the point:** rulings and decision rows · gate and tooling changes · canon and policy · debugging when a gate is wrong · **promotion `reviewed → gold`, which stays a HUB act and is not a Git state (CD-003)** · revocation of any authorization. **(d) THE VENUE MOVES, THE STANDARD DOES NOT.** CD-136(b)'s teacher-key rule is unchanged; what changes is who reads it. **A rule enforced by a different human is not a weakened rule** — and CD-136(e) already settled that a human is the right enforcer here, *because a gate cannot see the thing it judges*. **(e) THE BORDERLINE BAND IS THE HANDOFF AND IT IS DELIBERATE.** `PLAN` REPORTS stem pairs at 85–95% similarity rather than failing them, **precisely so a subject expert sees the per-word drills and rules on them**. The gate hands over what it cannot decide instead of guessing or going quiet — SOURCE_POLICY §7.17 in a new place. **(f) THE HUB READ IS NOT A PRECONDITION FOR A PUSH under CD-141.** Banks reach the Hub as `draft`; the expert read happens there. **Coupling the two would make every push wait on a human in a different system, which is the wait CD-141 exists to remove.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; drafted at `_wip/RULING_DRAFTS_teacher-lane_2026-08-15.md`; **AGENTS.md §6 amended to point here**; CD-136(b), CD-136(e), CD-136(g) — **venue amended, standard untouched**; CD-138(b), CD-138(g); CD-135; CD-003; REF-09 §9; CD-141 |
| CD-143 | 2026-08-15 | **IMPORT CONTRACT v1.1 IS VENDORED IN WHOLE — `question_batch`, upstream `D-#495` — and the row is minted HERE rather than in the upstream's series (Principal ruling 2026-08-15).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-142`; no `CD-143` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) WHAT WAS SUPERSEDED, AND HOW.** All three files scd-hub commit `7ad4903` touched — `import-contract.md`, `import-contract.schema.json` (**the source of truth**), `validate_import.py` — **replaced in whole, never patched**, per `tools/hub-export/VENDOR.md` and AGENTS §7. **The wrapper this repo now exports was written against the schema in the delivered file, not against the session brief's description of it** — the brief was explicit on that point and the reason is at (c). **(b) THE SERIES QUESTION, ruled because the brief said D-PROJ04 and this row is not one.** `D-PROJ04-###` is the **UPSTREAM's** series: this repo CITES it (`D-PROJ04-005` locked v1.0) and has never minted in it. **Minting `D-PROJ04-018` here would collide with whatever `scd-hub` mints next**, and AGENTS §4's rule that the agent assigns the next free number itself only works inside a series this repo owns. So the repo-side row is a **`CD-###` citing the upstream ruling** — `D-#495` — which is exactly how `D-PROJ04-005` is treated at VENDOR.md. **(c) TWO THINGS THE FILE SAYS THAT A PROSE READING WOULD HAVE GOT WRONG, recorded because they are the argument for supersede-only.** **`envelope_version` STAYS `"1.0"`** — the DOCUMENT is v1.1, the WIRE VALUE is not, because `question_batch` is additive, the same rule that kept question and stimulus at 1.0. An export stamping `"1.1"` fails the const. And **the wrapper carries almost nothing**: the batch branch sets `subject`, `class_level`, `address`, `curation_tag`, `pinned_to`, `provenance`, `tags`, `review_status`, `rendered_markdown` and `payload` to **false**, root `required` drops to `envelope_version` + `doc_type`, and root `additionalProperties` is **false** — so the wrapper is **exactly four keys** and anything helpfully added to it is a validation failure. **(d) `digest` IS OPTIONAL AND IS NOT AN INTEGRITY CHECK.** The schema's own words: *recorded on the batch audit row; **not recomputed at import***. This repo supplies it anyway, and it is `ENVELOPE-SYNC`'s digest, so **signature · export · import all carry one number** — but the number is an **audit trail across the boundary, not a guarantee the Hub enforces**, and a later session must not read it as one. **(e) `items` IS THE LOOSEST GATE IN THE CONTRACT, ON PURPOSE.** Its only structural bar is **nesting**. Everything else about an element is left to the per-element pass **so that ONE bad element fails alone and the rest still import** — which is why the repo side needed **no new validation logic**: the single path is still the contract, and each envelope here is already independently harness-clean. **(f) WHOLE-BATCH REJECTS ARE EXACTLY THREE, plus nesting**: `items` absent or empty · `item_count` != `items.length` · more than 500 items. The harness's new **L1b** layer holds the last two. **(g) NO REGRESSION, MEASURED.** The v1.1 harness was run against the 110 existing single envelopes before the supersede was committed: **110 × PASS (0 warn, 0 advisory), regressions none.** A contract bump that quietly moved the single path would have surfaced there. | Principal ruling 2026-08-15; **upstream `D-#495`, scd-hub commit `7ad4903`**; supersedes contract **LOCKED v1.0** (`D-PROJ04-005`, 2026-06-09); `tools/hub-export/VENDOR.md`; CD-003; AGENTS §7, §11; `ENVELOPE-SYNC`; `workstreams/question-banks/authoring/build_batch.py` |
| CD-144 | 2026-08-16 | **`CD-141(b)`'s PATH BOUNDARY GAINS TWO NAMED `log`-CLASS EXCEPTIONS — the row as filed could not log its own session, and the draft it was reviewed from shows it was never meant to (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-143`; no `CD-144` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) THE DEFECT, stated as the dry run met it rather than in the abstract.** `CD-141(a)` pre-approves the **`log`** class. `CD-141(b)` confines every authorized commit to `workstreams/question-banks/` and its `banks/envelopes/`, and is imperative about the mixed case: *"A commit whose CLASS is pre-approved but whose PATH is not — or the reverse — is NOT authorized and the agent stops."* **Root `SESSION_LOG.md` is `log` class at a root path.** The row therefore pre-approves a class whose canonical file it then places out of reach, and **AGENTS §3's End clause — which requires a root `SESSION_LOG.md` block at every session close — becomes unreachable from inside the lane it governs.** Not one session under CD-141 could have closed properly. **(b) THIS WAS A DROPPED PARENTHESIS, NOT A CHOSEN NARROWING, AND THE DRAFT IS THE EVIDENCE.** `workstreams/question-banks/_wip/RULING_DRAFTS_teacher-lane_2026-08-15.md` line 18 names the class members inline — **`log` (STATE.md, SESSION_LOG.md)** — and the filed `CD-141(a)` carries the class list **without the parenthesis**. **Both readings survive in the row as filed**, which is why the পাঠ ১৪ session stopped and handed its block over instead of choosing one. **A row that can be read two ways is not resolved by the reader**, and an agent that picks the convenient reading has made a ruling. **(c) AMENDED.** **Two files are named PATH EXCEPTIONS to `CD-141(b)`, for `log`-class commits ONLY:** root **`SESSION_LOG.md`**, and **`workstreams/question-banks/_wip/STATE.md`** — the second already inside the boundary, named here anyway so the `log` class's whole surface is stated in one place and neither half is left to inference. **No other root file. No other class.** A `build`, `corrections` or `promotion` commit touching `SESSION_LOG.md` is **NOT** authorized: the exception is class-scoped and CD-141(b)'s two tests still both have to hold. **(d) WHY THE EXCEPTION IS TWO FILENAMES AND NOT A RELAXED RULE.** The tempting form is *"root files the log class needs"* — and then every session decides what it needs, which is the boundary dissolving one convenience at a time. **Two filenames cannot be argued with**, and a third one arriving is a visible amendment rather than a quiet reading. This is CD-141(b)'s own device — *two independent statements of the same boundary* — kept rather than loosened. **(e) `CD-141`'s TEXT IS UNEDITED.** AGENTS §4: append-only, a reversal is a new row citing the old, never an edit. This row is that citation, and a pointer is added to this file's forward-only amendments section so a reader arriving at `CD-141(b)` from elsewhere is not left with the unamended form. **(f) WHAT THIS DOES NOT TOUCH.** `CD-141(c)`'s gate condition including `N/A`-is-not-CLEAN, `(g)`'s 40-item minimum, `(h)`'s dry-run requirement, `(i)`'s unilateral revocation, and `(f)`'s never-pre-approved list — **all unchanged**. In particular **a decision row is still never pre-approved**, so this amendment is not itself a teacher-lane commit and was not pushed as one. **(g) THE DRY RUN IS WHAT FOUND IT, AND THAT IS THE TRANSFERABLE PART.** `CD-141(h)` required one attended run end to end before handover, filed knowingly on a single ledger instance. That run produced a CLEAN 84-item পাঠ ১৪ bank **and this defect**, and **the defect was only visible from inside**: the clause reads correctly right up until a session tries to close under it. **A rule is tested by being used, not by being read** — which is the argument for (h) having been required at all. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; **`CD-141(b)` amended, its text unedited**; `CD-141(a)`, `CD-141(f)`; drafted at `workstreams/question-banks/_wip/RULING_DRAFTS_teacher-lane_2026-08-15.md` line 18; AGENTS.md §3 End clause, §3.1 (pointer updated), §4; CD-079; `QB-CR-014` |
| CD-145 | 2026-08-16 | **`CD-141(c)`'s ILLUSTRATIVE COUNT IS WRONG — TEN GATES REPORT `N/A` ON A qp6 BANK, NOT EIGHT — and it was wrong when written rather than having drifted (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-144`, minted this session; no `CD-145` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) THE NUMBER, MEASURED BOTH WAYS.** `CD-141(c)` reads *"Eight gates report `N/A` on every qp6 bank because they read the other shape."* A live run prints **TEN**: `POOL-MEMBERSHIP` · `ZERO-OVERLAP` · `ANSWER-SHAPE` · `RUBRIC-SPECIFICITY` · `FLAG-TRACE` · `QUOTE-VERBATIM` · `HONORIFIC` · `AS-MIX` · `NUMERALS` · `CEILING`. **Parsing the `GATES` table at `7cc4458` — the commit CD-141 was filed in — gives the same ten.** So this is **not drift and no gate has been added or removed**; the row's own figure never matched the suite it describes. The draft carries it too (`_wip/RULING_DRAFTS_teacher-lane_2026-08-15.md` line 98), which is where it came from. **(b) THE OPERATIVE TEST IS UNCHANGED AND WAS NEVER IN DOUBT.** `CD-141(c)`'s rule is *"no gate that SHOULD judge returned `N/A`"*, and that clause is exact, self-contained and does not depend on the count. **Every teacher-lane verdict given under CD-141 stands** — পাঠ ১৪ included, where `PLAN` and `ENVELOPE-SYNC` both judged and both passed. **Nothing is re-opened by this row.** **(c) SO WHY RULE ON IT AT ALL — because the count is what a session actually checks against.** A number in a decision row is read as a expected value. A session seeing ten where the row promised eight either raises two false alarms, or — far worse and far likelier — concludes the row's figure is approximate and **stops counting**. **The slack an illustrative number creates is exactly the space a real `N/A` hides in**, and a real one is the thing (c) exists to catch. **A figure that cannot be relied on is worse than no figure**, because no figure would have made the reader count. **(d) RULED: the count is TEN**, and `CD-141(c)`'s operative clause is otherwise untouched. `CD-141`'s text is **unedited** (AGENTS §4), and neither the draft nor the filed row is retro-corrected — a forward-only pointer carries the number. **(e) HOW IT SURFACED, and it is the transferable half.** The agent's 2026-08-16 session report asserted *"8 `N/A`, all of them the qp6-shape gates CD-141(c) names"* — **taken from the row instead of counted from the output that was on screen at the time.** The error propagated exactly one hop before being caught on a later re-read. **This is `QB-CR-012`'s pattern — reading a surface instead of the thing underneath — in its fifth instance, and the surface here is a decision row's own illustrative figure.** The pattern's behavioural residue already covers it: *do not cite a §-number, a CD row or a policy scope you have not read at source in this conversation* — **and this row extends the residue by one word: a COUNT is not read at source, it is COUNTED.** **(f) THE STANDING FORM, which is the executable part and the reason this is a row rather than a note.** **An illustrative count inside a ruling is verified by running the thing it counts, in the session that writes it, or it is not written.** A rule's prose can be reasoned about; its arithmetic cannot, and CD-141 now carries two defects — this one and the dropped parenthesis at `CD-144` — **that both entered through the same draft and neither of which reading the draft again would have caught.** **No gate is proposed:** a gate cannot know which figure in a sentence was meant to be a measurement. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; `QB-CR-012` (pattern, fifth instance); CD-144; AGENTS.md §4, §5 |

| CD-147 | 2026-08-16 | **S14 আবেদনপত্র AND S15 রচনা ARE PAPER-LEVEL FOR EVERY CHAPTER, CATEGORICALLY — CD-139's per-chapter reading is REVERSED, পাঠ ৪ INCLUDED AND NAMED (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-146`; no `CD-147` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) RULED.** **আবেদনপত্র / ফরম / ছক (S14) and রচনা (S15) never enter a chapter bank, whatever the chapter's content anchors.** They are authored **only in the paper/exam pipeline**.
