deleting the rows would leave the paper lane unable to see its own slots, and `chapter_authorable` (CD-138(f), derived never authored) now derives to false for them at every chapter without any chapter having said so. **(f) EXECUTABLE — `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`, constant `PAPER_LEVEL_SLOTS`.** **COVERAGE**: an item in S14/S15 **FAILs categorically**, reported as a DIFFERENT error from CD-138(e)'s *this chapter declared it inadmissible*, because the remedies differ — one re-declares, the other moves the item to the paper pipeline; a header **ADMITTING** S14/S15 FAILs even with no item in it; the completeness test **exempts** them. **PLAN** refuses a plan admitting them **in its own voice**, since PLAN asks whether a bank is SIGNABLE and a bank is offered as finished on its verdict. **Four seeds, and the two negatives are the half that matters**: a bank declaring NOTHING about S14/S15 stays CLEAN (the obligation this row REMOVED — a gate that only gained the FAIL would redden every conformant bank in the repo on its first run), and a bank still carrying a CD-139-era content reason is **accepted, not failed**, because correct history is not a defect (AGENTS §4). **AGENTS §5.1 checked and recorded: the gate reads structured fields (`slot_index`, `admissible_slots`), never prose, so naming `S14` in a ledger row or in this decision stays writeable.** **(g) THE পাঠ ১৮ QUESTION RAISED BY THE 2026-08-16 PROBE IS CLOSED BY THIS RULE.** The probe reported that `CD-139(d)`'s standing exclusion reason — *রচনার কোনো সূত্র, শিরোনাম বা নির্দেশক এই পাঠে নেই* — **fails at পাঠ ১৮**, whose অনুশীলনী ৫ carries a titled essay prompt (*"আমি যা হতে চাই"*) and which the extraction's own ছক names as an S15 source alongside পাঠ ২৩. **The report was correct and the conclusion it invited is refused: পাঠ ১৮ does not admit S15 either.** The anchor exists; the pipeline is still the paper's. **(h) `PENDING-P-038`'s LIVE CASE DISSOLVES RATHER THAN BEING SOLVED.** CD-139(f) made S14 at C5 Bangla a single-point-of-failure at exactly one admitting chapter. **It is now zero-admitted BY RULE, which is not the same defect and is not a defect at all** — the row's general question (nothing checks that a slot is admitted by ANY chapter) is unaffected and stays RAISED. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; | CD-148 | 2026-08-16 | **পাঠ ২১'s BANK IS DISCARDED, NOT MIGRATED — the 57-item old-shape bank is retired WHOLE and U21 is regenerated from scratch through the current pipeline (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-147`, minted this session; no `CD-148` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row.)* **(a) WHAT IS WRONG WITH IT, measured rather than asserted.** `C5_BAN_U21_QuestionBank_v1.json` is the older `qb` shape, so **`PLAN`, `COVERAGE`, `BLOOM-BAND` and `ENVELOPE-SYNC` all return `N/A` on it** — the four gates that decide whether a bank is signable do not judge it at all. Its 57 items carry `bloom_level` **Remember 23 · Understand 17 · Apply 14 · Evaluate 2 · Create 1 · Analyze 0**. **Analyze is ZERO.** At 57 items PLAN's 10% floor needs 6 and the margin rule needs **8**. **Size was never the problem** — 57 clears CD-141(g)'s n>=40 arithmetic with room; the tagging and the authoring are. **(b) RULED: DISCARDED, NOT MIGRATED.** A shape migration would carry 57 items authored before CD-134, CD-135, CD-136, CD-138 and CD-142 into a register the gates now read, **and every one of those rulings changes what a correct item looks like** — the admitted-task declaration, the Bloom floors, the teacher-key `model_note`, the per-chapter admissibility header. **A migration would produce a bank that passes because it was reshaped to, not because it was authored right**, and nothing downstream could tell the two apart. **(c) RETIRED WHOLE, ARCHIVED, NEVER DELETED** — supersede-with-archive (AGENTS §7, master §5.3). The bank, its authoring script and its four gate reports move to `archive/` under a dated path with a `README.md` naming this row. **The wave script is archived, not edited** (`QB-CR-014(g)`'s standing reason: editing it would make the record say the wave got it right). **(d) THE ENVELOPES ARE QUARANTINED, AND THAT IS THE HALF WITH A CONSUMER.** 57 `single/` envelopes plus the array are removed from `banks/envelopes/` and archived with the bank, **so `ENVELOPE-SYNC` cannot resolve them and no export path can serve a retired item.** This is `ENVELOPE-SYNC`'s own recorded lesson applied before it fires: *a stale ADDITION is loud, a stale SURVIVAL is silent, and the silent one is the one that reaches the Hub.* **(e) HUB-SIDE CLEANUP, IF ANY, IS THE PRINCIPAL'S — and the repo cannot know whether it is owed.** Items reach the Hub only by an upload the Principal performs (CD-003; promotion `reviewed → gold` is a Hub act and not a Git state). **Nothing in this repo records whether the U21 array was ever uploaded**, so no agent may assume either way. **If it was, the retirement is his to execute in the Hub**; this row is the notice, not the execution. **(f) U21 IS REGENERATED, NOT RESTORED.** Declaration → `PLAN` → authoring → suite, as a future teacher-lane session under CD-141. **পাঠ ২১ is `KEEP-AS-IS` সিরাত content** (the extraction's own 🟦★ marking, *কিছুই বদলানো যাবে না*) and `QB-CR-005`'s ruling stands: the ভাষণ text is the KEEP-AS-IS obligation, a personal name in an অনুশীলনী is not. **`QB-CR-008`'s `TOP-BAN-C5-13` correction to Q52 is history the archive preserves, and the regenerated bank inherits the conclusion, not the item.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; measured at HEAD by running the suite (`PLAN`/`COVERAGE`/`BLOOM-BAND`/`ENVELOPE-SYNC` all `N/A`, Analyze 0 of 57); CD-141(g) (n>=40 — not the binding constraint here); CD-135; CD-138; CD-142; CD-003; `QB-CR-005`; `QB-CR-008`; `QB-CR-014(g)`; AGENTS.md §7; master §5.3 |

At পাঠ ১৫ the span therefore runs *"থাকব না কো বদ্ধ ঘরে…"* through **"…কেমন করে দুঃসাহসী চলছে উড়ে স্বর্গপানে।"** **(b) THE AGENT'S PROPOSED পঙ্‌ক্তি READING IS REFUSED, and the measurement that refutes it is recorded rather than the conclusion alone (CD-145(f) — an arithmetic claim in a ruling is verified by running the thing it counts).** Counted at source over all four কবিতা, stanza gaps skipped: **under EIGHT PRINTED LINES the extraction's own delimiter falls exactly at line 8 in THREE of the four chapters** — পাঠ ১৩ *"…পাখির মতো ডাকতে।"*, পাঠ ১৮ *"…নামবে অন্ধকার।"*, পাঠ ২০ *"…সাফ্ সঞ্চারি অঙ্গুলি।"* — **and short only at পাঠ ১৫, at line 6.** Under the eight-পঙ্‌ক্তি reading the delimiter would agree at পাঠ ১৩ alone and be wrong at ১৫, ১৮ and ২০ (lines 5, 7 and 6). **The reading that makes the source self-consistent at three chapters is the reading that is right, and the one defective delimiter is পাঠ ১৫'s.** **(c) `CD-146`'s FLAG IS RESOLVED, AND IT WAS A REAL DEFECT rather than an ambiguity.** CD-146 recorded the extraction's *প্রথম ৮ পঙ্‌ক্তি* prose against its own endpoints and flagged the disagreement as a source limitation. **The limitation is now located: পাঠ ১৫'s delimited span is SHORT BY TWO PRINTED LINES**, and both the prose count and the endpoints were carrying it. **(d) THE স্বর্গপানে CAUTION STAYS OPEN — this row sets the SPAN, not the flag's resolution.** The eighth printed line contains **স্বর্গপানে**, and `C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md`'s পাঠ ১৫ সতর্কতা warns its sense here is *"আকাশের দিকে", ধর্মীয় অর্থে নয়*. The পাঠ ১৫ bank declined a শব্দার্থ item on the word for that reason and that decision is untouched. **The word now enters the memorisation span, which is a different question from what may be ASKED about it, and it goes to the scholar-review pass.** No agent resolves it. **(e) EXECUTION, per chapter, counted and not assumed.** **পাঠ ১৩ — CONFORMANT, verified by counting**: `QP-BAN-C5-U13-Q01`'s key holds eight lines ending *"পাখির মতো ডাকতে।"*, which is printed line 8, and its stem's *প্রথম আট পঙক্তি* is numerically right at this chapter because পাঠ ১৩ sets one পঙ্‌ক্তি per printed line. **পাঠ ১৫ — NONCONFORMING**: `QP-BAN-C5-U15-Q01` was authored from the defective delimiter and carries six printed lines; **corrected to the ruled span, key and stem endpoint both**, as a corrections-class act with exports regenerated and a new digest reported. **পাঠ ১৮ and পাঠ ২০ are unbanked**; their delimiters already agree with this row and need no adjustment when authored. **(f) THE REGISTER IS UNCHANGED.** `BAN-S01`'s `nape_frame`, `parts[]` and marks are correct as written and this row settles how one word in them is read. **No field moves.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-16, from the printed book (image supplied to the Principal's own reading; the endpoint independently corroborated here against `canon/marklogic/C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md`); **`CD-146` RESOLVED — its flag is closed and its (f) is superseded as to পাঠ ১৫, its text unedited**; CD-145(f); `canon/marklogic/SLOT_REGISTER.json` `BAN-S01` C5; `banks/C5_BAN_U13_QuestionBank_v1.json` Q01 (checked, conformant); `banks/C5_BAN_U15_QuestionBank_v1.json` Q01 (corrected); AGENTS.md §4 |
| CD-150 | 2026-08-16 | **ENG-S05, ENG-S13 AND ENG-S14 ARE PAPER-LEVEL — ENUMERATED BY (class, slot), NOT INFERRED, AND ENG-S05's REASON IS STRONGER THAN THE OTHER TWO (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-149`; no `CD-150` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row and the two gate files this session's gate-class commit wrote against it.)* **(a) RULED, AND THE PAIRS ARE ENUMERATED.** For English the paper-level bar is **`ENG-S05` at C3, C4, C5** · **`ENG-S13` at C4, C5** · **`ENG-S14` at C4, C5**. No chapter declaration owes a content reason for a barred pair; a chapter-lane ENG bank carrying one FAILs. **The bar attaches where the class's admitted task IS the paper-level task.** Below those classes the admitted task is a **D4 substitute — a DIFFERENT question, not a junior version of the same one** — and it stays in the chapter lane: `ENG-S13` is greeting fill-in-blanks at C1, five-sentence self-introduction at C2 and dialogue at C3; `ENG-S14` is the guided paragraph at C2 and C3. **The pairs are enumerated in canon and in code and are never derived from `d_code` or `admitted_task`** — a derived bar would move whenever a spine cell was re-coded, and CD-147's own finding is that **a slot's pipeline is not a fact about content.** **(b) ENG-S05's REASON IS STRONGER, AND IT IS RECORDED AS A SEPARATE FACT.** The second passage is **textbook-EXTERNAL by the spine's own statement**, so **no chapter can source it at all**. It is out of the chapter lane **permanently**, not merely absent from a paper's chapter draw. `ENG-S13`/`ENG-S14` are chapter-sourceable questions that the paper pipeline owns; `ENG-S05` is not sourceable from a chapter in the first place. **VERIFIED AT SOURCE BEFORE FILING, because the ruling would not have survived a C5-scoped reading:** the statement governs the SLOT, not only its C5 row, on three independent readings of `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_ENG_Spine.md` — the slot's own মূল কাঠামো header above the class table (*পাঠ্যবইয়ের বাইরের অংশ*); the prose below it, which says *এটাই **এই প্রশ্নের** আসল কাজ* and is not class-qualified, unlike its own sibling paragraph *তৃতীয় শ্রেণিতে নম্বর একটু বেশি কেন* which names its class explicitly; and the approval checklist item *দ্বিতীয় পড়া অংশটা পাঠ্যবইয়ের বাইরের*, unqualified in a list that class-qualifies wherever it means to (*C3: শুধু present simple*, *C1: শুধু a / an*). **Corroborated by the cause-codes themselves:** C4 is **D0** and C3 is **D1** — নম্বর বদল, *প্রশ্ন ও কাজ একই, নম্বর আলাদা* — so by the code's own definition the TASK is unchanged at C3. A content change would have been D3. **(c) AN UNSEEN-PASSAGE POOL, IF EVER BUILT, IS A SEPARATE NON-CHAPTER WORKSTREAM** minted by a later row. **This ruling neither reserves it nor precludes it**, and nothing here should be read as authorising one: what it settles is that `ENG-S05` cannot be served from the chapter lane, not what would serve it instead. **(d) ENG-S10 CARRIES NAPE's HALF-MARK FORM AS THE I-4 EXCEPTION, AND THE EXCEPTION IS A LITERAL IN THE PROVER, NOT A REGISTER FIELD.** `MarkLogic_Rules.md` I-4 bars আধা নম্বর *একমাত্র ব্যতিক্রম যেখানে নেপের কাঠামো নিজেই আধা নম্বর ব্যবহার করে … বর্তমানে এমন জায়গা একটাই: ইংরেজির S10*, and §৪ gives the reason the exception exists at all — নেপই ঠিক, আর নিয়মটা বদলাতে হবে. **`ENG-S10` is `0.5 × 10 = 5` at C5, C4 and C3; `ENG-S10` at C2 is `1 × 6 = 6` and is NOT a half-mark row** — the C2 task is different (৬টি আলাদা বাক্যে যতিচিহ্ন, each a whole task) and reading it as a half-mark cell would be an error. **The admitted set is `HALF_MARK_ADMITTED = {("ENG", "S10")}`, a closed literal in `tools/audits/slot_register_check.py`, keyed (subject, slot) and deliberately NOT readable from `SLOT_REGISTER.json`** (Principal ruling): a `half_mark_admitted` key in the register would let the row carrying the half mark also carry the permission for it, and **I-4 would be unfalsifiable by construction. A data row must not be able to silence an invariant** — the same class of finding as **CD-137**, where a frozen baseline was allowed to admit imported HISTORY the check cannot see while the agent's own NEW writing was FIXED rather than exempted. **Any other half-mark cell in the register is an error**, and widening the set takes a CD row. **MARK-VALUE needed no code change to admit 0.5** — it compares an item's marks against the register cell and the half mark rides through as DATA, which is seeded. **(e) ENG HAS NO `S15`. Its রচনা is `S14` and its চিঠি / আবেদনপত্র / ইমেইল is `S13`.** Nothing is to look for an `ENG-S15`, and this is recorded because the pre-CD-150 constant would have hunted one: BAN's রচনা is S15 and BAN's আবেদনপত্র is S14, so **the two subjects' shorts are offset by one at exactly the slots this row governs.** **(f) EXECUTABLE — `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`, constant `PAPER_LEVEL_SLOTS`, whose SHAPE this row changes and names, per CD-147(f)'s precedent.** It was a flat set of bare slot **shorts** (`{"S14", "S15"}`) tested with neither subject nor class in hand. It is now an enumerated frozenset of **(subject, class, slot)** read through `is_paper_level()` and `paper_level_slots()`, which replace eight bare membership tests. **BAN's behaviour is preserved exactly** — S14 and S15 at all five classes — so **CD-147 is not amended by this row; only the shape that carries it is.** **The old form could not have expressed ENG's bar and would have done damage trying:** `BAN-S05` is বহুনির্বাচনি (live D0, 5 marks, all five classes) and `BAN-S13` is এক কথায় প্রকাশ (live C3–C5), so a flat set holding those shorts for English's sake would have **categorically barred two live BAN teaching slots from every chapter bank in the repo**. It was also **already silently wrong on ENG**: run as it stood it would have barred ENG-S14 correctly by coincidence, hunted the ENG-S15 that does not exist, and left ENG-S05 and ENG-S13 wide open — nothing would have failed, the bar would simply have missed. **Seeded both directions and green:** 29 enumeration assertions with every boundary class asserted on BOTH sides; the BAN-S05/BAN-S13 regression asserted rather than argued; four ENG fail-seeds (an item in S05, S13 or S14 at C5, and a header merely ADMITTING S13); and **three negatives, which are the half that matters** — a C5 bank saying NOTHING about the three stays CLEAN (the obligation this row REMOVES; a gate that only gained the FAIL would redden every conformant ENG bank on its first run), and C3's S13 dialogue and C3's S14 guided paragraph stay chapter-lane. The prover's own subject axis is seeded at `SUBJECT-BLIND` and `SUBJECT-REFUSAL`. Full suite CLEAN; U13/U14/U15 unchanged. **(g) THE ENG REGISTER ROWS ARE NOT IN THIS COMMIT AND THE DATA COMMIT IS HELD — one cell, named.** `SLOT_REGISTER.json` still declares `scope_built: BAN C1-C5`, so no ENG bank is judgeable yet and COVERAGE refuses rather than passes. The hold is **`ENG-S11` (Form fill / numbers), whose C4 and C5 SELECTION the spine does not settle**: the মূল কাঠামো offers two forms under *(দুটোর যেকোনো একটা)*, C5's কারণ names **both** (*ফরম বা সংখ্যা*) and selects neither, C4's কারণ says **সংখ্যা** (*cardinal ও ordinal সংখ্যা*) while the slot's own prose says the form ladder runs *প্রথম → চতুর্থ → পঞ্চম* and that *চতুর্থ ও পঞ্চম শ্রেণির ফরম কঠিন লাগে না* — **two statements in one file that disagree at C4.** Every other ENG alternative cell is settled at source (`ENG-S07` selects suffix-prefix at C5 and a/an/the below; `ENG-S11` at C1 is COMPOSITE, সংখ্যা ৬ + ফরম ৫ = 11, stated twice in the spine). **A register column is all-or-nothing** — a partial ENG column fails I-1 at 95 and fails I-8 as an unbuilt rung — so the whole data commit waits on the ruling rather than landing with an invented selection. Compare BAN, where **every** C5 alternative row's কারণ names its selection outright (S06 *বিপরীত শব্দ*, S10 *পদ নির্ণয়*, S14 *আবেদনপত্র*); English's does not, and that difference is the whole of the hold. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16 (pre-approved content per CD-079; Q1/Q2/Q3 answered 2026-08-16); **`CD-147` CITED, NOT AMENDED — one row per subject, BAN's S14/S15 bar unchanged in scope and behaviour**; CD-147(f) for the executable-clause precedent; **CD-137** for why the I-4 exception is a prover literal and not a data field; CD-138(b) (task mode DECLARED, markers are authoring evidence only), CD-138(e), CD-138(f); CD-121(e) and QB-D-012 (seeds synthetic, controls may be live); `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_ENG_Spine.md` ENG-S05 / ENG-S10 / ENG-S11 / ENG-S13 / ENG-S14 and its approval checklist; `canon/marklogic/MarkLogic_Rules.md` I-4, I-8, §৩ (D0/D1/D3/D4), §৪; `tools/audits/slot_register_check.py`; `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`; AGENTS.md §4, §5 |
| CD-151 | 2026-08-16 | **THE FACTUAL/CURATION REVIEW IS ADOPTED AS TEACHER-LANE STEP 5b, AND THE TEMPLATE IT SITS IN IS VENDORED IN THE SAME ROW — because a step added to a procedure that lives only in chat is not added to anything (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-150` — the ENG register session's row, pushed at `5afc229` while this session was working — and no `CD-151` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row. **This row was first drafted as `CD-150` and renumbered on rebase: a concurrent session took that number and pushed first, so its row stands and this one moved.**)* **(a) THE TEMPLATE GAINS STEP 5b, between the suite and export.** The session runs the review **from its named path — `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`** — filling only that file's facts block from the CD-138(e) declaration and the source's own ⚠ block. **The prompt is NEVER composed in-session. An agent that writes its own review prompt has left the template**, and the reason is not procedural tidiness: the prompt chooses which questions get asked of the bank, so an author who writes it selects the grounds on which their own work is graded. That is not independent review however good the questions are. **(b) EVERY FIX IS LOGGED, WITH ITS QID AND WHAT CHANGED.** Defects found under 5b are fixed inside the lane; each fix is recorded in `SESSION_LOG.md`. **Unlogged self-correction after a self-run review is barred, and it is the same failure class as padding** — in both, the artifact is quietly moved toward the standard that judges it and the record shows only the result. Padding inflates a bank to clear a floor (CD-141(g)); silent post-review editing makes a clean verdict out of a dirty one, and neither leaves evidence that anything was ever wrong. **(c) A NON-CLEAN 5b VERDICT WHOSE FIX NEEDS A RULING, OR A CHANGE OUTSIDE THE LANE, IS A STOP-AND-REPORT. Push authorization under CD-141 now requires suite CLEAN **AND** 5b clean** — both, and the template's push step is amended to say so. **(d) 5b IS PRE-IMPORT SCREENING AND NARROWS NOTHING.** `CD-142(a)`'s item-level content review by the Hub's subject experts stands untouched; **a clean 5b verdict is not approval**, and promotion `reviewed → gold` remains the Principal's in the Hub (CD-003). 5b asks whether a bank is fit to be OFFERED for import. **(e) RETRO IS OWED ON FOUR BANKS, NOT THREE — `PENDING-P-039`.** পাঠ ১৩, ১৪ and ১৫ were authored and pushed before 5b existed. **পাঠ ১৬ joins them**: its last reviewer verdict was `1 REMAINING`, the defect was fixed and the gates re-passed, **but the gates check structure and the reviewer was checking fact**, so the correction stands unverified in the repo today — which is clause (b)'s gap in the one session that invented the step. **None is re-run here**, and the reason is uniformity: a retro run under the agent-composed prompt this row retires produces a verdict of unknown standing. **All four are re-run once against the vendored artifact, or the retro is not uniform.** **(f) WHY THE TEMPLATE IS VENDORED BY THIS ROW RATHER THAN CITED BY IT.** The template is the teacher lane's operational spine and it existed only in chat briefs — **a files-over-memory breach (AGENTS §4) in the one artifact that runs unattended.** A step cannot be added to a procedure with no path, no version and no diff; the row would have described a change nothing could be checked against. It is now `tools/teacher_lane_template.md`, and a teacher brief cites that path. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; body vendored verbatim from `HANDOFF_2026-08-16_teacher-lane-live.md` §5 as supplied — **that handoff is not in this repo and the absence is reported, not reconciled**; `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`; `tools/teacher_lane_template.md`; CD-141, esp. (a)(b)(c) and (g); CD-142(a); CD-144; CD-145; CD-003; `PENDING-P-039`; AGENTS.md §4, §5, §6 |
| CD-152 | 2026-08-16 | **`session_bootstrap.md` §1 STEP 4 SPLITS INTO TWO BRANCHES DECIDED BY COMPUTATION, NEVER BY JUDGEMENT — the stop stays for the case it was written for, and stops firing on the case CD-141 made routine (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-151`, minted this session; no `CD-152` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row. **Renumbered on rebase from `CD-151` for the reason given at `CD-151`.**)* **(a) BENIGN — THE MOUNT IS STRICTLY BEHIND. Both must hold, each COMPUTED and pasted verbatim in the session report:** `git merge-base --is-ancestor HEAD origin/main` → **true**, and `git rev-list --count origin/main..HEAD` → **0**. The clone then moves to `origin/main` and proceeds. **This is not reconciliation**: the clone holds nothing origin lacks, so nothing can be lost, and **`origin/main` is the authority every session already works from — the mount clone is a speed cache, not an authority** (§0 FACT 2: it exists because a GitHub clone takes thirty minutes, not because the mount is canonical). **(b) DIVERGENT, OR ANYTHING ELSE — UNCONDITIONAL STOP AND REPORT, unchanged.** This includes any case where the two commands cannot be run cleanly, and any case where `origin/main..HEAD` is non-empty **by even one commit**. **The agent does not diagnose its way past this branch** — the whole value of (a) is that it is a computation with no room for a judgement call, and an agent permitted to reason about (b) would import exactly the discretion (a) was built to exclude. **(c) UNDER (a) THE REPORT MUST STATE HOW FAR THE MOUNT LAGS AND NAME THE COMMITS.** Losing the stop must not lose the notification: the Principal keeps the signal that his working copy is stale, which is the only signal he gets, since the mount is pull-only for agents (§4) and nothing else tells him. **(d) REASON OF RECORD.** CD-141's lane **pushes unattended**, and the mount is pull-only for agents, so **a lagging mount is now the NORMAL opening state after every lane push, not an anomaly.** Step 4 as written fired on the routine case: two consecutive sessions stopped on it, both strictly behind, both benign. **A stop rule that fires on the routine case trains the operator to wave stops through** — and a stop that is waved through by habit is not protecting the case it was written for. That habit is the cost this row is paying down. **(e) FIRST APPLICATION IS THIS SESSION, and the row says so rather than leaving it to be inferred.** The governance session that files this row was itself stopped at step 4, strictly behind by the two commits the পাঠ ১৬ lane session had pushed. It proceeded under (a) on the Principal's in-session ruling, before this row existed — both computations run and pasted, the lag reported per (c). **A row applied in the session that files it is worth naming as such**; the alternative was a stop the Principal had already ruled against in the same breath. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; `tools/session_bootstrap.md` §1 step 4 amended to match; CD-141 (the unattended push that makes the lag routine); CD-083(b); AGENTS.md §3.1, §4, §9 |
| CD-153 | 2026-08-16 | **CD-141's PUSH CONDITION BECOMES REPO-WIDE GREEN, WITH THE DEFAULT INVERTED — the hole was never a missing gate name, it was that the condition was defined BY NAMING ONE SUITE (Principal ruling 2026-08-16).** *(Next free number verified at source before writing: `canon/DECISIONS.md` defines through `CD-152`, minted this session; no `CD-153` token existed anywhere in the repo outside this row. **Renumbered on rebase from `CD-152` for the reason given at `CD-151`.**)* **(a) THE FINDING.** The CD-141 lane pushed a repo with `canon_check.py` **red**, unattended, and nothing noticed: the পাঠ ১৬ build added `REF-1`/`REF-2` citations to two files, which is precisely the trigger AGENTS §5 names — *`canon_check.py` must pass before any push that touches `canon/` **or adds canon citations***. The lane's condition was *the whole suite CLEAN*, and *the suite* meant `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`. **A gate outside that name can go red invisibly, and adding `canon_check` to a list would close today's instance and leave the pattern.** **(b) RULED — the condition is REPO-WIDE GREEN, and the default is INVERTED.** Every gate in the repo is in the push condition unless this row names it out. **A gate added later is covered without amending anything**, which is the whole point of inverting the default: the failure mode being closed is a gate nobody remembered to add to a list. **(c) EXCLUSIONS, each named and each justified, from a run of every gate in the container clone at filing time (CD-145(f) — an enumeration in a ruling is produced by running the thing it enumerates).** **(i) Judge a NAMED ARTIFACT, not the repo, and self-test clean with no target:** `source_check.py` · `source_textcheck.py` · `math_arith_check.py`. They are run by the lane that authors the artifact, not by every push. **(ii) Cannot run without an argument the lane does not have:** `grid_count_check.py` (requires an image; `--selftest` PASSes) · `tools/hub-export/validate_import.py` (requires an envelope — **already run per-artifact at template step 6 under AGENTS §11**, so it is covered, not skipped) · `workstreams/lesson-plans/audits/validate_plan.py` (requires a plan). **(d) A ZERO-GATE `gates.py` IS NOT A GATE, AND THIS IS THE READING THE ROW TURNS ON.** Four workstream suites — `_template` · `curriculum-foundations` · `p01-nctb-stability` · `scholarship` — return **`RESULT: FAIL (a workstream with zero gates cannot declare anything final)`**. **Read literally, repo-wide green would be unsatisfiable on day one and the lane could never push again.** That message is not a red artifact; it is a placeholder asserting the ABSENCE of a gate, and **excluding it excludes no gate because there is none there.** The exclusion therefore **self-cancels**: the day any of those four gains a real gate the message stops printing and (b)'s default picks it up with no amendment. **This reading is the agent's and is flagged as such** — it was the only construction found that satisfies (b) without disabling the lane, and if the Principal reads it otherwise the row is corrected forward. **(e) THE STATE AT FILING, measured not assumed.** IN the condition and green: `tools_check.py` (1 warn) · `ledger_check.py` · `int_id_check.py` · `slot_register_check.py` · `bangla_script_check.py` · `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py` · `workstreams/support-books/audits/gates.py` (RED=0 GREY=4 PASS=13). **IN the condition and RED: `canon_check.py` — 2 fail**, the finding at (a), routed to a corrections session by the Principal's ruling of the same date rather than fixed here, because the defect sits in build-class and `_wip` files and this session is tools+ruling class (`QB-CR-016` exists because mixing classes is where debris enters). **(f) A SECOND FINDING, reported not rounded: `workstreams/support-books/audits/validate_admin_pass.py` CANNOT RUN IN ANY CONTAINER CLONE.** It crashes on hardcoded absolute paths outside the repo — `/mnt/user-data/uploads/…` and `/mnt/user-data/outputs/…` at lines 49–51. It is excluded under (c)(ii) **and the exclusion is a defect record, not an accommodation**: fixing the paths un-excludes it automatically under (b). **(g) BOTH COSTS ARE ACCEPTED ON THE RECORD.** **(i) The lane may stop on FAILs it did not cause.** Correct: **a red repo should not be pushed onto regardless of who reddened it**, and a lane that pushes onto red is how a red becomes permanent. **(ii) Authoring becomes gated on canon hygiene the lane does not control.** Acceptable — and **পাঠ ১৬ is the demonstration that build commits DO introduce canon citations**, so the lane is not a stranger to the hygiene it is now gated on. | Principal ruling 2026-08-16; **CD-141(c) amended as to its push condition, its text unedited**; AGENTS.md §5 (the clause the lane's condition failed to reach), §11; CD-145(f); `QB-CR-016`; CD-151; every gate enumerated at (c)–(f), each run in the container clone at filing time |

The scarce thing is the NUMBER, and every such series has the same race. **(d) IF THE NUMBER MOVED BETWEEN VERIFICATION AND COMMIT — STOP AND REPORT. Do NOT reconcile by taking the next one.** Silently sliding to the next free number is what makes the collision invisible: the row lands, nothing is duplicated, and **the evidence that two sessions raced is destroyed by the fix**. The next session resuming that clone then trips (b) — *non-empty by even one commit* — and stops on the most routine state the workflow produces. **CD-152(d) is the precedent and the warning: *a stop rule that fires on the routine case trains the operator to wave stops through*, and a stop waved through by habit is not protecting the case it was written for.** The 2026-08-17 session hit this on a single held `log-class` commit and stopped rather than reasoning past it, which is the behaviour CD-152 wanted — and the stop it obeyed was the wrong stop. **(b) RULED — BRANCH (c) BENIGN AHEAD. BOTH conditions computed, and both PASTED in the session report.** `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/main HEAD` → **true** (origin is an ancestor: the clone holds everything origin holds, so nothing can be lost), **AND every commit in `git log origin/main..HEAD` is one THIS SESSION'S OWN CHAIN made and is holding — enumerated by hash and subject in the report.** The enumeration is the load-bearing half: *ahead* alone is satisfied by a commit an earlier agent left behind, or by anything the session cannot account for, and neither is benign. **Accounting for each commit by name is what distinguishes held work from debris**, and it cannot be done by a computation alone — which is why (c) pairs one command with one enumeration rather than two commands. **(c) EVERYTHING ELSE REMAINS BRANCH (b), UNCONDITIONAL.** Any commit the session cannot account for · both-ahead-and-behind · the commands not running cleanly · a mismatch with a brief's expected HEAD. **CD-152's rule that the agent does not diagnose its way past (b) is untouched**, and (c) does not soften it: (c) is a NARROW, ENUMERATED exit, not a judgement call. **A session that cannot name every commit in the range has not qualified for (c) and is in (b).** **(d) WHY NOT SIMPLY PUSH FIRST.** Because push needs approval and approval may not come in the same session; the held state is legitimate and can persist across several sessions. Requiring a push to clear step 4 would make the approval gate and the bootstrap gate contradict each other, and the agent would satisfy one by breaking the other. **(e) EXECUTABLE — `tools/session_bootstrap.md` §1 step 4**, amended in the same session, with (c) written as a third branch beside (a) and (b) and the enumeration requirement stated in the file rather than left to this row. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-152 EXTENDED, not amended — its (a) and (b) branches and its no-diagnosing rule stand unchanged**; CD-152(d) cited as the precedent this row applies on the opposite axis; AGENTS.md §3.1, §5; CD-083(b); CD-154; `tools/session_bootstrap.md` §1 step 4 |

Run 2 returned `VERDICT: 4 DEFECT(S)` on a different four. **FALSE CLEARANCES: পাঠ ১৩ `Q52` was positively declared *verified sound this run* by TWO runs while its key offered জন্য as a word 'not in the poem' — and the poem prints জন্য.** Run 5 found it by mechanically testing every S12 offered word against the poem, which is what the earlier runs asserted rather than did. **(b) THE CONCLUSION, and it cuts both ways.** **A PASS on an item is not evidence the item is clean; a CLEAN verdict is not evidence the bank is clean.** Non-determinism is symmetric: it produces false clearances as readily as misses, and the false clearance is the dangerous half because nothing downstream re-examines a passed item. **`Step 5`'s *the verdict of record is the LAST run* is therefore a CONVENTION, not a finding** — it settles which report to cite, and it says nothing about whether the bank is clean. It reads as convergence and there is no evidence of convergence. **What it does buy is real and should not be discarded: it stops a fixer citing whichever run flatters the bank.** **(c) TWO CANDIDATE REMEDIES, RECORDED AND NEITHER BUILT** (Principal's instruction; he rules later). **(i) A VERDICT REQUIRES TWO CONSECUTIVE CLEAN RUNS.** Attacks false clearances directly and needs no change to the prompt — only to what counts as done. Costs a doubling of runs on every clean bank, and two runs of a non-deterministic reviewer can still agree wrongly, so it reduces the error rate without bounding it. **(ii) THE PRIOR REPORT IS AN INPUT TO THE NEXT RUN** — the reviewer is handed the previous findings and must, for each, state FIXED · STILL PRESENT · or WAS NEVER A DEFECT with its reason. Attacks misses directly, makes every dropped finding visible as an unanswered line, and costs one run rather than two. **Its risk is anchoring: a reviewer told what was found may confirm it rather than re-see the bank, and it would not have caught `Q52`, which no prior report named.** **(d) RECOMMENDED: (ii), WITH ONE ADDITION — and the recommendation is the agent's, not a ruling.** (ii) is cheaper, it fixes the failure mode actually measured most often here (three of the four evidence items are misses, one is a false clearance), and it converts the reports `CD-157` made durable into a working input rather than an archive. **The addition answers its own anchoring risk: require the enumerating sweep of check 1 to be run FRESH, before the prior report is opened** — the mechanical work first, the comparison second. `Q52` is the proof that the mechanical sweep is what finds what assertion misses. **(i) is not rejected; it is the stronger guarantee and the right choice if the Principal weights false clearances above misses**, and the two compose: (ii) every run, (i) before a bank is called finished. **(e) CD-157's VINDICATION, recorded because it was nearly not testable.** **This finding was only detectable because the reports are COMMITTED ARTIFACTS.** Comparing run 1's findings against run 2's silence requires both bodies on disk. **Under the paste-into-the-session practice CD-157 retired, run 2's silence would simply have ERASED run 1's findings** — the four defects would have vanished with the transcript, the bank would have carried a `VERDICT: 4` that omitted them, and the non-determinism itself would have been invisible. **The row that looked like bookkeeping is what made the measurement possible.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-151** step 5 (*verdict of record is the LAST run*) — read as a CONVENTION and not a convergence claim, its text unedited; **CD-157** (reports as committed artifacts) — vindicated at (e); **TOOLS-CR-008** (a fix that drops a sibling) — the same hazard on the fixer's side; CD-025 (the instrument before the verdict); `PENDING-P-039`; the six run reports at `workstreams/question-banks/reports/BAN_U1{3,4,5,6}_REVIEW_2026-08-17*.txt`; AGENTS.md §4, §5 |
| CD-160 | 2026-08-17 | **AN OUT-OF-SCOPE LIST SUPPRESSES COUNTING, NEVER SWEEPING — deferral partitions a bank BY ITEM while a factual defect lives BY CLAIM, and the two carvings cut across each other (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Next free number verified at source against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`816fc58`) immediately before this commit per CD-154: `origin/main` defines through `CD-155`, this branch through `CD-159`; no `CD-160` token existed anywhere in the repo.)* **(a) THE FINDING, and it will recur in every session that defers anything.** `TOOLS-CR-008` established that a factual defect is organised by **CLAIM**, and the check-1 amendment was written to sweep the whole bank for each flagged claim. An out-of-scope list partitions the same bank by **ITEM**. **The two partitions are not aligned, so a deferred qid can hide a claim-sibling from the very sweep the amendment exists to run.** **MEASURED:** run 6 flagged the claim *the poem uses word X where the poem prints another form* at `Q28`/`Q101`; the fix took those two; **`Q49` (ঘুম), `Q103` (ভরা) and `Q104` (লুকানো) carried the same claim, sat on the deferred list as check-6 S06 items, and were not swept.** Run 7 found them. **The enumerating check found the class; the out-of-scope list is what stopped the fix reaching it.** **(b) RULED.** **A claim sweep runs across the WHOLE bank regardless of deferral.** A deferred item carrying a swept claim is **REPORTED UNDER THE CLAIM, marked deferred — never omitted.** Deferral decides whether a finding is COUNTED against the verdict; it never decides whether the finding is LOOKED FOR. **The reviewer's job is to see; the list's job is to say what is owed to whom.** **(c) THE SECOND HALF, and it is the more general one: a hand-maintained list is the wrong instrument for membership in a claim class.** `Q79`'s key asserts শান্ত, which is nowhere in the section — it belongs to the deferred interpretive-keys group by every property that defines that group, and **it was simply missing from the list**, because the list was written out by hand in a brief and copied forward across runs. **THE SWEEP DEFINES MEMBERSHIP; THE LIST ONLY MARKS DISPOSITION.** A list that decides who is in a class will drift from the class; a list that records what is owed about members the sweep found cannot. **(d) EXECUTABLE — `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`, check 1 and the run instructions.** The prompt now states that an out-of-scope list suppresses counting and never sweeping, and that a deferred item carrying a swept claim is reported under the claim and marked deferred. **`Q79` is added to the interpretive-keys group, and the corrected group is recorded in the BANK's own `header.gaps`** rather than in a brief — the durable place, so the next session inherits the list from the artifact instead of from prose. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **`TOOLS-CR-008`** (a defect lives by claim, not by item) — this row is its second-order consequence; **CD-159** (non-determinism) — the same session's other finding, and this one is why a sweep must not be narrowed by a list; CD-157 (reports as committed artifacts); CD-151 step 5b; `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`; `workstreams/question-banks/banks/C5_BAN_U13_QuestionBank_v1.json` `header.gaps`; AGENTS.md §4, §5 |
| CD-161 | 2026-08-17 | **CD-159's TWO REMEDIES ARE BOTH ADOPTED AND THEY ARE NOT ALTERNATIVES — (ii) is the standing form of every 5b run, (i) is the PROMOTION condition (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Next free number verified at source against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`816fc58`) immediately before this commit per CD-154; `CD-160` minted in the same commit; no `CD-161` token existed anywhere in the repo.)* **(a) (ii) ADOPTED AS THE STANDING FORM OF EVERY RUN.** The prior report is an INPUT: the reviewer dispositions each previous finding **FIXED · STILL PRESENT · WAS NEVER A DEFECT** with its reason. **With the anti-anchoring addition that made it recommendable: the enumerating sweep of check 1 runs FRESH, before the prior report is opened.** Mechanical work first, comparison second — `Q52` is the standing proof, an item two runs asserted sound while a mechanical test of every offered word against the poem found it wrong. **Adopted on the agent's recommendation and its reasoning: it attacks the failure mode measured most often here, and it converts the reports CD-157 made durable into a working input rather than an archive.** **(b) (i) ADOPTED AS THE PROMOTION CONDITION, NOT THE REVIEW CONDITION — two consecutive CLEAN runs before a bank goes to Hub promotion.** The reasoning is where the cost belongs: **spend the expensive guarantee where irreversibility lives.** A review is repeatable and its verdict is revisable; **promotion `reviewed → gold` is a Hub act the repo cannot undo** (CD-003, and promotion is the Principal's). Doubling every run would buy the guarantee where it is cheap to recover and leave it unbought where it is not. **(c) THEY COMPOSE, and neither is weakened by the other.** (ii) every run, (i) before promotion. (ii) catches misses and makes dropped findings visible as unanswered lines; (i) attacks false clearances, which is the half nothing downstream re-examines. **Neither is a review waiver: a bank with two clean runs still gets (ii)'s disposition pass on its next run.** **(d) U14 IS THE FIRST BANK TO SATISFY (i) — and CD-159(c)(i)'s WARNING STANDS.** `BAN_U14`'s runs 3 and 4 both returned `VERDICT: CLEAN`, run 4 reached independently under (ii)'s fresh-sweep ordering and then AGREEING with run 3 while naming two places run 3 had glossed. **This is recorded as evidence, not as a proof of cleanliness: two runs of a non-deterministic reviewer can agree wrongly, so (i) is a NECESSARY condition and not a sufficient one.** No promotion follows from this row; promotion remains the Principal's act in the Hub. **(e) STATE OF THE FOUR at filing:** U14 **two consecutive CLEAN** · U15 **one CLEAN** (run 4) · U16 **one CLEAN** (run 3) · U13 **one claim class outstanding** at run 7, addressed the same session. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-159 AMENDED as to its remedies — its finding and its evidence unchanged, its (c)(i) warning expressly preserved at (d)**; CD-151 step 5b and step 5 (*verdict of record is the LAST run*, kept as a convention per CD-159(b)); CD-157 (durable reports, the input (ii) needs); CD-003 (promotion is a Hub act and the Principal's); CD-142(a) (item-level content review remains the Hub's subject experts'); `TOOLS-CR-008`; CD-160; `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`; AGENTS.md §4, §5, §11 |
| CD-162 | 2026-08-17 | **CD-158's পাঠ ১৩ `Understand` EXCEPTION IS RETRACTED FORWARD — it is INERT, not wrong (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Next free number verified at source against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`8798c16`) immediately before this commit per CD-154: `origin/main` defines through `CD-161`; no `CD-162` token existed anywhere in the repo.)* **(a) THE MEASUREMENT THAT RETIRES IT.** At **n = 107** the `Understand` requirement is `ceil(25% × 107) = 27`, and the bank carries **29 — margin +2 on the ORDINARY rule.** PLAN has been printing the fact against itself every run: *Understand margin +2 against a REDUCED requirement of +1*. **The exception has not been exercised since the commit that created the state it was written for.** **(b) WHY IT DISSOLVED, and this is the part worth keeping.** CD-158 was written when the bank stood at 111 items with `Understand` at +1. Four items then left it — `Q122` and `Q123` (retired as independently defective, neither kept to hold a number) and `Q103` and `Q104` (retired because their stems' premise was FALSE: they claimed the কবিতা uses words the section never prints standalone). **Every one of the four was a false-premise or duplicate item, and removing them RAISED every margin**, because the floor is a percentage and the denominator fell faster than the numerator. **THE MARGIN PROBLEM WAS AN ARTEFACT OF THE DEFECTS, NOT OF THE CHAPTER.** That is retrospective support, from arithmetic rather than argument, for the ruling that **keeping known-bad items to hold a number was the wrong trade** — the trade would have preserved a margin by preserving the very items that were depressing it. **(c) CD-158 WAS CORRECT WHEN MADE AND IS NOT BEING CALLED WRONG.** Its evidence stood: two authoring attempts had each drawn a defect, and wave 3's header had already named the content limit. **A ruling that becomes unnecessary is not a ruling that was mistaken**, and the distinction matters because the repo's append-only discipline makes the record of a superseded row permanent. **CD-158's text is UNEDITED**; a pointer is added to this file's *Forward-only amendments* section so a reader arriving at CD-158 from a citation elsewhere is not left with the live form. **(d) `PLAN_MARGIN_EXCEPTIONS` — the agent RECOMMENDS KEEPING U13's KEY, INERT, and does not choose.** **For keeping it:** the key is now a *test of the mechanism*, not a licence — it is the only live data proving that an honoured-but-unexercised exception is REPORTED rather than silent, which is the property CD-158(f) was seeded for and the one that stops a reduced margin coming to look like the rule; and the `cd158_selftest` block's negative case (*CD-158's chapter, Understand short by 1, quiet*) exercises this key, so dropping it would delete a seed's target and re-create `TOOLS-CR-007`'s vacuous-seed shape. **For dropping it:** an inert exception is a standing invitation to a later session to notice a short margin, find a key already present, and read it as permission — and CD-160's own finding is that lists drift from the thing they describe. **The agent's recommendation is KEEP, with this row as the reason and the seed as the guard**; the Principal rules. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-158 RETRACTED FORWARD, its text unedited, and expressly NOT called wrong**; CD-159 / CD-161 (the review discipline that produced the retirements); `TOOLS-CR-008` and CD-160 (the false-premise claim class whose removal dissolved the margin); `TOOLS-CR-007` (why deleting a seed's target is its own defect); `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py` `PLAN_MARGIN_EXCEPTIONS`; | CD-163 | 2026-08-17 | **NOTHING IN THE REPO ASKS WHY WORK IS STILL HELD *AFTER* APPROVAL — a durability ruling sat unpushed for six sessions and was caught by the Principal opening a folder (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Next free number verified at source against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`8798c16`) immediately before this commit per CD-154; `CD-162` minted in the same commit; no `CD-163` token existed anywhere in the repo.)* **(a) THE PATTERN, not the incident.** Every session in this chain ended *nothing pushed, awaiting approval*. **Approval was then given — in chat — and the next session's bootstrap treated the held commits as ROUTINE under branch (c)**, which is exactly what CD-156 built (c) to do: name them, account for them, proceed. **(c) asks whether the session can ACCOUNT for what it holds. It never asks how long it has been holding it, or whether the reason for holding has already expired.** So an approval granted in conversation and not executed leaves no trace anywhere in the repo, and every subsequent bootstrap re-affirms the hold as healthy. **The mechanism that made the hold safe is the same mechanism that made it invisible.** **(b) WHAT IT COST, measured.** **26 commits** — `CD-156` through `CD-161`, `TOOLS-CR-008`, sixteen committed review reports, every bank fix and every retirement — existed **only in a disposable container clone**. Among them: **`CD-157`, the row ruling that review findings must be committed artifacts BECAUSE transcript-only findings are unrecoverable.** **A durability ruling that was itself not durable, for six sessions.** The 61 per-qid findings it exists to preserve were, for that whole period, in precisely the state it forbids. **It was caught by the Principal noticing his own folder held no `*_REVIEW_*` files — not by any gate, not by any bootstrap branch, and not by the agent, which reported the hold accurately every single time.** Accurate reporting of a dangerous state is not detection of it. **(c) THE ASYMMETRY THAT MADE IT PERSIST.** The repo's push discipline is built entirely against pushing TOO EARLY — AGENTS §3.1, CD-083(b)'s per-commit range check, CD-153's repo-wide green, CD-141's boundary drawn twice. **There is no counterweight against pushing TOO LATE**, and the loss modes are not symmetric: a premature push is visible, reviewable and revertible; **an unpushed clone is one container reclamation from total loss, silently.** **(d) PROPOSED, NOT BUILT (Principal's instruction): step 4 reports HELD-COMMIT AGE.** Branch (c) already enumerates `origin/main..HEAD`; the addition is one line of the same data — the committer date of the LAST entry in `git rev-list origin/main..HEAD` — the **oldest** unpushed commit's date — printed in the session report beside the enumeration, with a **WARNING above a threshold** (one calendar day is the agent's suggestion, since a same-session hold is the routine case (c) exists for and an overnight one is not). **It is a WARNING and not a stop**, deliberately: a legitimately long hold exists — approval may genuinely be pending — and CD-152(d)'s lesson is that a stop firing on a routine case trains the operator to wave stops through. **What it changes is that the age becomes a number in the report rather than a fact nobody is looking at.** **(e) THE SECOND HALF, weaker but recorded because it is the real gap: the repo cannot see approval at all.** Age is a proxy. **Approval lives in conversation and nothing in the repo represents it**, so no check can ask the question that actually matters — *was this authorised to go, and did it go?* A durable form of approval (a line in `PENDING_PRINCIPAL.md`, a signed row, anything on disk) would let step 4 compare **authorised** against **pushed** instead of inferring from a clock. **Not proposed as work here** — it touches the Principal's own workflow rather than the agent's, and the age warning is the part buildable without it. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-156** branch (c) — extended in spirit, its computation unchanged; **CD-157** — the row this pattern nearly lost, and the reason the cost is measurable; CD-152(d) (a stop on the routine case trains the operator to wave stops through) — why (d) is a warning and not a stop; AGENTS.md §3.1, §4; CD-083(b); CD-153; `tools/session_bootstrap.md` §1 step 4; the push at `816fc58..8798c16` |
| CD-164 | 2026-08-17 | **UNPROPAGATED SUPERSEDE — a ruling that CORRECTS a source artifact was recorded and never applied TO that artifact, and the stale artifact then manufactured a false defect against a correct bank (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Next free number verified at source against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`53f9ac6`) immediately before this commit per CD-154: `origin/main` defines through `CD-163`; no `CD-164` token existed anywhere in the repo.)* **(a) WHAT HAPPENED.** `CD-149` (2026-08-16) ruled `BAN-S01`'s unit is the PRINTED LINE, counted all four কবিতা to prove it, and named **পাঠ ১৫'s delimiter as the one defective artefact** — short by two printed lines. It then corrected the BANK (`QP-BAN-C5-U15-Q01`) and **never touched the SOURCE FILE the defect lives in.** The row's own execution clause (e) lists the bank as corrected and the other chapters as needing no adjustment; **the file carrying the defect it had just located is absent from that list.** **(b) WHAT IT COST, and this is why the class is worth a row.** A later session built a review facts block by reading `C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md` at source — correctly, per the at-source discipline — and **inherited the stale delimiter**. The reviewer then reported `QP-BAN-C5-U15-Q01` as carrying two lines beyond its span: **a FALSE DEFECT against an item CD-149 had itself already corrected.** It cost a review finding, a session's attention, and a deferral entry that outlived three more runs. **A stale source does not sit inert — it actively generates wrong findings, and it generates them against the artifacts that obeyed the ruling.** **(c) THE CLASS, named so it can be looked for: UNPROPAGATED SUPERSEDE.** A ruling may correct (i) a policy, (ii) a gate, (iii) a bank, or (iv) **a source artifact**. For the first three the repo has machinery — policy versions, gate seeds, digests and review runs. **For (iv) it has nothing**: no gate reads a spine or an extraction against the rulings that correct it, by CD-138(b)'s deliberate design (*COVERAGE never reads a spine file — that is the point, not an omission*). So a source correction propagates **only if the session that rules it also edits the file**, and if it does not, **nothing will ever notice.** **(d) THE AUDIT, run and reported (Principal's instruction: fix nothing beyond পাঠ ১৫).** Every `CD-1xx` row naming a source artifact was examined for whether it CORRECTS one and whether the correction landed. **CD-149 — pointed out UNPROPAGATED, corrected this session (CR-003, lane c5-bangla).** **CD-127(e) — PROPAGATED**: it corrected two bullets in `C5_BAN_Source_01.md` under `CR-002`, and the file carries both the new text and a parenthetical preserving the old, which is the precedent CR-003 followed. **CD-146 — NOTHING OWED**: it FLAGGED the delimiter as a source limitation without ruling a correction, and `CD-149` resolved it. **CD-122 · CD-134 · CD-138 · CD-139 · CD-140 · CD-147 · CD-150 · CD-155 — NOTHING OWED**: each CITES a spine or an extraction as evidence and none rules an edit to one; `CD-150(b)` in particular verified an ENG-spine statement at source and changed nothing. **`PENDING-P-041` — DELIBERATELY UNPROPAGATED and correct**: it records a possible ENG-spine defect and says in terms that the spine is NOT amended, reading (i) applied. **Result: one instance, now closed, and no second unpropagated source correction on the record.** **(e) WHAT WOULD CATCH THE NEXT ONE — proposed, not built.** The audit above is a grep a gate could run: **a row whose text names a source artifact AND contains a correction verb is a candidate, and the candidate is discharged only by a corrections-ledger row citing that CD number.** That is mechanical — `ledger_check.py` already parses every ledger and every lane, so the missing half is the join from `canon/DECISIONS.md` to it. **It would have flagged CD-149 the day after it was filed.** Not built here: it is a new check on a canon-class file and the Principal rules whether a gate may read `DECISIONS.md` at all, which no gate currently does. **(f) THE SHAPE THIS SHARES WITH `CD-163`.** Both are gaps in *following through* rather than in deciding: CD-163's approvals were given and not executed; CD-164's correction was ruled and not applied. **In both, every individual artifact was correct and the link between them was missing, and in both the repo's machinery pointed at the wrong risk** — guarding against a premature push, and against a gate reading a surface — while the loss came from the step nobody was watching. | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-149** — the unpropagated row, its text unedited and its ruling now applied; **CD-146** (the flag CD-149 resolved); **CD-127(e)/CR-002** — the propagated precedent CR-003 followed; CD-124 (per-lane ledger numbering, and the CR-003 cross-lane deferral); CD-138(b) (why no gate reads a spine); CD-163 (the sibling follow-through gap); `canon/_wip/c5-bangla/CORRECTIONS.md` `CR-003`; `canon/marklogic/C5_Bangla_Source_13-23.md` পাঠ ১৫; AGENTS.md §4, §6 |
| CD-165 | 2026-08-17 | **THE C5 বিরামচিহ্ন SET IS REGISTER DATA — `taught_set` + `taught_set_source` at (BAN, C5, BAN-S11) AND NOWHERE ELSE, with ABSENCE READING AS *NOT DECLARED* RATHER THAN AS PERMISSION (Principal ruling 2026-08-17).** *(Number verified free against a freshly fetched `origin/main` (`dd40a93`, defines through `CD-163`) in the command sequence that wrote this row, per CD-154. `origin` moved once mid-session already, which is why the check is re-run and not remembered.)* **(a) THE SET.** C5 is taught **দাঁড়ি · কমা · প্রশ্নচিহ্ন · বিস্ময়চিহ্ন**. **ড্যাশ and সেমিকোলন are NOT admitted at C5.** The register carries the four as data because they are a fact about the C5 book: they differ per class and they are read by a gate, which is what the register is for. **(b) THE FORM, and it is CD-155's precedent doing the work.** `taught_set` is a list of mark names; `taught_set_source` beside it is REQUIRED and carries where the set comes from. UNSELECTED established that an assertion which cannot be traced is indistinguishable from an unfilled field, and D5's `absent_reason` established it before that. **The source line is also the honest record of a gap: the spine's C5 row names the SLOT (নেপ §১১ · বিরামচিহ্ন) and enumerates NO mark, so the four are this ruling's, not the spine's, and the field says so rather than implying the spine already held them.** **(c) WHICH ROWS MAY DECLARE ONE IS NOT DATA.** `TAUGHT_SET_DECLARABLE = {("BAN", 5, "BAN-S11")}` is a closed literal in `tools/audits/slot_register_check.py`, beside `HALF_MARK_ADMITTED` and for the identical reason (CD-137's laundering finding, CD-150's I-4 ruling): **a data row must not be able to mint its own permission.** Left open, the one place a wrong mark set would be invisible is the row that also granted itself the right to have one. **(d) ABSENCE IS NOT PERMISSION, and this is the half that makes the mechanism worth having.** `TAUGHT_SET_REQUIRED = {("BAN", 5, "S11")}` in the bank gate makes a MISSING `taught_set` a FAILURE for a class that carries S11 items — not a waiver. Read the other way the mechanism would be worse than nothing: **the only cell whose marks went unchecked would be the cell nobody declared, and the remedy for a failing bank would be to delete the declaration.** **(e) IT DOES NOT TRANSFER DOWNWARD, verified at source, and that is precisely why the key is C5-only.** The spine's **C2** row is the only S11 row in the file that names marks at all — *দাঁড়ি, কমা, প্রশ্নবোধক যোগ্যতাতেই নাম ধরে লেখা আছে* (যোগ্যতা ১৩.২), **three** marks, one fewer than C5's four. **C3, C4 and C5 name none.** So there is no ladder to interpolate: a C4 row carrying C5's four would be an invention with a data row's authority, and a C4 row carrying C2's three would be a guess. Each class waits for its own ruling, and until it has one its S11 items do not pass. **(f) WHAT THE ENFORCEMENT ASKS, and where each half lives.** The register declares the SET and the prover proves its shape; the BANK gate asks the question only a bank can answer — **does any item REQUIRE a mark the class is not taught?** — by scanning every field an S11 item names its marks in, `model_note` INCLUDED. That inclusion is deliberate: these items state their required marks in the marker-facing note, so a scan of student-facing strings alone would find every item naming no mark at all and **pass by looking in the wrong place.** **(g) MEASURED CONSEQUENCE, REPORTED AND HELD — the enforcement half is written and NOT COMMITTED, because it FAILS SEVEN ITEMS and CD-153 wants the suite green.** Three are the ruled re-keys (`U15 Q75` ড্যাশ · `Q80` কোলন+সেমিকোলন · `Q81` কোলন+সেমিকোলন) and are fixed in this session. **FOUR ARE OUTSIDE THE RULING AND ARE THE PRINCIPAL'S TO RULE: `U16 Q78` requires ড্যাশ, which this row bars by name; `U14 Q66` · `U16 Q79` · `U16 Q82` require উদ্ধরণ চিহ্ন, which this row NEITHER admits NOR bars — it is unmentioned, and (d) makes unmentioned mean not admitted.** The quotation case is not a re-key: `U14 Q66`'s stimulus is reported speech (*খরগোশ বলল …*), so the mark is required by the sentence's own shape and removing it means re-stemming the item. **Whether উদ্ধরণ চিহ্ন joins the C5 set is a teaching question about what the book prints, not a repair an agent may choose.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-17; **CD-155** (UNSELECTED — the form this field follows); **CD-150** + **CD-137** (why WHICH-ROW-MAY-DECLARE is a literal and not a field); CD-138(b)/(g) (the register is what COVERAGE reads); CD-153 (why (g) is held rather than committed red); `canon/marklogic/SLOT_REGISTER.json` (BAN, C5, BAN-S11) + `self_checks.TAUGHT_SET_C5_S11`; `MarkLogic_BAN_Spine.md` · `BAN-S11` C2 and C5 rows; `tools/audits/slot_register_check.py`; `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py` COVERAGE |
