CD-135's own argument is what carries it: *absence is the only thing a pool can be guilty of* was the reason a pool cannot fail a ceiling, and it is equally the reason a pool cannot fail a floor **once the floor's purpose is read** — a floor exists so a compliant PAPER stays constructible, and a paper is composed across chapters, not out of one. **CD-135 corrected which bound binds; this row corrects which ARTIFACT is bound.** **(ii) `CD-141(g)`'s 40-ITEM MINIMUM AND ITS margin-≥2 DERIVATION ARE RETIRED.** The minimum was never authored as a policy — CD-141(g) states it as *a stated consequence of the margin rule*, `0.80n + 8 <= n`, and the margin rule was PLAN's. **A consequence outlives its premise only if somebody re-rules it, and nobody has.** **(iii) `REF-09 §4.3`'s 20-ITEM FLOOR, AS READ AT CHAPTER SCOPE, IS RETIRED** — and it is named because it is the one an implication-reading would have missed. It is not derived from the four Bloom floors, so (a)'s first three retirements do not reach it, and it sits in live code at `g_coverage`. **REF-09 §4.3 and REF-08 §4.1 are not edited and are not wrong**: REF-08 §4.1 sizes a HOMEWORK pool at 20 so routine rotation and reactive top-ups do not run it dry, which is a claim about a teacher's draw over a year and not a claim about a question bank's conformance. **What is retired is the READING of it as a bank gate, not the number.** **(iv) `CD-138(g)`'s PER-SLOT DEMAND IS RETIRED AT POOL LEVEL ONLY** and is otherwise untouched — see (b). **AND THE BOUND THAT REMAINS IS `QUESTION_POLICY` §4's OWN SENTENCE, quoted here because its attribution has already been got wrong once:** *"**No ceiling.** The real upper bound is content: stop when the chapter's extraction is exhausted, because past that point new questions are near-duplicates of existing ones."* **That sentence is §4's own and is unattributed. It is NOT REF-08 §4.1** — §4.1's own words are *"minimum 20 questions per lesson Pool"*, the floor this row retires, and §4 already instructs *cite §4.3, not §4.1, for the chapter reading*. **The brief that produced this row carried the misattribution and it was caught at the verify-at-source pass, which is recorded rather than smoothed: this is CD-121(c)'s pattern — a citation acquiring authority by repetition — caught before it landed in canon rather than after.** **(b) THE AXES SURVIVE. THE ENFORCEMENT POINT MOVES TO THE PAPER, AND THIS ROW MOVES IT RATHER THAN DELETING IT.** Bloom levels are still tagged on every item; **they are RECORDED, NOT RATIONED**, and an untagged or wrongly-tagged item still FAILs. **REF-06 §3.6's bounds — both of them — and CD-138(g)'s full per-slot demand bind the PAPER**, which composes across chapters and is the only artifact that was ever obliged to hold a distribution. **No single chapter owes a paper's worth of any slot**, which is the substantive error the pool-level reading made: it charged every chapter with the whole paper's demand and then discovered, correctly, that thin chapters cannot pay it. **THIS AMENDS `UD-23` AS RECORDED IN `CD-121(a)`.** UD-23 reads *the Bloom axis governs the POOL, the domain axis governs the paper*; **its pool half is superseded to the extent it licenses pool-level ENFORCEMENT.** The AXIS ASSIGNMENT ITSELF STANDS — REF-06 §3.6 remains the Bloom axis and MarkLogic §৩ remains the domain axis, they still do not measure the same thing, and *the wider of* is still wrong. **CD-121(a) is superseded on the same narrow ground CD-135 already superseded it on, one step further: CD-135 said the pool reading is a floor not a band; this row says the pool is not the reading's subject.** **`CD-122` and `CD-133` are UNDISTURBED** — CD-122's *easy ≥30% present in the pool* is a PRESENCE test and not a count against a floor, and its no-pool-level-hard-test half is this row's own argument arriving early. **The supersede rows are filed in `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §9's table against CD-135 and against CD-121(a), and the superseded wording is quoted in place at each site per the file's forward-only rule.** **(c) WHAT BINDS A BANK — the complete list, and now the ONLY list.** **1.** `CD-138(e)`'s admissibility declaration, derived from the source at source, with a one-line CONTENT reason per excluded slot. **2.** Every item's task matches the spine's কারণ column for its (subject, class, slot) — **not its slot id, not its marks** (`CD-138(b)`, `CD-138(h)`). **3.** `PAPER_LEVEL_SLOTS` per `CD-147` and `CD-150`, keyed (subject, class, slot). **4.** §4's near-duplicate ban. **5.** Taught-set constraints — `CD-165` / `CD-166`; **no item may require a term the class's source does not print.** **THE AUTHORING INSTRUCTION IS: every admissible type, as many as the chapter supports.** It does not admit a paper-level slot and **it does not license padding** — `CD-141(g)`'s bar on authoring items to satisfy a gate is retired along with the gate that motivated it, but `CD-151(b)`'s reason survives it: an item authored to move a number is not an item, whatever number it moves. **(d) `PLAN` COMPUTES NO COUNTS.** It keeps (c)1–3 as conformance checks — the declaration, the task match, the paper-level bar — plus its within-slot near-duplicate scan. **`CD-142(b)`'s substitution of PLAN for the human countersign HOLDS for those**; the COUNT half of CD-142(b)'s enumeration — Bloom margins, full per-slot demand — goes with (a), and P-037's type rule is retained as a conformance check, being about a type and not a count. **A bank can no longer STOP at PLAN on count grounds, which is the point of the row and not a side effect of it.** **(e) `CD-170` STANDS. THERE IS NO IN-REPO FIXING OF পাঠ ১৩ · ১৪ · ১৫ · ১৬, AND `CD-169`'s RESIDUAL IS DISCHARGED WITHOUT TOUCHING A BANK.** CD-169 retained `U15 Q75` and `Q81` **solely** because the Apply floor could not absorb their retirement. **(a) removes the floor, so the retention is no longer forced — and CD-169(c)'s remedy is not what discharges it.** CD-169(c) says the two are RETIRED if Apply supply rises; supply has not risen, the floor has gone, and **retiring them now would be an in-repo fix of a bank CD-170 closed.** **`Q75` AND `Q81` REMAIN IN THE BANK**, no longer as items held by arithmetic but as **ordinary reviewable items on the `P039_HUB_HANDOFF_MANIFEST` — the Hub's subject experts dispose of them under `CD-142(a)`, which is where item-level content disposition has belonged since CD-142.** **CD-169(b)'s finding is not retracted**: both items require marks barred at C5 and both remain defective on that ground. What changes is that the repo no longer holds them for a reason it is embarrassed by. **`gates.py`'s RED-by-design residual — CD-169(d)'s deliberately red-capable COVERAGE — ends when Class 2's amendment lands**, and it ends by the count check leaving, not by the taught-set check weakening: **the taught-set enforcement at (c)5 is untouched and still FAILs these two items.** *(Recorded plainly, because it is the one place this row leaves a bank redder than it found it: CD-153's repo-wide-green condition is NOT restored for U15 by this row alone. The taught-set FAIL is real, is CD-169(d)'s intended behaviour, and its disposition is the Hub's under CD-170.)* **(f) `PENDING-P-036` IS CLOSED AS MOOT, AND THE THIN-CHAPTER QUESTION DISSOLVES RATHER THAN BEING RULED.** P-036 asked whether pool floors should be **absolute counts sized to demand** rather than percentages of whatever the pool holds. **There are no pool floors, so there is nothing to convert.** Its underlying observation is not lost and is the reason this row is right: *the pool is punished for growing*, on the one axis where §4 asks for growth. **The demand-side question P-036 could not answer — how many papers a chapter's pool must supply — is now the PAPER lane's, where the data to answer it lives.** **THE THIN-CHAPTER QUESTION WAS NEVER A PENDING ROW** — verified at source, no such row exists anywhere in `PENDING_PRINCIPAL.md` — **it lives in `CD-141(g)`'s grow-or-combine clause, which (a) retires.** **পাঠ ১৯ and ২৩ are buildable under this row with no per-chapter ruling, and NO THIN-CHAPTER CLASS IS MINTED.** A class of chapter defined by a threshold that no longer exists would be a category with no members. **(g) STEP 5b IS RETAINED AND IS NON-BLOCKING — THIS SUPERSEDES `CD-151(c)`.** 5b still runs from `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`, **never composed in session** (`CD-151(a)`), every fix still logged with its qid (`CD-151(b)`), and its report is still a COMMITTED ARTIFACT (`CD-157(d)` — a 5b run without a committed report is not a run). **CD-151(c)'s push precondition — *suite CLEAN AND 5b clean* — is SUPERSEDED: push requires the suite CLEAN only, and the 5b report accompanies the bank to the Hub as reviewer input.** **THE RATIONALE, on the row, because it is the clause most likely to be read as a loosening:** পাঠ ১৬ ran with 5b LIVE and returned **2 defects against ১৩–১৫'s average of 20**. **Removing 5b would not remove those defects — it would forward them to a human to find by reading**, which is the trade CD-151(f) refused and this row is not making. What 5b is losing is its veto, not its run. **CD-151(d) is untouched: a clean 5b verdict was never approval, and a non-clean one was never a bank's disqualification from being OFFERED.** **(h) THE TEACHER-LANE TEMPLATE IS SUBJECT-NEUTRAL AND TAKES A CHAPTER LIST — Principal-ruled here, not an agent diff.** `tools/teacher_lane_template.md` is rewritten to carry subject, class, chapter list and source path in its header, and to run declaration → author to the chapter's content limit → 5b → full suite → exports → one commit, per chapter. **Its hard boundaries are unchanged and are re-stated rather than assumed: never ask the operator (`CD-141(e)`), never pad, never patch policy, stop-and-report on anything outside the bank-authoring classes.** | Principal ruling 2026-08-18; **`CD-135` SUPERSEDED** (its text unedited); **`CD-121(a)`'s UD-23 pool half superseded, its axis assignment untouched**; **`CD-141(g)` retired**; **`CD-151(c)` superseded**; **`CD-138(g)` retired at pool level only**; `CD-138(b)`, `CD-138(e)`, `CD-138(h)`; `CD-142(a)`, `CD-142(b)`; `CD-147`; `CD-150`; `CD-165`, `CD-166`; **`CD-169` discharged by the floor's removal, its items retained**; **`CD-170` (no in-repo fixing) OBEYED, not amended**; CD-122, CD-133 (undisturbed); CD-153; CD-157; `PENDING-P-036` CLOSED-MOOT; `REF-06 §3.6`; `REF-08 §4.1` and `REF-09 §4.3` (read, not edited); `canon/QUESTION_POLICY.md` §4, §6, §9 → v2.0; `workstreams/question-banks/audits/gates.py`; `tools/teacher_lane_template.md`; AGENTS.md §4, §5, §6 |

## Forward-only amendments (pointers only — the cited rows' text is never edited)

Per this file's own rule, *reversal = new row citing the old*. These pointers exist so a reader
arriving at a superseded row from a citation elsewhere is not left with the superseded form.

- **CD-135** — **SUPERSEDED IN WHOLE by CD-171(a)(i).** The pool-level Bloom FLOOR it minted is
  retired with the ceiling it removed: **nothing binds a pool on the Bloom axis**, and REF-06 §3.6's
  bounds — both of them — bind the PAPER. **CD-135 corrected which BOUND binds; CD-171 corrects
  which ARTIFACT is bound**, and CD-135's own reasoning is what carries it — *absence is the only
  thing a pool can be guilty of* was always an argument about what a pool is FOR, and a floor exists
  so a compliant PAPER stays constructible across chapters. **CD-135(d) SURVIVES its own row**: a
  level the chapter cannot supply is still stated in the bank header as a content fact, which was
  never an arithmetic point. CD-135(h)'s পাঠ ১৩ projection and (g)'s *a percentage floor implicitly
  caps every other level* are retained as EVIDENCE FOR the supersede, not retracted by it.
  **CD-135's text is unedited.**
- **CD-121(a)** — its record of **UD-23**'s pool half (*the Bloom axis governs the pool*) is
  **superseded by CD-171(b) to the extent it licenses pool-level ENFORCEMENT, and no further**.
  **The axis assignment itself STANDS** and is the half CD-121(a) was filed for: REF-06 §3.6 is the
  Bloom axis, MarkLogic §৩ is the domain axis, the two never measured the same thing, and *"the
  wider of"* remains wrong. This is the **second** narrowing of the same clause — CD-135 said the
  pool reading is a floor and not a band; CD-171 says the pool is not the reading's subject — and
  the pair is recorded together so the order is legible. **(b) and (c)–(e) are untouched.**
  **CD-121's text is unedited.**
- **CD-141(g)** — **RETIRED by CD-171(a)(ii).** The 40-item minimum was never authored as policy:
  the row states it as *a stated consequence of the margin rule*, `0.80n + 8 <= n`, and CD-171(d)
  retires the margin rule. **A consequence outlives its premise only if somebody re-rules it, and
  nobody has.** The clause's grow-or-combine remedy goes with it, which is why **the thin-chapter
  question dissolves rather than being ruled** (CD-171(f)) — it was never a PENDING row and lived
  only here. **CD-141(g)'s bar on padding is retired as a gate consequence and survives as a
  reason** (CD-151(b)): an item authored to move a number is not an item. **CD-141(a)–(f) and
  (h)–(j) are untouched, and the lane's authorization is not widened by one clause leaving.**
  **CD-141's text is unedited.**
- **CD-138(g)** — **RETIRED AT POOL LEVEL ONLY by CD-171(a)(iv).** *An admissible chapter owes the
  paper's FULL per-slot demand* is no longer asked of a chapter: `items_per_paper` is what a PAPER
  owes, and **no single chapter owes a paper's worth of any slot**. **The rest of CD-138(g) is
  intact** — demand is still undivided, there is still no per-chapter divisor, and the reading that
  produced the divisor question is what left rather than the rule. `PENDING-P-036`'s `min()` cap
  named in the same clause is CLOSED-MOOT with the row. **CD-138(a)–(f) and (h) are untouched.**
  **CD-138's text is unedited.**
- **CD-151(c)** — **SUPERSEDED by CD-171(g).** *Push authorization under CD-141 now requires suite
  CLEAN AND 5b clean* becomes **suite CLEAN only**. **Step 5b itself is retained in full**: it still
  runs from `tools/bank_factual_review_prompt.md`, is still never composed in session (CD-151(a)),
  every fix is still logged with its qid (CD-151(b)), and its report is still a committed artifact
  (CD-157(d)). **What 5b loses is its veto, not its run**, and its report now travels to the Hub as
  reviewer input. **CD-151(d)'s boundary is untouched** — a clean 5b verdict was never approval.
  **CD-151(a)(b)(d)(e)(f) are untouched. CD-151's text is unedited.**
- **CD-147(f)** — the constant it names, `PAPER_LEVEL_SLOTS`, is **re-keyed by CD-150** from a
  flat set of slot shorts to an enumerated `(subject, class, slot)` frozenset read through
  `is_paper_level()`. **CD-147's ruling is unchanged and unamended** — BAN's S14/S15 bar still
  holds at all five classes — and only the shape carrying it moved, because a set of bare shorts
  cannot express a second subject without colliding with the first (`BAN-S05` বহুনির্বাচনি and
  `BAN-S13` এক কথায় প্রকাশ are live slots). **CD-147's text is unedited.**
- **CD-158** — the পাঠ ১৩ `Understand` margin exception is **RETRACTED FORWARD by CD-162**: at
  n = 107 the requirement fell to 27 and the bank clears at **+2 on the ordinary rule**, so the
  exception is **INERT**. CD-162 records that CD-158 was **correct when made and is not called
  wrong** — the margin problem was an artefact of four false-premise or duplicate items, and it
  dissolved when they were retired. **CD-158's text is unedited**, and its
  `PLAN_MARGIN_EXCEPTIONS` key is retained inert pending the Principal's ruling per CD-162(d).
- **CD-079(b)** — the per-commit form of the ruling-only push carve-out is **superseded by CD-083(b)**:
  permission is evaluated over **every commit in `git log origin/main..HEAD`**, not over the top
  commit alone. CD-079's rationale (text that originated with the Principal and travels unchanged)
  is unaffected; only the unit of permission changes. **CD-079's text is unedited.**
- **CD-081(a)** — the per-commit form of the immediate-push-on-correction clause is **superseded by
  CD-083(b)**, and constrained by **CD-083(d)**: a correction must be committed **alone**, never
  with new extraction, or the clause cannot release it without releasing the page. CD-081(a)'s
  rationale (exposure time) is unaffected. **CD-081's text is unedited.**
- **CD-050(b)** — the single-layer form of the পাঠ ১২ exclusion is **partially reversed by CD-127**.
  The exclusion now stands at **two layers that can be true separately**: **extraction is PERMITTED**
  when the Principal calls for it (none exists today), while **consumption remains EXCLUDED** — no
  bank, model paper, class test or other consuming artifact may source পাঠ ১২ until a further
  ruling. CD-050(b)'s naming of itself as a **named exception to §3** is unaffected for as long as
  no extraction exists, and `SOURCE_POLICY` §7.6's *"remaining scope is পাঠ ১–১১"* still describes
  what is extracted **today**. **CD-050's text is unedited, and §7.6's text is unedited.**
- **CD-121(a)** — the Bloom row's reading **as a BAND at pool level is superseded by CD-135**: at
  pool level the check is **REF-06 §3.6's LOWER BOUNDS ONLY**, and no upper bound fails a pool.
  **CD-121(a)'s axis assignment is untouched and remains in force** — the Bloom axis governs the
  pool, the domain axis governs the paper (UD-23), and MarkLogic §৩ still appears at paper level
  and nowhere else. What CD-135 changes is what *governs* means on the Bloom axis, not which axis
  governs what. The band, both bounds, continues to apply at **paper** level. **CD-121's text is
  unedited.**
- **CD-122(b)** — the **header-stated target** fallback for COVERAGE is **superseded by CD-138**.
  CD-122(b) ruled the fallback *because* the per-chapter slot-mapping did not exist as data, and
  said so in its own words: *"The day a slot-mapping is committed as data, this gate changes."*
  **That day is CD-138.** COVERAGE now reads `canon/marklogic/SLOT_REGISTER.json` and checks the
  **admitted task**, not slot-id presence. **CD-122(b)'s reasoning is not reversed — it is spent**:
  the prohibition it rests on (*inverting a best/alternative table into a per-chapter obligation is
  inventing the mapping*) still stands, and the register is not that inversion — it is the spine's
  own কারণ column read as data. **CD-122's text is unedited.**
- **CD-141(c)** — the clause's **illustrative count is corrected by CD-145**: **TEN** gates report
  `N/A` on a qp6 bank, not eight — `POOL-MEMBERSHIP` · `ZERO-OVERLAP` · `ANSWER-SHAPE` ·
  `RUBRIC-SPECIFICITY` · `FLAG-TRACE` · `QUOTE-VERBATIM` · `HONORIFIC` · `AS-MIX` · `NUMERALS` ·
  `CEILING`. Measured at `7cc4458`, the commit CD-141 was filed in, and at HEAD: **the figure never
  matched the suite, so nothing drifted and no verdict is re-opened.** The operative test — *no gate
  that SHOULD judge returned `N/A`* — is **unchanged and does not depend on the count**.
  **CD-141's text is unedited.**
- **CD-141(b)** — the path boundary is **amended by CD-144**: root `SESSION_LOG.md` and
  `workstreams/question-banks/_wip/STATE.md` are **named path exceptions for `log`-class commits
  only**. CD-141(b)'s reasoning is not reversed — it is completed: the row's own draft named
  `SESSION_LOG.md` inside the `log` class and the filed text dropped the parenthesis, leaving a
  lane that could not write the session log AGENTS §3 requires of it. **The two-test rule is
  untouched**, and the exception is class-scoped: a `build`, `corrections` or `promotion` commit
  touching a root file is still not authorized. **CD-141's text is unedited.**
- **CD-146** — its `BAN-S01` **source-limitation flag is RESOLVED by CD-149**, and its **(f)** —
  *পাঠ ১৫'s Q01 stands and nothing is re-authored* — is **superseded as to পাঠ ১৫**. The unit is the
  **printed line**; the flag was not an ambiguity but a **defective delimiter in the extraction**,
  short by two printed lines at that one chapter. CD-146's (b)–(e) are unaffected: the register was
  and is unchanged, and the count it recorded was correctly counted — it was counting the wrong
  unit. **CD-146's text is unedited.**
- **CD-139(d)** — its admission of **S14 at পাঠ ৪** is **REVERSED by CD-147**: S14 আবেদনপত্র and
  S15 রচনা are **paper-level for every chapter, categorically**, and belong to the paper/exam
  pipeline whatever a chapter's content anchors. **The পাঠ ৪ evidence is not retracted** —
  `C5_BAN_Source_04.md`'s *সরাসরি বাঁধা* and its crops on disk are real — **it is no longer what
  decides the question**, because a slot's pipeline is not a fact about a chapter's content.
  **CD-139's per-chapter admissibility MECHANISM stands for every other slot**, as does its
  range-of-verification lesson at (g). CD-139(f)'s single-point-of-failure case dissolves: S14 at
  C5 Bangla is zero-admitted by rule, which is not the defect `PENDING-P-038` describes.
  **CD-139's text is unedited.**
- **CD-136(c)** — its categorical bar on **S14/S15 in chapter banks** was scope-amended by CD-139
  and is **restored by CD-147**, on **wider ground than CD-136(c) itself claimed**: CD-136(c)
  rested on *they anchor to nothing in the chapter*, a CONTENT claim, and CD-139 was right that a
  content claim is falsifiable per chapter. **CD-147 does not revive the content claim** — it rules
  the pipeline instead, which is why পাঠ ৪ and পাঠ ১৮ both fall under it despite genuinely
  anchoring their slots. **Neither row's text is edited.**
- **CD-136(c)** — the bar on **S14/S15 in chapter banks** is **scope-amended by CD-139**: the
  exclusion is a **per-chapter content declaration** (CD-138(e)), not a slot-level rule. CD-136(c)'s
  reasoning is unaffected wherever the content claim holds — at পাঠ ১৩ it holds — but it does not
  hold at **পাঠ ৪**, where `C5_BAN_Source_04.md` records S14 as *সরাসরি বাঁধা* with evidence crops on
  disk. **CD-136's remaining halves — the teacher-key ruling at (b), the boundary at (c) for
  content with no chapter anchor, the refused gate change at (e)/(f) — are untouched. CD-136's text
  is unedited.**
